--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: architecure.md ---
# OptiSim architecture 

## 1. Class diagram


```mermaid
classDiagram
    class SimulationApp {
        -SimulationController controller
        -MainWindow main_window
        -list open_windows
        +start()
        +handle_simulation_finished(payload)
        +handle_error(err_msg)
        +open_strategy_window(track_name)
    }

    class MainWindow {
        -SimulationController controller
        -LoadingOverlay loading_overlay
        +openCompareRequested: pyqtSignal
        +openStrategyRequested: pyqtSignal
        +on_run_single_clicked()
        +on_lap_map_clicked()
        -_get_car_params() dict
    }

    %% Data Transfer Objects (DTOs)
    class BaseRequest {
        +dict car_params
        +float ds
    }
    class SingleLapRequest
    class EnduranceRequest
    class LapMapTestRequest
    class StrategyOptRequest
    class DeploymentOptRequest
    
    BaseRequest <|-- SingleLapRequest
    BaseRequest <|-- EnduranceRequest
    BaseRequest <|-- LapMapTestRequest
    BaseRequest <|-- StrategyOptRequest
    StrategyOptRequest <|-- DeploymentOptRequest

    class SimulationController {
        -SimulationWorker active_worker
        +simulationStarted: pyqtSignal
        +simulationProgress: pyqtSignal
        +simulationFinished: pyqtSignal
        +simulationError: pyqtSignal
        +handle_request(req: BaseRequest)
        -_create_solver(...)
    }

    class SimulationWorker {
        -SimulationTask task
        +progress: pyqtSignal
        +finished: pyqtSignal
        +error: pyqtSignal
        +run()
    }

    %% Tasks
    class SimulationTask {
        <<Abstract>>
        +execute(progress_callback) SolverPayload
    }
    class SingleLapTask
    class LaptimeMapTask
    class StrategyOptTask
    class DeploymentOptTask
    
    SimulationTask <|-- SingleLapTask
    SimulationTask <|-- LaptimeMapTask
    SimulationTask <|-- StrategyOptTask
    SimulationTask <|-- DeploymentOptTask

    class SolverPayload {
        +str kind
        +object data
        +dict meta
    }

    %% Relationships
    SimulationApp *-- SimulationController : Holds instance
    SimulationApp *-- MainWindow : Holds instance
    
    MainWindow ..> BaseRequest : Creates DTO
    MainWindow --> SimulationController : Calls handle_request(req)
    
    SimulationController ..> SimulationTask : Creates based on Request
    SimulationController *-- SimulationWorker : Manages thread
    
    SimulationWorker o-- SimulationTask : Executes
    SimulationTask ..> SolverPayload : Returns
```

## 2. State diagram 


```mermaid


stateDiagram-v2
    [*] --> Idle: Application waits for user

    state "UI Layer (MainWindow / StrategyWindow)" as UI {
        Idle --> ReadInput: Button click (e.g., Run Single Lap)
        ReadInput --> CreateRequest: Create DTO (e.g., SingleLapRequest)
        CreateRequest --> SendRequest: controller.handle_request(req)
    }

    state "Controller Layer (Assembly)" as Controller {
        SendRequest --> AssembleCar: Initialize Car() from req.car_params
        AssembleCar --> Routing: What type of Request is it?
        Routing --> CreateSingleTask: type(req) is SingleLapRequest
        Routing --> CreateLapMapTask: type(req) is LapMapTestRequest
        Routing --> CreateStrategyTask: type(req) is StrategyOptRequest
        Routing --> CreateDeploymentTask: type(req) is DeploymentOptRequest
        
        CreateSingleTask --> StartWorker
        CreateLapMapTask --> StartWorker
        CreateStrategyTask --> StartWorker
        CreateDeploymentTask --> StartWorker
        
        StartWorker --> active_worker.start()
    }

    state "Loading (MainWindow)" as Loading {
        AssembleCar --> ShowLoading: emit simulationStarted()
    }

    state "Worker Thread (SimulationWorker)" as Worker {
        active_worker.start() --> TaskExecute: task.execute(callback)
        TaskExecute --> TaskExecute: emit progress(current, total)
        TaskExecute --> CreatePayload: Computation finished
    }

    state "Results Processing (SimulationApp)" as Results {
        CreatePayload --> Finish: emit finished(SolverPayload)
        
        Finish --> CreateWindow: App catches signal and checks payload.kind
        CreateWindow --> ShowWindow: App stores and shows window (Results, Sensitivity, etc.)
    }

    ShowWindow --> Idle
    Finish --> HideLoading: UI catches signal and hides overlay
    HideLoading --> Idle
```
--- KONEC SOUBORU: architecure.md ---

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: main.py ---
import os
import sys
import ctypes
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import QApplication, QMessageBox
from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt
from PyQt6.QtGui import QIcon

from src.ui.main_window import MainWindow
from src.ui.results_window import ResultsWindow
from src.ui.endurance_strategy import LaptimeAppMainWindow
from src.ui.endurance_results import EnduranceResultsWindow
from src.ui.style_manager import StyleManager
from src.handlers.controller import SimulationController
from src.optimization.work_thread import SimulationWorker
from src.handlers.requests import StrategyOptRequest, DeploymentOptRequest
from src.ui.optimization_results_dialog import OptimizationResultsDialog

class SimulationApp:
    def __init__(self):
        
        # Create controller
        self.controller = SimulationController()
        
        # Create main window and pass the controller to it
        self.main_window = MainWindow(self.controller)
        
        # here we track references to windows
        self.open_windows = []

        # sginals from controller to update UI
        self.controller.simulationFinished.connect(self.handle_simulation_finished)
        self.controller.simulationError.connect(self.handle_error)
        
        # signals from main window
        self.main_window.openStrategyRequested.connect(self.open_strategy_window)
        self.main_window.openCompareRequested.connect(self.open_compare_window)

    def start(self):
        self.main_window.show()

    # ==========================================
    # opening different windows based on user actions
    # ==========================================
    def open_strategy_window(self, track_name):
        
        
        name = track_name + "_laptime_map"
        tracks_dir = "data/tracks/lapmaps/"
        filepath = os.path.join(tracks_dir, f"{name}.npz")

        if not os.path.exists(filepath):
            QMessageBox.warning(
                self.main_window, 
                "Missing data", 
                f"Laptime map '{name}.npz' not found.\nPlease generate it first using 'Generate laptime map' button."
            )
            return
        
        try:
            win = LaptimeAppMainWindow(filepath)
            
            # get actual car params from  MainWindow
            car_params = self.main_window._get_car_params()
            ds = self.main_window.input_ds.value()
            laps = self.main_window.car_params["laps"]
            
            
            # connect strategy optimization button
            win.optimization_requested.connect(
                lambda r, d, min_e, max_e, m_data: self.controller.handle_request(
                    StrategyOptRequest(
                        car_params=car_params,
                        ds=ds,
                        laps=laps,
                        regen_val=r,
                        delta_val=d,
                        min_e_target=min_e,
                        max_e_target=max_e,
                        map_data=m_data
                    )
                )
            )
            
            # connect deployment button
            win.deployment_requested.connect(
                lambda r, d, min_e, max_e, m_data: self.controller.handle_request(
                    DeploymentOptRequest(
                        car_params=car_params,
                        ds=ds,
                        laps=laps,
                        regen_val=r,
                        delta_val=d,
                        min_e_target=min_e,
                        max_e_target=max_e,
                        map_data=m_data
                    )
                )
            )

            # connect progress updates
            self.controller.simulationProgress.connect(win.loading_overlay.update_progress)
            self.controller.simulationFinished.connect(win.stop_loading)
            self.controller.simulationError.connect(win.stop_loading)
            
            self._register_and_show_window(win)
        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self.main_window, "Error", f"Could not load laptime map:\n{e}")

    def open_compare_window(self):
        from src.ui.results_tab import CompareWindow
        win = CompareWindow()
        self._register_and_show_window(win)

    # ==========================================
    # handling the results
    # ==========================================
    def handle_simulation_finished(self, payload):
        kind = payload.kind
        data = payload.data
        meta = payload.meta
    
        if kind == "single_lap":
            win = ResultsWindow(data, meta['car'], meta['track'], meta['ds'])
            self._register_and_show_window(win)

        elif kind == "endurance":
            win = EnduranceResultsWindow(data, meta['car'], meta['track'], meta['ds'])
            self._register_and_show_window(win)
        
        elif kind in ("endurance_map", "endurance_deployment"):
    
            for win in self.open_windows:
                if isinstance(win, LaptimeAppMainWindow):
                    win.add_result_tab(payload)
                    break

        elif kind == "optimization":
            win = OptimizationResultsDialog(payload=data, parent=self.main_window)
            win.show()
                
        elif kind == "track_generation":
            # route the smoothed track data back to main window to finish the DRS / Save process
            self.main_window.finish_track_generation(data)

    def handle_error(self, err_msg):
        
        QMessageBox.critical(self.main_window, "Simulation error", str(err_msg))

    def _register_and_show_window(self, window):
        
        self.open_windows.append(window)
    
        window.setAttribute(Qt.WidgetAttribute.WA_DeleteOnClose)
        window.destroyed.connect(lambda: self.open_windows.remove(window) if window in self.open_windows else None)
        window.show()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        myappid = 'optisim' 
        ctypes.windll.shell32.SetCurrentProcessExplicitAppUserModelID(myappid)
    except ImportError:
        pass # Not on windows

    app = QApplication(sys.argv)

    global_style = StyleManager.get_global_stylesheet()
    app.setStyleSheet(global_style)

    icon_path = os.path.join("data", "logo.png")
    app_icon = QIcon(icon_path)
    app.setWindowIcon(app_icon)
    app.setStyle("Fusion")
    
    sim_app = SimulationApp()
    sim_app.start()
    
    sys.exit(app.exec())
--- KONEC SOUBORU: main.py ---

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: pack.py ---
import os

#script for vibe coding, packs the entire project into txt which can be inserted into gemini or chat gpt for the ai to understand the context


PROJECT_DIR = "." 
OUTPUT_FILE = "project_context.txt" 

IGNORE_DIRS = {'.git', 'venv', 'env', '__pycache__', '.idea', '.vscode', 'build', 'dist'}


ALLOWED_EXTS = {'.py', '.txt', '.md', '.json', '.ini', '.yaml'}

def pack_project():
   
    print("Packing the project")
    with open(OUTPUT_FILE, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as outfile:
      
        outfile.write("=== STRUKTURA PROJEKTU ===\n")
        
        for root, dirs, files in os.walk(PROJECT_DIR):
          
            dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in IGNORE_DIRS]
            
            for file in files:
                if any(file.endswith(ext) for ext in ALLOWED_EXTS) and file != OUTPUT_FILE:
                    filepath = os.path.relpath(os.path.join(root, file), PROJECT_DIR)
                    outfile.write(f"- {filepath}\n")
        
        outfile.write("\n=== ZDROJOVÉ KÓDY ===\n\n")
        

        for root, dirs, files in os.walk(PROJECT_DIR):
            dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in IGNORE_DIRS]
            
            for file in files:
                if any(file.endswith(ext) for ext in ALLOWED_EXTS) and file != OUTPUT_FILE:
                    filepath = os.path.join(root, file)
                    rel_path = os.path.relpath(filepath, PROJECT_DIR)
                    
                    outfile.write(f"--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: {rel_path} ---\n")
                    try:
                        with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as infile:
                            outfile.write(infile.read() + "\n")
                    except Exception as e:
                        outfile.write(f"// CHYBA PŘI ČTENÍ SOUBORU: {e}\n")
                    outfile.write(f"--- KONEC SOUBORU: {rel_path} ---\n\n")

    print("Done")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    pack_project()
  
--- KONEC SOUBORU: pack.py ---

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: src\core\car.py ---
from dataclasses import dataclass,field
import numpy as np
import casadi as ca


@dataclass
class Car:
    # --- 1. Parameters from UI ---
    name: str
    weight: float           # [kg]
    cd: float             # drag coefficient (CdA) [m^2]
    cl: float             # lift coefficient (positive for downforce) [-]
    cd_drs: float # drag coefficient with drs closed
    cl_drs: float # drag coefficient with drs open
    acp_capacity: float # Max usabel ACP capacity [kWh]
    drs_allowed: bool
    power_max: float # max power [W]
    regen_eff: float # [-] efficiency of regenerative braking
    soc_start: float # Batterty state of charge at start of simulation (0-1)
    cop: float   # center of pressure as % of wheelbase from front axle (0-1)
    wb: float    # [m] wheelbase
    tw: float    # [m] car track width
    wd: float    # [-] weight distribution as % of load on front axle (0-1)
    cogz: float   # [m] height of center of gravity
    i: float     #car gear ratio
    M_max: float  #max torque of the car [Nm]
    energy_limit: float = None  # [kWh] 
    performance_delta: float = 0.0 # [-] performance modifier for strategy optimization, dont change


    model_type: str = "mass_point"

    # Constants
    rho = 1.225
    g = 9.81

    # Powertrain
    brake_max = 100000
    power_eff = 0.88
    fix_drag = 230
    wheel_radius = 0.205
    brake_bias = 0.55
    power_dist = 0.5
    v_max = 40 #not used for max bounds of GVV calcullation
    
    #grip
    mu_x: float = 1.25           # [-] 
    mu_y: float = 1.25           # [-] 
    

    #soc limitations
    soc_grid_power = [0.0, 0.04, 0.08, 0.12, 0.22, 0.32, 0.42, 0.52, 0.70, 1.00,1.1]
    power_limit_W = [10e3, 14e3, 21e3, 35e3, 62e3, 79e3, 89e3, 95e3, 100e3, 100e3,100e3]
    soc_to_power_interp = ca.interpolant('soc_power', 'bspline', [soc_grid_power], power_limit_W)

    
    

    tire_model = { 
        "cold_factor": 0.8, 
        "warmup_tau": 1.6, 
        "noise_std": 0.03, 
        "mu_x_nominal": 1.22,
        "mu_y_nominal":1.22
    }




    # car calculation functions 
    def get_aero_drag(self, v, cd = None):
        #if we pass cd as optimization parameter
        use_cd = cd if cd is not None else self.cd
        return self.fix_drag + 0.5 * self.rho * use_cd * v**2
    

    def get_recuperation_limit(self, SoC_ca):
       
        P_max = 40e3   
        soc_mid = 0.885 
        k = 120.0       
        
        return P_max / (1.0 + ca.exp(k * (SoC_ca - soc_mid)))
    
    def tire_fy(self, alpha, Fz):
            """
            Linear tire model
            Fy = (alpha/pi*180)/5 * Fz * tire_fyFactor
            """
            return (alpha * 180.0 / ca.pi) / 5.0 * Fz * self.mu_y
    
    def split_long_forces(self, F_acc, F_brake):
        """
        Inputs:
          F_acc   >= 0
          F_brake <= 0
        Returns:
          Ff, Fr  (front/rear longitudinal forces)
        """
        Ff_acc = self.power_dist * F_acc
        Fr_acc = (1.0 - self.power_dist) * F_acc

        Ff_brk = self.brake_bias * F_brake
        Fr_brk = (1.0 - self.brake_bias) * F_brake

        Ff = Ff_acc + Ff_brk
        Fr = Fr_acc + Fr_brk

        return Ff, Fr
    
        
    def get_friction_limit(self, v, weight=None, use_cl=None):
        weight = weight if weight is not None else self.weight
        use_cl = use_cl if use_cl is not None else self.cl

        F_aero = 0.5 * self.rho * use_cl * v**2

        Fz = weight * self.g + F_aero
        
       
        max_x = self.mu_x * Fz
        max_y = self.mu_y * Fz
        
        return (max_x, max_y)
    

    def get_fz(self,v,cla_ef):
        #for single track, returns Fz on front and rear
        fz_aero = 0.5*self.rho*cla_ef*v**2
        fz_aero_front = fz_aero*(1-self.cop)
        fz_aero_rear = fz_aero*self.cop

        fz_f = self.fz_static_f + fz_aero_front
        fz_r = self.fz_static_r + fz_aero_rear

        return fz_f,fz_r
    
    def get_tire_dy(self,fx,fz):

        #save option
        #term = (self.mu_y * fz)**2 - fx**2
        #term_safe = ca.log(1 + ca.exp(term))
        #dy =ca.sqrt(term_safe)
        max_lateral_sq = (self.mu_y * fz)**2 - fx**2
        
        dy = ca.sqrt(ca.fmax(max_lateral_sq, 1))
        return dy

        #dy = ca.sqrt((self.mu_y*fz)**2 -fx**2) 
        #return dy
    
    def get_cornering_stiffness(self, fz):
       
        PKY1 = 19.7276 
        PKY2 = 3.3304
        PKY4 = 7.5668
        FNOMIN = 2000  # Z tir souboru

        # Pacejka vzorec pro Stiffness (bez Camberu)
        # C_alpha = PKY1 * FNOMIN * sin(PKY4 * arctan(Fz / (PKY2 * FNOMIN)))
        
        term_inner = fz / (PKY2 * FNOMIN)
        c_alpha = PKY1 * FNOMIN * ca.sin(PKY4 * ca.arctan(term_inner))
    
        return c_alpha
    
    
    def update_regen_usage(self, base_drift=-0.0015, sigma=0.02):
    
        noise = np.random.normal(0.0, sigma)
        self.regen_eff += np.round(base_drift + noise,2)
        self.regen_eff = round(np.clip(self.regen_eff, 0.5, 1.0),2)

    def update_grip(self, lap):
        
        #tire warmup
        warmup_progress = 1 - np.exp(-(lap + 1) / self.tire_model["warmup_tau"])

        mu_x_cold = self.tire_model["cold_factor"] * self.tire_model["mu_x_nominal"]
        current_base_mu_x = mu_x_cold + (self.tire_model['mu_x_nominal'] - mu_x_cold) * warmup_progress
        
        # Y složka
        mu_y_cold = self.tire_model["cold_factor"] * self.tire_model["mu_y_nominal"]
        current_base_mu_y = mu_y_cold + (self.tire_model['mu_y_nominal'] - mu_y_cold) * warmup_progress

        noise = self.tire_model["noise_std"] * np.random.randn()
      
        self.mu_x = np.round(current_base_mu_x + noise, 3)
        self.mu_y = np.round(current_base_mu_y + noise, 3)

     
        print(f"Lap {lap+1} Grip: Mu_X={self.mu_x}, Mu_Y={self.mu_y} (Warmup: {warmup_progress:.1%})")
        
       # return self.mu_x, self.mu_y

    def singletrack_time_ode(self, z, u, cl_eff=None, cd_eff=None):
            """
            Time-domain bicycle ODE.

            State:
            z = [vx, vy, psi, dpsi, X, Y]

            Input:
            u = [delta_f, F_acc, F_brake]

            Returns:
            dz/dt (CasADi expression)
            """

            vx   = z[0]
            vy   = z[1]
            psi  = z[2]
            r    = z[3]
            X    = z[4]
            Y    = z[5]

            delta_f = u[0]
            F_acc   = u[1]
            F_brake = u[2]

            # effective aero params 
            if cl_eff is None:
                cl_eff = self.cl
            if cd_eff is None:
                cd_eff = self.cd  

            # distribute long forces
            Ff, Fr = self.split_long_forces(F_acc, F_brake)

            # normal loads 
            Fz_f, Fz_r = self.get_fz(vx, cl_eff)

            # slip angles 
            alpha_f = -ca.atan2(
                (vy + self.lf * r) * ca.cos(delta_f) - vx * ca.sin(delta_f),
                (vy + self.lf * r) * ca.sin(delta_f) + vx * ca.cos(delta_f)
            )

            alpha_r = -ca.atan2(
                (vy - self.lr * r), vx
            )

            # lateral tire forces
            F_fy = self.tire_fy(alpha_f, Fz_f)
            F_ry = self.tire_fy(alpha_r, Fz_r)

            # aero drag (uses vx only, cd is CdA)
            F_drag = 0.5 * self.rho * cd_eff * vx**2 + self.fix_drag

            # dynamics (Matlab style)
            dvx = (Ff * ca.cos(delta_f) - F_fy * ca.sin(delta_f) + Fr - F_drag) / self.mass + r * vy
            dvy = (Ff * ca.sin(delta_f) + F_fy * ca.cos(delta_f) + F_ry) / self.mass - r * vx

            dpsi = r
            dr   = (self.lf * (Ff * ca.sin(delta_f) + F_fy * ca.cos(delta_f)) - self.lr * F_ry) / self.Iz

            dX = vx * ca.cos(psi) - vy * ca.sin(psi)
            dY = vx * ca.sin(psi) + vy * ca.cos(psi)

            return ca.vertcat(dvx, dvy, dpsi, dr, dX, dY)
    
    def singletrack_path_ode(self, s, z, u, track, cl_eff=None, cd_eff=None):
        """
        Path-domain bicycle ODE.

        Independent variable: s

        State:
          z = [vx, vy, psi, dpsi, n, t]

        Input:
          u = [delta_f, F_acc, F_brake]

        Track must provide interpolation:
          th(s) = track heading
          C(s)  = curvature

        Returns:
          dz/ds
        """

        vx   = z[0]
        vy   = z[1]
        psi  = z[2]
        r    = z[3]
        n    = z[4]
        t    = z[5]

        delta_f = u[0]
        F_acc   = u[1]
        F_brake = u[2]

        # effective aero params
        if cl_eff is None:
            cl_eff = self.cl
        if cd_eff is None:
            cd_eff = self.cd  # CdA

        # distribute long forces
        Ff, Fr = self.split_long_forces(F_acc, F_brake)

        # track geometry 
        th = track.heading(s)     # == track.psi(s)
        C  = track.curvature(s)   # == track.kappa(s)

        eps = psi - th

        
        denom = (vx * ca.cos(eps) - vy * ca.sin(eps))
        Sf = (1.0 - n * C) / denom

        # normal loads depend on vx only
        Fz_f, Fz_r = self.get_fz(vx, cl_eff)

        # slip angles 
        alpha_f = -ca.atan2(
            (vy + self.lf * r) * ca.cos(delta_f) - vx * ca.sin(delta_f),
            (vy + self.lf * r) * ca.sin(delta_f) + vx * ca.cos(delta_f)
        )

        alpha_r = -ca.atan2(
            (vy - self.lr * r), vx
        )

        # lateral tire forces
        F_fy = self.tire_fy(alpha_f, Fz_f)
        F_ry = self.tire_fy(alpha_r, Fz_r)

        # aero drag
        F_drag = 0.5 * self.rho * cd_eff * vx**2 + self.fix_drag

        # time-domain derivatives (same as time ODE)
        dvx_dt = (Ff * ca.cos(delta_f) - F_fy * ca.sin(delta_f) + Fr - F_drag) / self.mass + r * vy
        dvy_dt = (Ff * ca.sin(delta_f) + F_fy * ca.cos(delta_f) + F_ry) / self.mass - r * vx
        dpsi_dt = r
        dr_dt   = (self.lf * (Ff * ca.sin(delta_f) + F_fy * ca.cos(delta_f)) - self.lr * F_ry) / self.Iz

        # path coordinates dynamics
        dn_dt = vx * ca.sin(eps) + vy * ca.cos(eps)
        dt_dt = 1.0

        # convert dt derivatives to ds derivatives
        dvx_ds  = Sf * dvx_dt
        dvy_ds  = Sf * dvy_dt
        dpsi_ds = Sf * dpsi_dt
        dr_ds   = Sf * dr_dt
        dn_ds   = Sf * dn_dt
        dt_ds   = Sf * dt_dt

        return ca.vertcat(dvx_ds, dvy_ds, dpsi_ds, dr_ds, dn_ds, dt_ds)

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: src\core\track.py ---
# src/core/track.py
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
import casadi as ca
import os
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from src.core.trackGenerator import TrackGenerator

@dataclass
class Track:
    name: str
    
    # Data for visualisation
    s: np.ndarray = field(repr=False)
    x: np.ndarray = field(repr=False)
    y: np.ndarray = field(repr=False)
    z: np.ndarray = field(repr=False)
    drs: np.ndarray = field(repr=False)
    psi: np.ndarray = field(repr=False)
    kappa: np.ndarray = field(repr=False)
    alpha: np.ndarray = field(repr=False)

    # Telemetry from Real Log (Reference)
    speed: np.ndarray = field(repr=False) # m/s
    brake: np.ndarray = field(repr=False)
    power: np.ndarray = field(repr=False) # kW
  
    length: float = 0.0
    
    # CasADi interpolant for solver
    kappa_interp: ca.Function = field(repr=False, default=None)
    psi_interp: ca.Function = field(repr=False,default=None)
    drs_interp: ca.Function = field(repr=False, default=None)

    @property
    def ref_data(self):
        """interface for validation module"""
        if self.speed is None:
            return None
            
        return {
            's': self.s,          # Vzdálenost (Osa X reference)
            'v': self.speed,      # Rychlost [m/s]
            'p': self.power       # Výkon [kW]
        }

    @classmethod
    def load(cls, track_name, tracks_dir="data/tracks"):
        """
        loads the .npz track for the simulation
        """
        filepath = os.path.join(tracks_dir, f"{track_name}.npz")
        
        if not os.path.exists(filepath):
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Baked track '{track_name}' not found. Run baking first.")
            
        
        data = np.load(filepath)
        
        s, x, y = data['s'], data['x'], data['y']
        kappa = data['kappa']
        drs = data['drs'] if 'drs' in data else None
        
        psi = data['psi'] if 'psi' in data else None
        z = data['z'] if 'z' in data else None
        alpha = data['alpha'] if 'alpha' in data else None
        speed = data['speed'] if 'speed' in data else None
        brake = data['brake'] if 'brake' in data else None
        power = data['power'] if 'power' in data else None
       
       
        kappa_func = ca.interpolant('KappaProfile', 'bspline', [s], kappa)
        if psi is not None:
            psi_func = ca.interpolant('PsiProfile','linear',[s],psi)
        else:
            psi_func = None

        drs_func = None
        if drs is not None:
            
            drs_func = ca.interpolant('DRSProfile', 'linear', [s], drs)
        
        return cls(
            name=track_name,
            s=s, 
            x=x, 
            y=y, 
            z=z,
            drs=drs,
            psi=psi, 
            kappa=kappa,
            alpha=alpha,
            speed = speed,
            brake= brake,
            power = power,
            length=s[-1],
            kappa_interp=kappa_func,
            psi_interp = psi_func,
            drs_interp=drs_func
        )
    
   

    def get_curvature(self, s_var):
        if self.kappa_interp is None:
            raise RuntimeError("Interpolant not initialized.")
        return self.kappa_interp(s_var)
    
    def get_heading(self,s_var):
        if self.psi_interp is None:
            raise RuntimeError("Interpolant not initialized.")
        return self.psi_interp(s_var)
    
    def get_drs_flag(self, s_var):
        
        if self.drs_interp is None:
            
            if isinstance(s_var, (ca.MX, ca.SX)):
                return ca.DM.zeros(s_var.shape)
            return 0.0
            
        return self.drs_interp(s_var)
--- KONEC SOUBORU: src\core\track.py ---

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: src\core\trackGenerator.py ---
import numpy as np
import casadi as ca
from scipy.interpolate import PchipInterpolator, interp1d

def latlon_to_meters(lat, lon):

    lat_rad, lon_rad = np.radians(lat), np.radians(lon)
    lat0, lon0 = lat_rad[0], lon_rad[0]
    R = 6371000 
    x = R * (lon_rad - lon0) * np.cos(lat0)
    y = R * (lat_rad - lat0)
    return x, y

class TrackGenerator:
    @staticmethod
    def smooth_track(x_smpl, y_smpl, z_smpl, ds=0.1, w_pos=50.0, r=3000, is_closed=True):
        """smooth the track with rk4 and casadi."""
        x_smpl, y_smpl, z_smpl = np.array(x_smpl).flatten(), np.array(y_smpl).flatten(), np.array(z_smpl).flatten()
        dx, dy = np.diff(x_smpl), np.diff(y_smpl)
        s_tmp = np.concatenate(([0], np.cumsum(np.sqrt(dx**2 + dy**2))))
        
        total_length = s_tmp[-1]
        N = max(2, int(np.round(total_length / ds)))
        ds_real = total_length / N
        s_traj = np.linspace(0, total_length, N) 
        
        fx, fy, fz = PchipInterpolator(s_tmp, x_smpl), PchipInterpolator(s_tmp, y_smpl), PchipInterpolator(s_tmp, z_smpl)
        x_init, y_init, z_init = fx(s_traj), fy(s_traj), fz(s_traj)
        
        dz_init = np.diff(z_init) / ds_real
        alpha_init = np.append(np.arcsin(np.clip(dz_init, -0.2, 0.2)), 0) 
        alpha_init[-1] = alpha_init[-2]

        th_init = np.unwrap(np.arctan2(np.diff(y_init), np.diff(x_init)))
        th_init = np.append(th_init, th_init[-1])
        C_init = np.append(np.diff(th_init) / ds_real, 0)
        
        opti = ca.Opti()
        Z = opti.variable(N, 6) 
        z_C, z_th, z_x, z_y, z_alp, z_z = Z[:,0], Z[:,1], Z[:,2], Z[:,3], Z[:,4], Z[:,5]
        u = opti.variable(N-1, 2) 
        
        x_dev = w_pos*((z_x[:-1] - x_init[:-1])**2 + (z_x[1:] - x_init[1:])**2) / 2
        y_dev = w_pos*((z_y[:-1] - y_init[:-1])**2 + (z_y[1:] - y_init[1:])**2) / 2
        z_dev = w_pos*((z_z[:-1] - z_init[:-1])**2 + (z_z[1:] - z_init[1:])**2) / 2
        reg_term = r * u[:,0]**2 + 10*r * u[:,1]**2
        
        opti.minimize(ca.sum1(ds_real * (reg_term + x_dev + y_dev + z_dev)))
        
        def f_dyn(z_in, u_in):
            return ca.vertcat(u_in[0], z_in[0], ca.cos(z_in[1]), ca.sin(z_in[1]), u_in[1], ca.sin(z_in[4]))

        for k in range(N-1):
            z_k, u_k = Z[k, :].T, u[k, :].T
            k1 = f_dyn(z_k, u_k)
            k2 = f_dyn(z_k + ds_real/2*k1, u_k)
            k3 = f_dyn(z_k + ds_real/2*k2, u_k)
            k4 = f_dyn(z_k + ds_real*k3, u_k)
            opti.subject_to(Z[k+1, :].T == z_k + ds_real/6 * (k1 + 2*k2 + 2*k3 + k4))

        if is_closed:
            opti.subject_to(Z[-1, 2] == Z[0, 2]) 
            opti.subject_to(Z[-1, 3] == Z[0, 3]) 
            opti.subject_to(Z[-1, 5] == Z[0, 5]) 
            opti.subject_to(Z[-1, 0] == Z[0, 0]) 
            opti.subject_to(Z[-1, 4] == Z[0, 4]) 
            n_loops = np.round((th_init[-1] - th_init[0]) / (2*np.pi))
            opti.subject_to(z_th[-1] == z_th[0] + 2*np.pi*n_loops)

        opti.set_initial(z_x, x_init); opti.set_initial(z_y, y_init); opti.set_initial(z_z, z_init)
        opti.set_initial(z_th, th_init); opti.set_initial(z_C, C_init); opti.set_initial(z_alp, alpha_init)
        
        opti.solver('ipopt', {'expand': True}, {'max_iter': 500, 'print_level': 0, 'sb': 'yes'})
        
        try:
            sol = opti.solve()
            Z_opt = sol.value(Z)
            return {'s': s_traj, 'kappa': Z_opt[:, 0], 'psi': Z_opt[:, 1], 'x': Z_opt[:, 2], 'y': Z_opt[:, 3], 'alpha': Z_opt[:, 4], 'z': Z_opt[:, 5]}
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Optimalizace selhala: {e}")
            return None



def generate_smoothed_track(all_data, lap_infos, is_closed, r_penalty, w_penalty):
    """processes raw data, smooths via casadi and averages if multiple laps are provided."""
    tg = TrackGenerator()
    smoothed_list, telemetry_list = [], []

    print(f"processing {len(lap_infos)} laps with r={r_penalty}, w={w_penalty}...")

    for lap in lap_infos:
        s_idx, e_idx = lap['indices']
        raw_lat, raw_lon = all_data['Latitude'][s_idx:e_idx], all_data['Longitude'][s_idx:e_idx]
        raw_power, raw_brake = all_data['Power'][s_idx:e_idx], all_data['Brake'][s_idx:e_idx]
        raw_speed, raw_alt = all_data['Speed'][s_idx:e_idx], all_data['Altitude'][s_idx:e_idx]

        x_m, y_m = latlon_to_meters(raw_lat, raw_lon)
        # pass the w_penalty as w_pos
        sm = tg.smooth_track(x_m, y_m, raw_alt, ds=0.1, w_pos=w_penalty, r=r_penalty, is_closed=is_closed)
        if sm is None: raise RuntimeError("smoothing failed for one of the laps.")

        s_raw = np.concatenate(([0], np.cumsum(np.sqrt(np.diff(x_m)**2 + np.diff(y_m)**2))))
        _, unique_indices = np.unique(s_raw, return_index=True)
        s_raw = s_raw[unique_indices]
        
        from scipy.interpolate import interp1d
        f_power = interp1d(s_raw, raw_power[unique_indices], kind='linear', fill_value='extrapolate')
        f_brake = interp1d(s_raw, raw_brake[unique_indices], kind='linear', fill_value='extrapolate')
        f_speed = interp1d(s_raw, raw_speed[unique_indices], kind='linear', fill_value='extrapolate')
        f_alt = interp1d(s_raw, raw_alt[unique_indices], kind='linear', fill_value='extrapolate')

        smoothed_list.append(sm)
        telemetry_list.append({'power': f_power, 'brake': f_brake, 'speed': f_speed, 'alt': f_alt})

    if len(smoothed_list) == 1:
        res = smoothed_list[0]
        fns = telemetry_list[0]
        final_s, final_kappa, final_psi, final_x, final_y, final_alpha = res['s'], res['kappa'], res['psi'], res['x'], res['y'], res['alpha']
        final_z, final_power, final_brake, final_speed = fns['alt'](final_s), fns['power'](final_s), fns['brake'](final_s), fns['speed'](final_s)
        avg_lap_time = lap_infos[0]['time']
    else:
        min_len = min([s_arr['s'][-1] for s_arr in smoothed_list])
        N_common = min(len(s_arr['s']) for s_arr in smoothed_list)
        final_s = np.linspace(0, min_len, N_common)

        vals = {k: [] for k in ['x', 'y', 'z', 'kappa', 'alpha', 'psi_sin', 'psi_cos', 'p', 'b', 'v']}
        for sm, fns in zip(smoothed_list, telemetry_list):
            for k in ['x', 'y', 'kappa', 'alpha']: vals[k].append(interp1d(sm['s'], sm[k])(final_s))
            vals['z'].append(fns['alt'](final_s))
            psi_curr = interp1d(sm['s'], sm['psi'])(final_s)
            vals['psi_sin'].append(np.sin(psi_curr))
            vals['psi_cos'].append(np.cos(psi_curr))
            vals['p'].append(fns['power'](final_s)); vals['b'].append(fns['brake'](final_s)); vals['v'].append(fns['speed'](final_s))

        final_x, final_y, final_z = np.mean(vals['x'], axis=0), np.mean(vals['y'], axis=0), np.mean(vals['z'], axis=0)
        final_kappa, final_alpha = np.mean(vals['kappa'], axis=0), np.mean(vals['alpha'], axis=0)
        final_psi = np.arctan2(np.mean(vals['psi_sin'], axis=0), np.mean(vals['psi_cos'], axis=0))
        final_power, final_brake, final_speed = np.mean(vals['p'], axis=0), np.mean(vals['b'], axis=0), np.mean(vals['v'], axis=0)
        avg_lap_time = float(np.mean([l['time'] for l in lap_infos]))

    return {
        's': final_s, 'kappa': final_kappa, 'psi': final_psi, 'x': final_x, 'y': final_y, 'z': final_z,
        'alpha': final_alpha, 'power': final_power, 'brake': final_brake, 'speed': final_speed, 'lap_time': avg_lap_time
    }

from PyQt6.QtCore import QObject, pyqtSignal
from src.core.car import Car
from src.core.track import Track
from src.optimization.solvers import MassPointSolver, StrategyMaker, QuasiSteadySolver
from src.optimization.solvers import InitConditions, calculate_initial_guess
from src.optimization.work_thread import SimulationWorker

# import tasks
from src.optimization.tasks import SingleLapTask, LaptimeMapTask, StrategyOptTask, DeploymentOptTask, TrackGenerationTask, OptimizationTask


# import requests
from src.handlers.requests import (
    SingleLapRequest, EnduranceRequest, LapMapTestRequest, 
    StrategyOptRequest, DeploymentOptRequest, TrackGenerationRequest, OptimizationRequest
)

class SimulationController(QObject):
    # central signals for ui updates
    simulationStarted = pyqtSignal()
    simulationProgress = pyqtSignal(int, int)
    simulationFinished = pyqtSignal(object) 
    simulationError = pyqtSignal(str)

    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.active_worker = None

    def _create_solver(self, car: Car, track: Track, laps: int):
       
        if car.model_type == "mass_point_rk4":
            return MassPointSolver(car, track)
        elif car.model_type == "quasi_steady":
            return QuasiSteadySolver(car, track)
        else:
            return MassPointSolver(car, track)

    def handle_request(self, req):
        # single entry point for all incoming simulation requests
        self.simulationStarted.emit()

        try:
            # build the core car object shared across all requests
            car = Car(**req.car_params)
            task = None

            # route logic based on the specific type of request
            if isinstance(req, SingleLapRequest):
                track = Track.load(req.track_name)
                solver = self._create_solver(car, track, laps=1)
                task = SingleLapTask(solver, car, track, req.ds)
                
            elif isinstance(req, EnduranceRequest):
                track = Track.load(req.track_name)
                solver = self._create_solver(car, track, laps=req.laps)
                # task = EnduranceTask(solver, car, track, req.ds, req.laps)
                raise NotImplementedError("endurance task not implemented yet")

            elif isinstance(req, LapMapTestRequest):
                track = Track.load(req.track_name)
                #we use strictly the mass point solver since it can limit energy
                solver = MassPointSolver(car, track)
                task = LaptimeMapTask(solver, car, track, req.ds, req.laps)

            elif isinstance(req, TrackGenerationRequest):
                # track generation task doesnt use the car object
                task = TrackGenerationTask(
                    req.data, req.lap_infos, req.is_closed, req.r_penalty, req.w_penalty
                )
            
            elif isinstance(req, OptimizationRequest):
                # Zde nevytváříme 'car' a 'solver' přímo v kontroleru, 
                # protože to za nás udělá OptimizationTask pro každý trial.
                task = OptimizationTask(
                    car_params=req.car_params,
                    ds=req.ds,
                    vehicle_name=req.vehicle_name,
                    tracks=req.tracks,
                    bounds=req.bounds
                )

            elif isinstance(req, (StrategyOptRequest, DeploymentOptRequest)):
                # update car with strategy specific modifiers
                car.regen_eff = req.regen_val
                car.performance_delta = req.delta_val
                
                laptime_interpolant = req.map_data['interpolant']
                solver = StrategyMaker(
                    car, laptime_interpolant, req.laps, 
                    req.min_e_target + 0.01, req.max_e_target - 0.01
                )
                
                if type(req) is StrategyOptRequest:
                    task = StrategyOptTask(solver, car)
                elif type(req) is DeploymentOptRequest:
                    task = DeploymentOptTask(solver, car)
            else:
                # catch unknown requests
                raise TypeError(f"unknown request type: {type(req).__name__}")

            # delegate the task to the worker thread
            self.active_worker = SimulationWorker(task)
            
            # connect signals
            self.active_worker.progress.connect(self.simulationProgress.emit)
            self.active_worker.error.connect(self._on_worker_error)
            self.active_worker.finished.connect(self.simulationFinished.emit)
            self.active_worker.finished.connect(self._cleanup_worker)

            # start computation
            self.active_worker.start()

        except Exception as e:
            self.simulationError.emit(f"failed to initialize task: {str(e)}")

    def _on_worker_error(self, err_msg):
        # emit error and clean up
        self.simulationError.emit(err_msg)
        self._cleanup_worker()

    def _cleanup_worker(self):
        # safely delete the c++ thread object
        if self.active_worker:
            self.active_worker.deleteLater()
            self.active_worker = None

from dataclasses import dataclass

# base request containing parameters shared across all simulations
@dataclass
class BaseRequest:
    car_params: dict
    ds: float

# specific request types extending the base request
@dataclass
class SingleLapRequest(BaseRequest):
    track_name: str

@dataclass
class EnduranceRequest(BaseRequest):
    track_name: str
    laps: int

@dataclass
class LapMapTestRequest(BaseRequest):
    track_name: str
    laps: int

@dataclass
class StrategyOptRequest(BaseRequest):
    laps: int
    regen_val: float
    delta_val: float
    min_e_target: float
    max_e_target: float
    map_data: dict

@dataclass
class DeploymentOptRequest(StrategyOptRequest):
    # inherits all fields from strategyoptrequest, used just for routing
    pass

@dataclass
class TrackGenerationRequest(BaseRequest):
    # request for track generation and smoothing
    data: dict
    lap_infos: list
    is_closed: bool
    r_penalty: float
    w_penalty: float


@dataclass
class OptimizationRequest(BaseRequest):
    vehicle_name: str
    tracks: list      
    bounds: dict

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: src\handlers\telemetry_parser.py ---
import scipy.io
import numpy as np

def filter_data(mat_file):
    """
    loads a .mat telemetry file, performs interpolation to a common time axis
    and trims data based on gps signal accuracy.
    """
    print(f"Loading and filtering data from file: {mat_file}")
    mat_data = scipy.io.loadmat(mat_file)
    
    # gps and speed (ins)
    longitude = np.array(mat_data['INS'][0,0]['EKF_POS'][0,0]['Longitude']).flatten()
    latitude = np.array(mat_data['INS'][0,0]['EKF_POS'][0,0]['Latitude']).flatten()
    gps_time = np.array(mat_data['INS'][0,0]['EKF_POS'][0,0]['t']).flatten()

    altitude = np.array(mat_data['INS'][0,0]['EKF_ALTITUDE'][0,0]['Altitude']).flatten()
    altitude_time =  np.array(mat_data['INS'][0,0]['EKF_ALTITUDE'][0,0]['t']).flatten()
    
    long_acc = np.array(mat_data['INS'][0,0]['EKF_POS_ACC'][0,0]['Longitude_acc']).flatten()
    lat_acc = np.array(mat_data['INS'][0,0]['EKF_POS_ACC'][0,0]['Latitude_acc']).flatten()
    altitude_acc = np.array(mat_data['INS'][0,0]['EKF_POS_ACC'][0,0]['Altitude_acc']).flatten()
    precision_time = np.array(mat_data['INS'][0,0]['EKF_POS_ACC'][0,0]['t']).flatten()
    
    speed = np.array(mat_data['INS'][0,0]['EKF_VEL_BODY'][0,0]['VELOCITY_X']).flatten()
    speed_time = np.array(mat_data['INS'][0,0]['EKF_VEL_BODY'][0,0]['t']).flatten()

    # power (ams)
    p_out = np.array(mat_data['AMS'][0,0]['TsMeas'][0,0]['Pout']).flatten()
    p_time = np.array(mat_data['AMS'][0,0]['TsMeas'][0,0]['t']).flatten()
    
    # brakes
    brakeF = np.array(mat_data['PDL'][0,0]['Brakes'][0,0]['Front']).flatten()
    brakeR = np.array(mat_data['PDL'][0,0]['Brakes'][0,0]['Rear']).flatten()
    brake_time = np.array(mat_data['PDL'][0,0]['Brakes'][0,0]['t']).flatten()

    # yaw rate
    yaw_rate = np.array(mat_data['INS'][0,0]['IMU_GYRO'][0,0]['Gyro_Z']).flatten()
    yaw_time = np.array(mat_data['INS'][0,0]['IMU_GYRO'][0,0]['t']).flatten()

    # interpolation to speed_time
    lat_interp = np.interp(speed_time, gps_time, latitude)
    lon_interp = np.interp(speed_time, gps_time, longitude)
    altitude_interp = np.interp(speed_time, altitude_time, altitude)
    lat_acc_interp = np.interp(speed_time, precision_time, lat_acc)
    long_acc_interp = np.interp(speed_time, precision_time, long_acc)
    altitude_acc_interp = np.interp(speed_time, precision_time, altitude_acc)
    p_interp = np.interp(speed_time, p_time, p_out)
    brakeF_interp = np.interp(speed_time, brake_time, brakeF)
    brakeR_interp = np.interp(speed_time, brake_time, brakeR)
    yaw_rate_interp = np.interp(speed_time, yaw_time, yaw_rate)
    brake = brakeF_interp + brakeR_interp

    # filter start based on gps accuracy
    mask = (lat_acc_interp < 1.5) & (long_acc_interp < 1.5) & (altitude_acc_interp < 1.5)
    valid_indices = np.where(mask)[0]
    
    if len(valid_indices) == 0:
        raise ValueError("No data with sufficient GPS accuracy found (acc < 1.5).")
        
    start_index = valid_indices[0]
    end_index = valid_indices[-1]

    return {
        'Speed': speed[start_index:end_index], 
        'Latitude': lat_interp[start_index:end_index],
        'Longitude': lon_interp[start_index:end_index], 
        'Altitude': altitude_interp[start_index:end_index],
        'Power': p_interp[start_index:end_index],
        'Brake': brake[start_index:end_index], 
        'Time': speed_time[start_index:end_index],
        'YawRate': yaw_rate_interp[start_index:end_index] 
    }

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: src\optimization\work_thread.py ---
from PyQt6.QtCore import QThread, pyqtSignal
from src.optimization.tasks.base_task import SimulationTask

class SimulationWorker(QThread):
    """Thread that executes simulation tasks"""
    finished = pyqtSignal(object) # returns SolverPayload
    error = pyqtSignal(str)
    progress = pyqtSignal(int, int)     

    def __init__(self, task: SimulationTask):
        super().__init__()
        self.task = task
    
    def run(self):
        try:
            payload = self.task.execute(progress_callback=self.progress.emit)
            self.finished.emit(payload)
        except Exception as e:
            self.error.emit(str(e))
