--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: src\optimization\solvers\base_solver.py ---
# src/optimization/solvers/base_solver.py
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
import numpy as np

@dataclass
class SolverResults:
    #mandatory
    success: bool
    lap_time: float
    t: np.ndarray
    v: np.ndarray #total velocity
    s: np.ndarray
    #inputs 
    f_acc: np.ndarray
    f_brake: np.ndarray
    recuperation:np.ndarray
    #model specific
    vx: np.ndarray      = field(default_factory=lambda: np.array([]))
    vy: np.ndarray      = field(default_factory=lambda: np.array([]))
    n: np.ndarray         = field(default_factory=lambda: np.array([]))  # Lateral deviation
    r: np.ndarray         = field(default_factory=lambda: np.array([]))
    xi: np.ndarray       = field(default_factory=lambda: np.array([]))  # Heading error
    kappa_car: np.ndarray = field(default_factory=lambda: np.array([]))  # Actual curvature
    delta: np.ndarray     = field(default_factory=lambda: np.array([]))  # Steering angle
    E: np.ndarray         = field(default_factory=lambda: np.array([]))  
    drs_usage: np.ndarray = field(default_factory=lambda: np.array([]))
    alpha_f: np.ndarray = field(default_factory=lambda: np.array([]))  # Slip angle front
    alpha_r: np.ndarray = field(default_factory=lambda: np.array([]))  # Slip angle rear
    SoC: np.ndarray = field(default_factory=lambda: np.array([]))

    # Sensitivities (s/unit)
    sens: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    #
    mu_x: float = 0.0
    mu_y: float = 0.0
    regen_eff: float = 0.0
    ap_id: int = 0
@dataclass
class InitConditions:
    v: float = 0.0  
    T: float = 0.0   
    E: float = 0.0 
    n: float = 0.0
    xi: float = 0.0
    E_target: float = 0.0
    target_time: float = None

class BaseSolver(ABC):
    
    def __init__(self, car, track, laps=1):
        self.car = car
        self.track = track
        self.laps = laps

    @abstractmethod
    def solve(self, init_cond: InitConditions, init_guess=None, ds=0.5) -> SolverResults:
        """
        Abstract method for all solvers
        """
        pass
--- KONEC SOUBORU: src\optimization\solvers\base_solver.py ---

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: src\optimization\solvers\init_guess.py ---
# src/optimization/solvers/utils.py
import numpy as np
from src.optimization.solvers.base_solver import InitConditions

def calculate_initial_guess(car,track, init_cond: InitConditions, ds=1.0):
        #creates warm start for the solver based on simple forward-backward integration and curve speed limits
       
        total_length = track.length
        N = int(np.round(total_length / ds))
        s_sim = np.linspace(0, total_length, N + 1)
        
        ds_const = total_length / N 
        
        kappa_dm = track.get_curvature(s_sim)
        kappa = np.array(kappa_dm).flatten()
        
        m = car.weight
        g = 9.81
        rho = 1.225
        Cl = car.cl
        Cd = car.cd
        mu_x = car.mu_x - 0.1
        mu_y = car.mu_y - 0.1
        P_max = car.power_max-5000
        F_max_abs = 8000
        
    
        #curve limits vectorized
        denom = m * np.abs(kappa) - mu_y * 0.5 * rho * Cl
        
      
        v_max_car = 40.0 
        v_curve = np.full(N+1, v_max_car)
        
        
        mask = denom > 0
        v_sq_limit = (mu_y * m * g) / denom[mask]
        v_curve[mask] = np.sqrt(v_sq_limit)
        
    
        v_curve = np.minimum(v_curve, v_max_car)

        #forward integration (acceleration)
        v_fwd = np.zeros(N+1)
        v_fwd[0] = max(init_cond.v, 3.0) 
        
        for k in range(N):
            v_curr = v_fwd[k]
            
            # Aero Drag
            F_drag = 0.5 * rho * Cd * v_curr**2
            
            # Grip: F_z = m*g + AeroDown
            F_z = m*g + 0.5 * rho * Cl * v_curr**2
            F_grip = mu_x * F_z
            
            # Power: P = F*v -> F = P/v
            F_power = P_max / max(v_curr, 2.0) * car.power_eff
            
            # Useable accel force
            F_acc_max = min(F_grip, F_power, F_max_abs)
            
            # Net force & accel
            F_net = F_acc_max - F_drag
            acc = F_net / m
            
            # kinematics
            v_next = np.sqrt(max(v_curr**2 + 2 * acc * ds_const, 1.0))
            
            # limit by curve
            v_fwd[k+1] = min(v_next, v_curve[k+1])

        # backward integration (braking)
        v_bwd = np.zeros(N+1)
        v_bwd[-1] = v_curve[-1] 
        
        for k in range(N, 0, -1):
            v_curr = v_bwd[k] 
            
            # Aero
            F_drag = 0.5 * rho * Cd * v_curr**2
            F_z = m*g + 0.5 * rho * Cl * v_curr**2
            
            # Max Brake Force
            F_grip = mu_x * F_z
            F_brake_max = min(F_grip, F_max_abs) 
                    
            acc_back = (F_brake_max + F_drag) / m
            
            v_prev = np.sqrt(v_curr**2 + 2 * acc_back * ds_const)
            
            # Min (Backward, Forward, Curve)
            v_bwd[k-1] = min(v_prev, v_curve[k-1], v_fwd[k-1])

        # Final velocity profile
        v_guess = v_bwd 
        
        #smoothing for IPOPT
        window_size = 5
        v_smooth = np.convolve(v_guess, np.ones(window_size)/window_size, mode='same')
        v_smooth[0] = v_guess[0] 
        v_smooth[-1] = v_guess[-1] 
        
        #calculate forces and energy for the guess
        v_sq_diff = np.diff(v_smooth**2)
        acc_req = v_sq_diff / (2 * ds_const)
        
        v_mid = 0.5 * (v_smooth[:-1] + v_smooth[1:])
        
        # Drag
        F_drag_vec = 0.5 * rho * Cd * v_mid**2
        
        # F
        F_net_req = m * acc_req
        F_long_req = F_net_req + F_drag_vec
        
        # time
        dt_vec = ds_const / np.maximum(v_mid, 1.0)
        T_guess = np.concatenate(([0], np.cumsum(dt_vec))) + init_cond.T
        
        # Inputs splitting
        F_acc_guess = np.maximum(F_long_req, 0)
        F_brake_guess = np.minimum(F_long_req, 0)
        
        # Energy
        P_mech = F_long_req * v_mid
       
        dE = P_mech * dt_vec / 3.6e6 
        E_guess = np.concatenate(([init_cond.E], np.cumsum(dE) + init_cond.E))

        return {
            'v': np.array(v_smooth).flatten(),       
            'T': np.array(T_guess).flatten(),        
            'E': np.array(E_guess).flatten(),        
            'F_acc': np.array(F_acc_guess).flatten(),   
            'F_brake': np.array(F_brake_guess).flatten()
        }
--- KONEC SOUBORU: src\optimization\solvers\init_guess.py ---

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: src\optimization\solvers\mass_point.py ---
import casadi as ca
import numpy as np
from src.core.car import Car
from src.optimization.solvers.base_solver import BaseSolver, SolverResults, InitConditions


class MassPointSolver(BaseSolver):
    def __init__(self, car, track):

        super().__init__(car, track)

    def solve(self, init_cond: InitConditions, init_guess=None, ds=0.5):

        opti = ca.Opti()

        # =========================
        # Parameters
        # =========================
        p_weight = opti.parameter()
        p_cl   = opti.parameter()
        p_cd   = opti.parameter()
     

        # Set params values
        opti.set_value(p_weight, self.car.weight)
        opti.set_value(p_cl,   self.car.cl)
        opti.set_value(p_cd,   self.car.cd)


        # =========================
        # Track data
        # =========================
        total_length = self.track.length
        N = int(np.round(total_length / ds))

        s_sim = np.linspace(0, total_length, N + 1)
        ds = s_sim[1:] - s_sim[:-1]

        kappa_grid = self.track.get_curvature(s_sim)

        # =========================
        # States
        # =========================
        v = opti.variable(N + 1)   # speed [m/s]
        T = opti.variable(N + 1)   # time [s]
        E = opti.variable(N + 1)   # energy used [kWh]
        
        # =========================
        # Inputs
        # =========================
        F_acc   = opti.variable(N)   # Gas [N]
        F_brake_mech = opti.variable(N)
        F_brake_regen = opti.variable(N)
        F_brake = F_brake_mech + F_brake_regen
        drs     = opti.variable(N)

        
        # =========================
        # DRS
        # =========================
        if self.car.drs_allowed and self.track.drs is not None:
            drs_flag = self.track.get_drs_flag(s_sim[:-1])
            drs_ef = drs * drs_flag
            cl_eff = p_cl + drs_ef * (self.car.cl_drs - p_cl)
            cd_eff = p_cd + drs_ef * (self.car.cd_drs - p_cd)
        else:
            drs_ef = ca.DM.zeros(N)
            cl_eff = p_cl
            cd_eff = p_cd

        opti.subject_to(drs >= 0)
        opti.subject_to(drs <= 1)

        # Current speed (k)
        v_k = v[:-1]

        # =========================
        # Aero
        # =========================
       # F_drag = Car.get_aero_drag(self.car, v_k, cd=cd_eff)

        F_max_x, F_max_y = Car.get_friction_limit(
            self.car, v_k,
            weight=p_weight,    
            use_cl=cl_eff
        )

        # Lateral Force: F_lat = m * v^2 * curvature
        F_lat = p_weight * v_k**2 * ca.fabs(kappa_grid[:-1])

        # Longitudinal force
        F_long_k = F_acc + F_brake

        # =========================
        # Pedal constraints
        # =========================
        opti.subject_to(F_acc >= 0)
        opti.subject_to(F_brake_regen <= 0)
        opti.subject_to(F_brake_mech <= 0)

        # =========================
        # Friction ellipse
        # =========================
        term_long = (F_long_k / F_max_x)**2
        term_lat  = (F_lat    / F_max_y)**2
        opti.subject_to(term_long + term_lat <= 1)

        #SoC
        E_left = self.car.acp_capacity - E[:-1]
        SoC = E_left/self.car.acp_capacity

        # Recuperation limit at given SoC
        recuperation_max = self.car.get_recuperation_limit(SoC)

        P_recuperated = -F_brake_regen*v_k

        opti.subject_to(P_recuperated/recuperation_max <= 1)
        opti.subject_to((-F_brake_mech*v_k)/self.car.brake_max <= 1)
        opti.subject_to(F_brake/self.car.brake_max <= 1)

  
        # rk4 integration
       
        def get_derivatives(v_val):
            
            f_drag_step = Car.get_aero_drag(self.car, v_val, cd=cd_eff)
            f_net_step = F_long_k - f_drag_step
            acc_step = f_net_step / p_weight
            
            dv_ds = acc_step / v_val
            dT_ds = 1.0 / v_val
            
            dE_ds = (F_acc / self.car.power_eff - (-F_brake_regen * self.car.regen_eff * 0.9)) / 3.6e6
            
            return dv_ds, dT_ds, dE_ds

        
        k1_v, k1_T, k1_E = get_derivatives(v_k)
        k2_v, k2_T, k2_E = get_derivatives(v_k + 0.5 * ds * k1_v)
        k3_v, k3_T, k3_E = get_derivatives(v_k + 0.5 * ds * k2_v)
        k4_v, k4_T, k4_E = get_derivatives(v_k + ds * k3_v)

       
        v_next = v_k + (ds / 6.0) * (k1_v + 2*k2_v + 2*k3_v + k4_v)
        T_next = T[:-1] + (ds / 6.0) * (k1_T + 2*k2_T + 2*k3_T + k4_T)
        E_next = E[:-1] + (ds / 6.0) * (k1_E + 2*k2_E + 2*k3_E + k4_E)

        opti.subject_to(v[1:] == v_next)
        opti.subject_to(T[1:] == T_next)
        opti.subject_to(E[1:] == E_next)

        # =========================
        # Torque limit (30 Nm per wheel)
        # =========================
        opti.subject_to(
            ((self.car.wheel_radius * F_acc)) /
            (self.car.M_max * self.car.i * 4) < 1
        )

        # =========================
        # Power limit
        # =========================

        P_out = (F_acc * v_k) / self.car.power_eff

        # Limit from SoC (dynamic)
        P_max_dynamic = self.car.soc_to_power_interp(SoC)
        opti.subject_to(P_out <= P_max_dynamic)

        # Hard limit
        opti.subject_to(P_out <= self.car.power_max)

        # Bounding SoC
        opti.subject_to(SoC >= 0.0)

        # Speed limits
        opti.subject_to(v >= 1.0)  

        # =========================
        # Initial conditions
        # =========================
        opti.subject_to(v[0] == init_cond.v)
        opti.subject_to(T[0] == init_cond.T)
        opti.subject_to(E[0] == init_cond.E)
        
        opti.subject_to(drs[0] == 0)  # start with DRS closed

        # =========================
        # Energy limitation
        # =========================
   
        if self.car.energy_limit is not None:
            opti.subject_to(E[-1]-init_cond.E <= self.car.energy_limit)

        # =========================
        # Objective function
        # =========================
        J_Time = T[-1]

        F_norm_factor = (self.car.weight * self.car.g)**2
        J_overlap = 0.004 * ca.sum1((F_acc * -F_brake) / F_norm_factor)

        J_final_v = 0.1 * v[-1]

        J_drs_binary = 0.002 * ca.sum1(drs * (1 - drs))

        opti.minimize(J_Time + J_overlap - J_final_v)

        # =========================
        # Initial guess
        # =========================
        if init_guess is not None:
            opti.set_initial(v, 0.9*init_guess['v'])
            opti.set_initial(F_acc, 0.9*init_guess['F_acc'])
           
            
        else:
            opti.set_initial(v, 15.0)
            opti.set_initial(F_acc, 100.0)

        # =========================
        # Solver setup
        # =========================
        p_opts = {"expand": True}
        s_opts = {"max_iter": 2000, "tol": 1e-2, "print_level": 5}

        opti.solver("ipopt", p_opts, s_opts)

        try:
            sol = opti.solve()

            # =========================
            # Sensitivity analysis
            # =========================
            L = opti.f + ca.dot(opti.lam_g, opti.g)

            sens_w   = sol.value(ca.jacobian(L, p_weight))
            sens_cl  = sol.value(ca.jacobian(L, p_cl))
            sens_cd  = sol.value(ca.jacobian(L, p_cd))
         

            print("sensitivity results")
            print(f"Weight: {sens_w:+.4f} s/kg")
            print(f"CL:   {sens_cl:+.4f} s/unit")
            print(f"CdA:  {sens_cd:+.4f} s/unit")

            return SolverResults(
                success=True,
                lap_time=sol.value(T[-1]),
                t=sol.value(T),
                v=sol.value(v),
                s=s_sim,
                E=sol.value(E),
                SoC=sol.value(SoC)*100,
                f_acc=sol.value(F_acc) / self.car.power_eff,
                f_brake=sol.value(F_brake),
                recuperation=sol.value(P_recuperated*self.car.regen_eff),
                drs_usage=sol.value(drs_ef),
                sens={
                    "dTime_dm": sens_w,
                    "dTime_dCL": sens_cl,
                    "dTime_dCdA": sens_cd,
                }
            )

        except RuntimeError as e:
            print(f"Solver failed: {e}")
            return SolverResults(False, 0, [], [], [], [], [], [], {})
--- KONEC SOUBORU: src\optimization\solvers\mass_point.py ---

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: src\optimization\solvers\mpcc.py ---
import casadi as ca
import numpy as np
from src.core.car import Car
from src.optimization.solvers.base_solver import BaseSolver, SolverResults, InitConditions



#does not work, i leave it here only for reference, it was an attempt but ended up being too slow and not converging well, you might want to use some of this code if you want to implement a working MPCC in the future


class MPCC_Solver(BaseSolver):
    def __init__(self, car, track):

        super().__init__(car, track)





def solve(self, init_cond: InitConditions, init_guess=None, ds=0.5):

        opti = ca.Opti()

        # =========================
        # Parameters
        # =========================
        p_mass = opti.parameter()
        p_cl   = opti.parameter()
        p_cd   = opti.parameter()
        p_mu_x = opti.parameter()
        p_mu_y = opti.parameter()

        # Set params values
        opti.set_value(p_mass, self.car.mass)
        opti.set_value(p_cl,   self.car.cl)
        opti.set_value(p_cd,   self.car.cd)
        opti.set_value(p_mu_x, self.car.mu_x)
        opti.set_value(p_mu_y, self.car.mu_y)

        # =========================
        # Track data
        # =========================
        total_length = self.track.length
        N = int(np.round(total_length / ds))

        s_sim = np.linspace(0, total_length, N + 1)
        ds = s_sim[1:] - s_sim[:-1]

        kappa_grid = self.track.get_curvature(s_sim)

        # =========================
        # States
        # =========================
        v = opti.variable(N + 1)   # speed [m/s]
        T = opti.variable(N + 1)   # time [s]
        E = opti.variable(N + 1)   # energy used [kWh]
        n = opti.variable(N + 1)   # distance of centerline
        phi = opti.variable(N + 1) # heading error [rad]

        # =========================
        # Inputs
        # =========================
        F_acc   = opti.variable(N)   # Gas [N]
        F_brake = opti.variable(N)   # Brake [N]
        k_car   = opti.variable(N)   # Curvature of the car's path
        drs     = opti.variable(N)   # DRS position [0-1]

        # =========================
        # DRS
        # =========================
        if self.car.drs_allowed and self.track.drs is not None:
            drs_flag = self.track.get_drs_flag(s_sim[:-1])
            drs_ef = drs * drs_flag
            cl_eff = p_cl + drs_ef * (self.car.cl_drs - p_cl)
            cd_eff = p_cd + drs_ef * (self.car.cd_drs - p_cd)
        else:
            drs_ef = ca.DM.zeros(N)
            cl_eff = p_cl
            cd_eff = p_cd

        opti.subject_to(drs >= 0)
        opti.subject_to(drs <= 1)

        # Current speed (k)
        v_k = v[:-1]

        # Race line curvature at current position
        sf = (1 - n[:-1] * kappa_grid[:-1])
        # d(n)/ds = sf * tan(phi)
        dn_ds = sf * ca.tan(phi[:-1])
        # d(phi)/ds = k_car * (ds_car / ds_center) - k_ref
        # ds_car / ds_center = sf / cos(phi)
        dphi_ds = k_car * (sf / ca.cos(phi[:-1])) - kappa_grid[:-1]
        # d(t)/ds = (ds_car / ds_center) / v
        dt_ds = sf / (v_k * ca.cos(phi[:-1])) 

        # Integrate geometry (euler)
        opti.subject_to(n[1:]   == n[:-1]   + dn_ds * ds)
        opti.subject_to(phi[1:] == phi[:-1] + dphi_ds * ds)
        opti.subject_to(T[1:]   == T[:-1]   + dt_ds * ds)

        #limit racing line to 2 m from center
        opti.subject_to(opti.bounded(-1,n,1))
        # limit heading error to ~30 degrees
        opti.subject_to(opti.bounded(-0.5, phi, 0.5)) 
        opti.subject_to(opti.bounded(-0.15, k_car, 0.15)) #limit max curvature

        # =========================
        # Aero
        # =========================
        F_drag = Car.get_aero_drag(self.car, v_k, cd=cd_eff)

        F_max_x, F_max_y = Car.get_friction_limit(
            self.car, v_k,
            mass=p_mass,
            mu_x=p_mu_x,
            mu_y=p_mu_y,
            cl=cl_eff
        )

        #change to car curvature
        # Lateral Force: F_lat = m * v^2 * curvature
        F_lat = p_mass * v_k**2 * ca.fabs(k_car)

        # Longitudinal force
        F_long_k = F_acc + F_brake

        # =========================
        # Pedal constraints
        # =========================
        opti.subject_to(F_acc >= 0)
        opti.subject_to(F_brake <= 0)

        # =========================
        # Friction ellipse
        # =========================
        term_long = (F_long_k / F_max_x)**2
        term_lat  = (F_lat    / F_max_y)**2
        opti.subject_to(term_long + term_lat <= 1)

        # =========================
        # Dynamics
        # =========================
        F_net = F_long_k - F_drag
        acc = F_net / p_mass

        # Speed integration: v^2 = v0^2 + 2*a*ds
        #but we have to use ds car 
        ds_car = ds * sf / ca.cos(phi[:-1])

        v_next_sq = v_k**2 + 2 * acc * ds_car
        opti.subject_to(v[1:]**2 == v_next_sq)

        # Time integration: T = T0 + ds/v
       # opti.subject_to(T[1:] == T[:-1] + ds_car / v_k)

        # Energy integration
        dE_step = (F_acc / self.car.power_eff + F_brake * self.car.regen_eff) * ds_car
        opti.subject_to(E[1:] == E[:-1] + dE_step / 3.6e6)  # J -> kWh

        # =========================
        # Torque limit (30 Nm per wheel)
        # =========================
        opti.subject_to(((self.car.wheel_radius * F_acc)) /
                    (self.car.max_torque * self.car.gear_ratio * 4) < 1)

        # =========================
        # Power limit
        # =========================
        opti.subject_to(((F_acc * v_k) / self.car.power_eff) / self.car.power_max <= 1)

        # Regen limit
        opti.subject_to(((-F_brake * v_k) * self.car.regen_eff) / self.car.brake_max <= 1)

        # Speed limits
        opti.subject_to(v >= 1.0)  # avoid division by zero

        # =========================
        # Initial conditions
        # =========================
        opti.subject_to(v[0] == init_cond.v)
        opti.subject_to(T[0] == init_cond.T)
        opti.subject_to(E[0] == init_cond.E)
        opti.subject_to(n[0] == 0) #start with car on the line
        opti.subject_to(phi[0] == 0) #start with zero heading error
        opti.subject_to(drs[0] == 0)  # start with DRS closed

        # =========================
        # Energy limitation
        # =========================
        if not self.endurance:
            if self.car.energy_limit is not None:
                opti.subject_to(E[-1] <= self.car.energy_limit)
        else:
            target = init_cond.E + init_cond.E_target
            opti.subject_to(E[-1] <= target)

        # =========================
        # Objective function
        # =========================
        J_Time = T[-1]

        F_norm_factor = (self.car.mass * self.car.g)**2
        J_overlap = 0.004 * ca.sum1((F_acc * -F_brake) / F_norm_factor)

        J_reg_steer = 1.0 * ca.sumsqr(k_car[1:] - k_car[:-1])

        W_line = 10
        J_line = W_line * ca.sumsqr(n)

        opti.minimize(J_Time + J_overlap)

        # =========================
        # Initial guess
        # =========================
        if init_guess is not None:
            opti.set_initial(v, 0.9*init_guess['v'])
            opti.set_initial(T, init_guess['t'])
            opti.set_initial(F_acc, 0.9*init_guess['F_acc'])
            opti.set_initial(F_brake,0.9* init_guess['F_brake'])
        else:
            opti.set_initial(v, 15.0)
            opti.set_initial(F_acc, 100.0)

        opti.set_initial(k_car,kappa_grid[:-1])

        # =========================
        # Solver setup
        # =========================
        p_opts = {'expand': True}
        s_opts = {'max_iter': 2000, 'tol': 1e-3, 'print_level': 5}

        opti.solver('ipopt', p_opts, s_opts)

        try:
            sol = opti.solve()

            # =========================
            # Sensitivity analysis
            # =========================
            L = opti.f + ca.dot(opti.lam_g, opti.g)

            sens_m   = sol.value(ca.jacobian(L, p_mass))
            sens_cl  = sol.value(ca.jacobian(L, p_cl))
            sens_cd  = sol.value(ca.jacobian(L, p_cd))
            sens_mux = sol.value(ca.jacobian(L, p_mu_x))
            sens_muy = sol.value(ca.jacobian(L, p_mu_y))

            print("sensitivity results")
            print(f"Mass: {sens_m:+.4f} s/kg")
            print(f"CL:   {sens_cl:+.4f} s/unit")
            print(f"CdA:  {sens_cd:+.4f} s/unit")

            return SolverResults(
                success=True,
                lap_time=sol.value(T[-1]),
                t=sol.value(T),
                v=sol.value(v),
                s=s_sim,
                kappa_car = sol.value(k_car),
                n=sol.value(n),
                xi=sol.value(phi),
                E=sol.value(E),
                f_acc=sol.value(F_acc) / self.car.power_eff,
                f_brake=sol.value(F_brake) * self.car.regen_eff,
                drs_usage=sol.value(drs_ef),
                sens={
                    'dTime_dm': sens_m,
                    'dTime_dCL': sens_cl,
                    'dTime_dCdA': sens_cd,
                    'dTime_dMuX': sens_mux,
                    'dTime_dMuY': sens_muy
                }
            )

        except RuntimeError as e:
            print(f"Solver failed: {e}")
            return SolverResults(False, 0, [], [], [], [], [], [], {})
--- KONEC SOUBORU: src\optimization\solvers\mpcc.py ---

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: src\optimization\solvers\quasi_steady.py ---
import os
import math
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from scipy.spatial import ConvexHull
from src.optimization.solvers.base_solver import BaseSolver, SolverResults, InitConditions


class Tire:
    def __init__(self, filename, Rl=0.2, rr=0.015):
        if not os.path.exists(filename):
            filename = os.path.join("src", "core", "pacejka94.txt")
            
        self.data = np.loadtxt(filename, delimiter=',')
        self.Br = self.data[0]
        self.Bt = self.data[1]
        self.Bx = self.data[2]
        self.By = self.data[3]
        self.Ct = self.data[4]
        self.Cx = self.data[5]
        self.Cy = self.data[6]
        self.Dr = self.data[7]
        self.Dt = self.data[8]
        self.Dx = self.data[9]
        self.Dy = self.data[10]
        self.Et = self.data[11]
        self.Ex = self.data[12]
        self.Ey = self.data[13]
        self.mu_y_grad = self.data[14]
        self.mu_x_grad = self.data[15]
        self.Rl = Rl                 
        self.rr = rr
        self.track_grip_factor = 0.75                 

    def Fy(self, alpha, Fz):
        Dy = (self.Dy - self.mu_y_grad * Fz) * self.track_grip_factor
        Fy = Fz * Dy * np.sin(self.Cy * np.arctan(self.By * alpha - self.Ey * (self.By * alpha - np.arctan(self.By * alpha))))
        return Fy
    
    def Fx(self, kappa, Fz):
        Dx = (self.Dx - self.mu_x_grad * Fz) * self.track_grip_factor
        resistance = Fz * self.rr
        Fx = Fz * Dx * np.sin(self.Cx * np.arctan(self.Bx * kappa - self.Ex * (self.Bx * kappa - np.arctan(self.Bx * kappa)))) - resistance
        return Fx
    
    def Mz(self, alpha, Fz):
        Fy = self.Fy(alpha, Fz)
        t = self.Dt * np.cos(self.Ct * np.arctan(self.Bt * alpha - self.Et * (self.Bt * alpha - np.arctan(self.Bt * alpha))))
        Mzr = self.Dr * np.cos(np.arctan(self.Br * alpha))
        Mz = t * Fy + Mzr
        return Mz


class QS_Car:
    def __init__(self, tire_file, ay_max, ax_max, v_max, m, CoGz, WB, TW, wd, CdA, ClA, CoP, P_max, gear_res, M_max, i, Rl):
        self.tire = Tire(tire_file, Rl=Rl)
        self.ay_max = ay_max         
        self.ax_max = ax_max         
        self.v_max = v_max           
        self.m = m                   
        self.CoGz = CoGz             
        self.WB = WB                 
        self.TW = TW                 
        self.wd = wd                 
        self.CdA = CdA               
        self.ClA = ClA               
        self.CoP = CoP               
        self.P_max = P_max           
        self.gear_res = gear_res     
        self.M_max = M_max          
        self.i = i
        self.brake_bias = 0.6             

    def Fd(self, v, rho=1.225): 
        return 0.5 * rho * self.CdA * v**2
    
    def Frr(self): 
        return self.m * 9.81 * self.tire.rr
    
    def Fgear(self, v): 
        return v * self.gear_res 
    
    def Fdown(self, v, rho=1.225): 
        return 0.5 * rho * self.ClA * v**2

    def Favailable(self, v): 
        v = max(v, 1.0) 
        omega_wheel = v / self.tire.Rl 
        omega_motor = omega_wheel * self.i  
        if omega_motor * 4 * self.M_max < self.P_max * 1000: 
            return 4 * self.M_max * self.i / self.tire.Rl  
        else:
            M = (self.P_max * 1000) / (omega_motor * 4)  
            return 4 * M * self.i / self.tire.Rl       

    def N_all(self, v, ax, ay):
        Fdown = self.Fdown(v)
        mf = self.m * self.wd 
        mr = self.m * (1 - self.wd)
        N_aero_f = Fdown * self.CoP
        N_aero_r = Fdown * (1 - self.CoP)
        
        N_FL = mf * 9.81 / 2 + N_aero_f / 2 - (mf * ay * self.CoGz) / self.TW - (self.m/2 * ax * self.CoGz) / self.WB
        N_FR = mf * 9.81 / 2 + N_aero_f / 2 + (mf * ay * self.CoGz) / self.TW - (self.m/2 * ax * self.CoGz) / self.WB
        N_RL = mr * 9.81 / 2 + N_aero_r / 2 - (mr * ay * self.CoGz) / self.TW + (self.m/2 * ax * self.CoGz) / self.WB
        N_RR = mr * 9.81 / 2 + N_aero_r / 2 + (mr * ay * self.CoGz) / self.TW + (self.m/2 * ax * self.CoGz) / self.WB

        return N_FL, N_FR, N_RL, N_RR
    
    def slip_angles(self, v, delta, beta, r):
        a1 = self.WB * (1 - self.wd)  
        a2 = self.WB * self.wd          
        vy = v * np.sin(-beta)

        vx_FL = (v - r * self.TW / 2) * np.cos(delta) + (vy + r * a1) * np.sin(delta)
        vx_FR = (v + r * self.TW / 2) * np.cos(delta) + (vy - r * a1) * np.sin(delta)
        vx_RL = (v - r * self.TW / 2)
        vx_RR = (v + r * self.TW / 2)

        vy_FL = -(v - r * self.TW / 2) * np.sin(delta) + (vy + r * a1) * np.cos(delta)
        vy_FR = -(v + r * self.TW / 2) * np.sin(delta) + (vy + r * a1) * np.cos(delta)
        vy_RL = vy - r * a2
        vy_RR = vy - r * a2

        alpha_FL = -np.arctan2(vy_FL, vx_FL)
        alpha_FR = -np.arctan2(vy_FR, vx_FR)
        alpha_RL = -np.arctan2(vy_RL, vx_RL)
        alpha_RR = -np.arctan2(vy_RR, vx_RR)

        return alpha_FL, alpha_FR, alpha_RL, alpha_RR
    
    def friction_ellipse_limit(self, Fx, Fy, Fz):
        if Fz <= 0.0:
            return 0.0, 0.0
            
        mu_y = self.tire.Dy - self.tire.mu_y_grad * Fz
        mu_x = self.tire.Dx - self.tire.mu_x_grad * Fz

        mu_x = max(mu_x, 1e-6)
        mu_y = max(mu_y, 1e-6)

        Fx_max = mu_x * Fz
        Fy_max = mu_y * Fz

        g = math.sqrt((Fx / Fx_max)**2 + (Fy / Fy_max)**2)

        if g <= 1.0:
            return Fx, Fy

        s = 1.0 / g
        return Fx * s, Fy * s
    
    def tire_forces(self, v, delta, beta, r, ay, ax, kappa):
        alpha_FL, alpha_FR, alpha_RL, alpha_RR = self.slip_angles(v, delta, beta, r)
        N_FL, N_FR, N_RL, N_RR = self.N_all(v, ax, ay)
        
        Fy_FL = self.tire.Fy(alpha_FL, N_FL)
        Fy_FR = self.tire.Fy(alpha_FR, N_FR)
        Fy_RL = self.tire.Fy(alpha_RL, N_RL)
        Fy_RR = self.tire.Fy(alpha_RR, N_RR)
        Favailable = self.Favailable(v)

        Fx_FL = min(self.tire.Fx(kappa, N_FL), Favailable / 4.0) 
        Fx_FR = min(self.tire.Fx(kappa, N_FR), Favailable / 4.0) 
        Fx_RL = min(self.tire.Fx(kappa, N_RL), Favailable / 4.0) 
        Fx_RR = min(self.tire.Fx(kappa, N_RR), Favailable / 4.0) 

        Fx_FL_w, Fy_FL_w = self.friction_ellipse_limit(Fx_FL, Fy_FL, N_FL)  
        Fx_FR_w, Fy_FR_w = self.friction_ellipse_limit(Fx_FR, Fy_FR, N_FR)
        Fx_RL_w, Fy_RL_w = self.friction_ellipse_limit(Fx_RL, Fy_RL, N_RL)
        Fx_RR_w, Fy_RR_w = self.friction_ellipse_limit(Fx_RR, Fy_RR, N_RR)
    
    
        if kappa < 0 and self.brake_bias is not None and 0 < self.brake_bias < 1:
         
            Fx_front_cap = Fx_FL_w + Fx_FR_w
            Fx_rear_cap = Fx_RL_w + Fx_RR_w

     
            F_total_limit_front = Fx_front_cap / self.brake_bias
            F_total_limit_rear = Fx_rear_cap / (1.0 - self.brake_bias)

          
            F_total_actual = max(F_total_limit_front, F_total_limit_rear)


            Fx_FL_w = (F_total_actual * self.brake_bias) / 2.0
            Fx_FR_w = (F_total_actual * self.brake_bias) / 2.0
            Fx_RL_w = (F_total_actual * (1.0 - self.brake_bias)) / 2.0
            Fx_RR_w = (F_total_actual * (1.0 - self.brake_bias)) / 2.0

        Fx_FL = Fx_FL_w * np.cos(delta) - Fy_FL_w * np.sin(delta)
        Fx_FR = Fx_FR_w * np.cos(delta) - Fy_FR_w * np.sin(delta)
        Fx_RL = Fx_RL_w
        Fx_RR = Fx_RR_w

        Fy_FL = Fx_FL_w * np.sin(delta) + Fy_FL_w * np.cos(delta)
        Fy_FR = Fx_FR_w * np.sin(delta) + Fy_FR_w * np.cos(delta)
        Fy_RL = Fy_RL_w
        Fy_RR = Fy_RR_w

        return Fx_FL, Fy_FL, Fx_FR, Fy_FR, Fx_RL, Fy_RL, Fx_RR, Fy_RR 

    def find_GGV(self, delta_max, beta_max, kappa_max, tol, N=9):
        delta = np.linspace(0, delta_max, N)  
        beta = np.linspace(0, beta_max, N)
        kappa = np.linspace(-kappa_max, kappa_max, N)  
        v = np.linspace(2, self.v_max, N)

        self.GGV_points = []
        point = []
        point_mirror = []

        print("calculating ggv diagram (in memory)...")
        for v_i in v:
            for kappa_i in kappa:
                for beta_i in beta:
                    for delta_i in delta:
                        ay_old = 0 
                        ay = 10
                        ax_old = 10
                        ax = 10
                        r_old = 0 
                        r = 0
                        i_while = 0

                        while abs(ay - ay_old) > tol:
                            ay_old = ay
                            r_old = r
                            ax_old = ax
                            i_while += 1 

                            Fx_FL, Fy_FL, Fx_FR, Fy_FR, Fx_RL, Fy_RL, Fx_RR, Fy_RR = self.tire_forces(v_i, delta_i, beta_i, r, ay, ax, kappa_i)   
                            F_drag = self.Fd(v_i)

                            Fy = Fy_FL + Fy_FR + Fy_RL + Fy_RR
                            Fx = Fx_FL + Fx_FR + Fx_RL + Fx_RR - F_drag

                            ax = Fx / self.m 
                            ay = Fy / self.m
                            r = ay / v_i

                            r = (1 * r + 2 * r_old) / 3
                            ay = (1 * ay + 2 * ay_old) / 3
                            ax = (1 * ax + 2 * ax_old) / 3

                            if i_while > 50:
                                ay_old = (ay_old + ay) / 2
                                ay = ay_old

                        point.append([ay, ax, v_i])
                        point_mirror.append([-ay, ax, v_i])

        data1 = np.array(point)  
        data_mirrored = np.array(point_mirror)
        self.GGV_points = np.vstack((data1, data_mirrored)) 

    def create_lookup_tables(self, steps=50):
        data = self.GGV_points
        unique_v = np.unique(data[:, 2])
        final_rows = []

        for v in unique_v:
            subset = data[data[:, 2] == v, :2] 
            if len(subset) < 3: continue

            subset_mirror = subset.copy()
            subset_mirror[:, 0] *= -1 
            full_cloud = np.vstack((subset, subset_mirror))

            try:
                hull = ConvexHull(full_cloud)
                hull_pts = full_cloud[hull.vertices]
            except:
                continue

            min_ay_idx = np.argmin(hull_pts[:, 0])
            max_ay_idx = np.argmax(hull_pts[:, 0])
            
            sorted_indices = np.argsort(hull_pts[:, 0])
            sorted_pts = hull_pts[sorted_indices]

            max_ay_abs = np.max(hull_pts[:, 0])
            input_ay = np.linspace(0, max_ay_abs, steps)
            
            limit_ax_throttle = []
            limit_ax_brake = []

            for ay_val in input_ay:
                y_candidates = []
                for i in range(len(hull_pts)):
                    p1 = hull_pts[i]
                    p2 = hull_pts[(i + 1) % len(hull_pts)]
                    x1, x2 = p1[0], p2[0]
                    
                    if (ay_val >= min(x1, x2)) and (ay_val <= max(x1, x2)):
                        if x1 != x2:
                            slope = (p2[1] - p1[1]) / (p2[0] - p1[0])
                            y_on_line = p1[1] + slope * (ay_val - p1[0])
                            y_candidates.append(y_on_line)
                        else:
                            y_candidates.append(max(p1[1], p2[1])) 

                if y_candidates:
                    limit_ax_throttle.append(max(y_candidates)) 
                    limit_ax_brake.append(min(y_candidates))    
                else:
                    limit_ax_throttle.append(0)
                    limit_ax_brake.append(0)

            max_ax = np.max(hull_pts[:, 1])
            min_ax = np.min(hull_pts[:, 1])
            
            input_ax_pos = np.linspace(0, max_ax, steps)
            input_ax_neg = np.linspace(0, min_ax, steps)
            
            limit_ay_throttle = [] 
            limit_ay_brake = []    

            def get_max_ay_for_ax(ax_val):
                x_candidates = []
                for i in range(len(hull_pts)):
                    p1 = hull_pts[i]
                    p2 = hull_pts[(i + 1) % len(hull_pts)]
                    y1, y2 = p1[1], p2[1]
                    if (ax_val >= min(y1, y2)) and (ax_val <= max(y1, y2)):
                        if y1 != y2:
                            slope_inv = (p2[0] - p1[0]) / (p2[1] - p1[1])
                            x_on_line = p1[0] + slope_inv * (ax_val - p1[1])
                            x_candidates.append(x_on_line)
                        else:
                            x_candidates.append(max(p1[0], p2[0]))
                if x_candidates: return np.max(np.abs(x_candidates))
                return 0.0

            for val in input_ax_pos:
                limit_ay_throttle.append(get_max_ay_for_ax(val))
                
            for val in input_ax_neg:
                limit_ay_brake.append(get_max_ay_for_ax(val))

            for i in range(steps):
                final_rows.append({
                    'v': v,
                    'input_ay': input_ay[i],
                    'input_ax_pos': input_ax_pos[i],
                    'input_ax_neg': input_ax_neg[i],
                    'limit_ax_throttle': limit_ax_throttle[i],
                    'limit_ax_brake': limit_ax_brake[i],
                    'limit_ay_throttle': limit_ay_throttle[i],
                    'limit_ay_brake': limit_ay_brake[i]
                })

        df = pd.DataFrame(final_rows)
        self.lookup_df = df
        return df


# ==========================================================
# Optimization Solver Wrapper
# ==========================================================

class QuasiSteadySolver(BaseSolver):
    def __init__(self, car, track, laps=1):
        super().__init__(car, track, laps)
        
        
        self.qs_car = QS_Car(
            tire_file="src/core/pacejka94.txt",
            ay_max=15.0,
            ax_max=15.0,
            v_max=40.0,
            m=self.car.weight,
            CoGz=self.car.cogz,
            WB=self.car.wb,
            TW=self.car.tw, 
            wd=self.car.wd,
            CdA=self.car.cd,
            ClA=self.car.cl,
            CoP=self.car.cop,
            P_max=(self.car.power_max / 1000.0)*self.car.power_eff, # kW
            gear_res=0.0,
            M_max=self.car.M_max,
            i=self.car.i,
            Rl=self.car.wheel_radius

        )
        #print(f"Power max: {self.qs_car.P_max}")


    def generate_ggv(self):
        self.qs_car.find_GGV(delta_max=0.2, beta_max=0.07, kappa_max=0.15, tol=0.1, N=9)
        self.df = self.qs_car.create_lookup_tables(steps=40)
        self._setup_luts()

    def set_ggv(self, df, slices_ax_pos, slices_ax_neg, slices_ay_throttle, slices_ay_brake):
       
        self.df = df
        self.slices_ax_pos = slices_ax_pos
        self.slices_ax_neg = slices_ax_neg
        self.slices_ay_throttle = slices_ay_throttle
        self.slices_ay_brake = slices_ay_brake
        self.v_levels = np.sort(self.df['v'].unique())

    def _setup_luts(self):
        self.v_levels = np.sort(self.df['v'].unique())

        self.slices_ax_pos = {}
        self.slices_ax_neg = {}
        self.slices_ay_throttle = {}
        self.slices_ay_brake = {}

        for v_i in self.v_levels:
            # Ax pos -> Ay max
            s1 = self.df[self.df['v'] == v_i].sort_values('input_ax_pos')
            ax1, inv1 = np.unique(s1['input_ax_pos'].to_numpy(), return_inverse=True)
            ay1 = np.bincount(inv1, weights=s1['limit_ay_throttle'].to_numpy()) / np.bincount(inv1)
            self.slices_ax_pos[v_i] = (ax1, ay1)

            # Ax neg -> Ay max
            s2 = self.df[self.df['v'] == v_i].sort_values('input_ax_neg')
            ax2, inv2 = np.unique(s2['input_ax_neg'].to_numpy(), return_inverse=True)
            ay2 = np.bincount(inv2, weights=s2['limit_ay_brake'].to_numpy()) / np.bincount(inv2)
            self.slices_ax_neg[v_i] = (ax2, ay2)

            # Ay -> Ax throttle
            s3 = self.df[self.df['v'] == v_i].sort_values('input_ay')
            ax3, inv3 = np.unique(s3['input_ay'].to_numpy(), return_inverse=True)
            ay3 = np.bincount(inv3, weights=s3['limit_ax_throttle'].to_numpy()) / np.bincount(inv3)
            self.slices_ay_throttle[v_i] = (ax3, ay3)

            # Ay -> Ax brake
            s4 = self.df[self.df['v'] == v_i].sort_values('input_ay')
            ax4, inv4 = np.unique(s4['input_ay'].to_numpy(), return_inverse=True)
            ay4 = np.bincount(inv4, weights=s4['limit_ax_brake'].to_numpy()) / np.bincount(inv4)
            self.slices_ay_brake[v_i] = (ax4, ay4)

    def _query_lut(self, slices, accel_input, v_input):
        vq = float(v_input)
        if vq <= self.v_levels[0]:
            v0 = v1 = self.v_levels[0]
        elif vq >= self.v_levels[-1]:
            v0 = v1 = self.v_levels[-1]
        else:
            j = np.searchsorted(self.v_levels, vq)
            v0 = self.v_levels[j-1]
            v1 = self.v_levels[j]

        def interp_on_v(v_i, axq):
            ax, ay = slices[v_i]
            axq_c = np.clip(axq, ax.min(), ax.max())
            return np.interp(axq_c, ax, ay)

        y0 = interp_on_v(v0, accel_input)
        y1 = interp_on_v(v1, accel_input)

        if v0 == v1: return float(y0)
        t = (vq - v0) / (v1 - v0)
        return float(y0 + t * (y1 - y0))

    def _find_v_ay_lim(self, radius_array, tol=0.1):
        v_test = np.ones(len(radius_array)) * 2.0
        ay_lim = np.zeros(len(radius_array))

        for i, R in enumerate(radius_array):
            if R > 5000:
                v_test[i] = self.qs_car.v_max
                ay_lim[i] = 0
                continue

            while True:
                ay_max = self._query_lut(self.slices_ax_pos, 0, v_test[i])
                ay_test = (v_test[i]**2) / R

                if ay_test > (ay_max - tol):
                    break

                v_test[i] += np.abs(ay_max - ay_test) * 0.5
                v_test[i] = min(v_test[i], self.qs_car.v_max)
                
                if v_test[i] >= self.qs_car.v_max:
                    break

            ay_lim[i] = (v_test[i]**2) / R

        return v_test, ay_lim

    def solve(self, init_cond: InitConditions, init_guess=None, ds=0.5) -> SolverResults:
        if not hasattr(self, 'df'):
            self.generate_ggv()

        # 1. evaluate track geometry
        total_length = self.track.length
        N = int(np.round(total_length / ds))
        s_eval = np.linspace(0, total_length, N + 1)
        
        kappa = np.array(self.track.get_curvature(s_eval)).flatten()
        radius = np.zeros_like(kappa)
        for i in range(len(kappa)):
            if abs(kappa[i]) < 1e-4:
                radius[i] = 10000.0
            else:
                radius[i] = 1.0 / abs(kappa[i])
       
        v_limit, ay_lim = self._find_v_ay_lim(radius, 0.1)
        
        n_points = len(v_limit)
        v_fwd = np.zeros(n_points)
        v_bwd = np.zeros(n_points)
        
        v_fwd[0] = max(init_cond.v, 1.0)
        v_bwd[-1] = v_limit[-1]

        max_jerk = 20.0  
        a_fwd = np.zeros(n_points)

        # forward pass
        for i in range(n_points - 1):
            ay = min((v_fwd[i]**2) / radius[i], ay_lim[i])
            ax_max_lut = self._query_lut(self.slices_ay_throttle, ay, v_fwd[i])
            
            F_avail = self.qs_car.Favailable(v_fwd[i])
            F_drag = self.qs_car.Fd(v_fwd[i]) + self.car.fix_drag
            ax_powertrain_limit = (F_avail - F_drag) / self.qs_car.m
            
            ax_max = min(ax_max_lut, ax_powertrain_limit)

            v_next = np.sqrt(max(v_fwd[i]**2 + 2 * ax_max * ds, 0))
            v_fwd[i+1] = min(v_next, v_limit[i+1])
    
        brake_driver_factor = 0.55
        
        # backward pass
        for i in range(n_points - 1, 0, -1):
            ay = min((v_bwd[i]**2) / radius[i], ay_lim[i])
            ax_min = np.abs(self._query_lut(self.slices_ay_brake, ay, v_bwd[i]))
            ax_min_effective = ax_min * brake_driver_factor
            v_prev = np.sqrt(max(v_bwd[i]**2 + 2 * ax_min_effective * ds, 0)) 
            v_bwd[i-1] = min(v_prev, v_limit[i-1])

        v_final = np.minimum(v_fwd, v_bwd)
        
        # 3. post process arrays to match user's expected format
        v_avg = np.maximum(1e-6, 0.5 * (v_final[:-1] + v_final[1:]))
        dt_array = ds / v_avg
        
        
        t_final = np.concatenate(([init_cond.T], init_cond.T + np.cumsum(dt_array)))

       
        acc_long = np.diff(v_final**2) / (2 * ds)
        
       
        f_net = self.car.weight * acc_long
        f_drag = np.array([self.car.get_aero_drag(v) for v in v_avg]).flatten()
        f_long_total = f_net + f_drag
        
        f_acc = np.maximum(f_long_total, 0)/self.car.power_eff
        f_brake = np.minimum(f_long_total, 0)   

        E_used = np.cumsum(f_acc * v_avg * dt_array) / 3.6e6

        SoC = (self.car.soc_start - E_used /self.car.acp_capacity)*100
        
       
        empty_controls = np.zeros_like(v_avg)   

        return SolverResults(
            success=True,
            lap_time=t_final[-1] - init_cond.T,
            t=t_final,              # N+1
            v=v_final,              # N+1
            s=s_eval,               # N+1
            f_acc=f_acc,            # N
            f_brake=f_brake,        # N
            E=E_used,               # N+1 
            SoC=SoC,                # N+1
            recuperation=empty_controls, # N
            drs_usage=empty_controls,    # N
            sens={}
        )
--- KONEC SOUBORU: src\optimization\solvers\quasi_steady.py ---

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: src\optimization\solvers\single_track.py ---
import casadi as ca
import numpy as np
from src.core.car import Car
from src.optimization.solvers.base_solver import BaseSolver, SolverResults, InitConditions

#does not work, i leave it here only for reference, it was an attempt but ended up being too slow and not converging well, you might want to use some of this code if you want to implement a working MPCC in the future

class SingleTrackSolver(BaseSolver):
    def __init__(self, car, track):

        super().__init__(car, track)

    def solve(self, init_cond: InitConditions,init_guess=None, ds=1.0):
        opti = ca.Opti()

        # =========================
        # Parameters
        # =========================
        p_mass = opti.parameter()
        p_cl   = opti.parameter()
        p_cd   = opti.parameter()
        p_mu_x = opti.parameter()
        p_mu_y = opti.parameter()

        # Set params values
        opti.set_value(p_mass, self.car.mass)
        opti.set_value(p_cl,   self.car.cl)
        opti.set_value(p_cd,   self.car.cd)
        opti.set_value(p_mu_x, self.car.mu_x)
        opti.set_value(p_mu_y, self.car.mu_y)

        # =========================
        # Track data & Grid
        # =========================
        total_length = self.track.length
        N = int(np.round(total_length / ds))
        
        s_sim = np.linspace(0, total_length, N + 1)
        ds_val = s_sim[1] - s_sim[0] # Actual uniform step
        
        # Curvature along the path
        kappa_grid = self.track.get_curvature(s_sim[:-1]) # Curvature at steps 0..N-1

        # =========================
        # Variables (States at k=0..N)
        # =========================
        # Note: We use N+1 points for states, N for controls
        vx  = opti.variable(N + 1) # Longitudinal speed
        vy  = opti.variable(N + 1) # Lateral speed
        r   = opti.variable(N + 1) # Yaw rate
        n   = opti.variable(N + 1) # Lateral deviation
        xi  = opti.variable(N + 1) # Heading error (psi - psi_track)
        t   = opti.variable(N + 1) # Time
        E   = opti.variable(N + 1) # Energy

        # =========================
        # Controls (at k=0..N-1)
        # =========================
        delta   = opti.variable(N) # Steering angle
        F_acc   = opti.variable(N) # Drive force (at wheels)
        F_brake = opti.variable(N) # Brake force (at wheels)
        drs     = opti.variable(N) # DRS State

        # =========================
        # Helper / Constants
        # =========================
        # Track limits (Tunnel)
        track_width_limit = 0.5 
        opti.subject_to(opti.bounded(-track_width_limit, n, track_width_limit))

        # Steering limits
        opti.subject_to(opti.bounded(-self.car.steeringAngle_max, delta, self.car.steeringAngle_max))

        # Speed limit (avoid singularity at 0)
        opti.subject_to(vx >= 1.0) 

        # Pedal constraints
        opti.subject_to(F_acc >= 0)
        opti.subject_to(F_brake <= 0)

        # DRS Logic
        use_drs_vec = False # Flag to control indexing inside loop

        if self.car.drs_allowed and self.track.drs is not None:
            drs_flag = self.track.get_drs_flag(s_sim[:-1])
            drs_ef = drs * drs_flag
            
            cl_eff = p_cl + drs_ef * (self.car.cl_drs - p_cl)
            cd_eff = p_cd + drs_ef * (self.car.cd_drs - p_cd)
            use_drs_vec = True
        else:
            drs_ef = ca.DM.zeros(N)
            cl_eff = p_cl # Scalar parameter
            cd_eff = p_cd # Scalar parameter
            opti.subject_to(drs == 0)
            use_drs_vec = False #

        opti.subject_to(drs >= 0)
        opti.subject_to(drs <= 1)

        
        # Slip limit for linear model validity (5 degrees in rad)
        alpha_limit = 5.0 * (np.pi / 180.0)

        for k in range(N):
            # Current states
            vx_k = vx[k]
            vy_k = vy[k]
            r_k  = r[k]
            n_k  = n[k]
            xi_k = xi[k]
            t_k  = t[k]
            
            # Current inputs
            delta_k   = delta[k]
            F_acc_k   = F_acc[k]
            F_brake_k = F_brake[k]
            
            curv_k = kappa_grid[k]

            # 1. Force distribution
            
            Ff_acc = self.car.power_dist * F_acc_k
            Fr_acc = (1.0 - self.car.power_dist) * F_acc_k
            
            Ff_brk = self.car.brake_bias * F_brake_k
            Fr_brk = (1.0 - self.car.brake_bias) * F_brake_k
            
            Fx_f = Ff_acc + Ff_brk
            Fx_r = Fr_acc + Fr_brk

            # 2. Normal loads (fz)
            
            if use_drs_vec:
                cl_k = cl_eff[k]
                cd_k = cd_eff[k]
            else:
                cl_k = cl_eff 
                cd_k = cd_eff

            # Fz calculations
            fz_aero = 0.5 * self.car.rho * cl_k * vx_k**2
            fz_aero_f = fz_aero * (1 - self.car.cop)
            fz_aero_r = fz_aero * self.car.cop
            
            Fz_f = self.car.fz_static_f + fz_aero_f
            Fz_r = self.car.fz_static_r + fz_aero_r

            # 3. Slip angles - kinematics
            # alpha_f = -atan2(...)
            # alpha_r = -atan2(...)
            
            # Front slip
            vy_f_kin = (vy_k + self.car.lf * r_k)
            alpha_f = -ca.atan2(
                vy_f_kin * ca.cos(delta_k) - vx_k * ca.sin(delta_k),
                vy_f_kin * ca.sin(delta_k) + vx_k * ca.cos(delta_k)
            )

            # Rear slip
            vy_r_kin = (vy_k - self.car.lr * r_k)
            alpha_r = -ca.atan2(vy_r_kin, vx_k)

            # 4. Lateral forces
            # Fy = (alpha_deg / 5.0) * Fz * mu_y
            # Convert alpha to deg: alpha * 180/pi
            # Formula: alpha_rad * (180/pi) / 5.0 * Fz * mu_y
            #        = alpha_rad * (36.0/pi) * Fz * mu_y
            
            Fy_f_lin = alpha_f * (36.0 / np.pi) * Fz_f * p_mu_y
            Fy_r_lin = alpha_r * (36.0 / np.pi) * Fz_r * p_mu_y

            # 5. constraints
            
            # A) Hard limit on slip angle to keep linear model valid
            opti.subject_to(opti.bounded(-alpha_limit, alpha_f, alpha_limit))
            opti.subject_to(opti.bounded(-alpha_limit, alpha_r, alpha_limit))

            # B) Friction ellipse 
            # (Fx / Fx_max)^2 + (Fy / Fy_max)^2 <= 1
            # Fx_max = mu_x * Fz, Fy_max = mu_y * Fz
        
            
            mu_x_fz_f = p_mu_x * Fz_f
            mu_y_fz_f = p_mu_y * Fz_f
            
            mu_x_fz_r = p_mu_x * Fz_r
            mu_y_fz_r = p_mu_y * Fz_r

            ellipse_f = (Fx_f / mu_x_fz_f)**2 + (Fy_f_lin / mu_y_fz_f)**2
            ellipse_r = (Fx_r / mu_x_fz_r)**2 + (Fy_r_lin / mu_y_fz_r)**2
            
            opti.subject_to(ellipse_f <= 1.0)
            opti.subject_to(ellipse_r <= 1.0)

            
            # Power limit: P = F * v <= P_max
            opti.subject_to( ((F_acc_k * vx_k) / self.car.power_eff)/self.car.power_max <= 1 )
            
            # Regen limit
            opti.subject_to( ((-F_brake_k * vx_k) * self.car.regen_eff)/self.car.brake_max <= 1 )

        
            # Drag
            F_drag = 0.5 * self.car.rho * cd_k * vx_k**2 + self.car.fix_drag

            # Body frame forces
            # Sum forces in x and y direction
            F_x_total = Fx_f * ca.cos(delta_k) - Fy_f_lin * ca.sin(delta_k) + Fx_r - F_drag
            F_y_total = Fx_f * ca.sin(delta_k) + Fy_f_lin * ca.cos(delta_k) + Fy_r_lin
            
            # Moment
            M_z = self.car.lf * (Fx_f * ca.sin(delta_k) + Fy_f_lin * ca.cos(delta_k)) - self.car.lr * Fy_r_lin

            # Time derivatives (dt)
            dvx_dt = F_x_total / p_mass + r_k * vy_k
            dvy_dt = F_y_total / p_mass - r_k * vx_k
            dr_dt  = M_z / self.car.Iz
            
            # Path scaling factor (Sf = dt/ds)
            # d_psi/dt = r
            # d_theta/dt = vx * curvature? No, d_theta/ds = curvature -> d_theta/dt = ds/dt * curvature
            # We need Sf = 1 / (ds/dt)
            # s_dot = (vx * cos(xi) - vy * sin(xi)) / (1 - n * curvature)
            
            # eps (in code) is xi (here). 
           
            s_dot_denom = (1.0 - n_k * curv_k)
            s_dot_num   = (vx_k * ca.cos(xi_k) - vy_k * ca.sin(xi_k))
            
            # Avoid division by zero
            ds_dt = s_dot_num / ca.fmax(s_dot_denom, 0.1)
            Sf = 1.0 / ds_dt  # dt/ds

            # Spatial derivatives (d/ds = d/dt * dt/ds)
            dvx_ds = dvx_dt * Sf
            dvy_ds = dvy_dt * Sf
            dr_ds  = dr_dt  * Sf
            dt_ds  = Sf
            
            # Kinematics in path coordinates
            # dn/dt = vx * sin(xi) + vy * cos(xi)
            dn_ds = (vx_k * ca.sin(xi_k) + vy_k * ca.cos(xi_k)) * Sf
            
            # dxi/ds = dpsi/ds - dtheta/ds = r * Sf - curvature
            dxi_ds = r_k * Sf - curv_k

            # Energy
            dE_ds = ((F_acc_k / self.car.power_eff + F_brake_k * self.car.regen_eff) * Sf) / 3.6e6

            # Euler Step
            opti.subject_to(vx[k+1] == vx_k + dvx_ds * ds_val)
            opti.subject_to(vy[k+1] == vy_k + dvy_ds * ds_val)
            opti.subject_to(r[k+1]  == r_k  + dr_ds  * ds_val)
            opti.subject_to(n[k+1]  == n_k  + dn_ds  * ds_val)
            opti.subject_to(xi[k+1] == xi_k + dxi_ds * ds_val)
            opti.subject_to(t[k+1]  == t_k  + dt_ds  * ds_val)
            opti.subject_to(E[k+1] == E[k] + dE_ds * ds_val)


        # =========================
        # Boundary Conditions
        # =========================
        # Start
        opti.subject_to(vx[0] == init_cond.v)
        opti.subject_to(vy[0] == 0) # Assume 0 lateral speed at start
        opti.subject_to(r[0]  == 0) # Assume 0 yaw rate at start (or match curvature)
        opti.subject_to(n[0]  == init_cond.n)
        opti.subject_to(xi[0] == init_cond.xi)
        opti.subject_to(t[0]  == init_cond.T)
        opti.subject_to(E[0]  == init_cond.E)

        # Cyclic condition (if needed, enable for closed track)
        # opti.subject_to(vx[0] == vx[-1])
        
        # Energy limit
        if self.car.energy_limit is not None:
            opti.subject_to(E[-1] <= self.car.energy_limit)

        # =========================
        # Objective function
        # =========================
        J_Time = t[-1]
        
        # Regularization terms
        # 1. Steering Rate (smooth steering)
        # sum (delta_k - delta_k-1)^2
        diff_delta = delta[1:] - delta[:-1]
        J_steer_rate = 7e-4 * ca.sumsqr(diff_delta)
        
        # 2. Acceleration smoothness (throttle smoothing)
        diff_acc = F_acc[1:] - F_acc[:-1]
        J_acc_smooth = 1e-3 * ca.sumsqr(diff_acc) / 1000.0 # Scale down force magnitude

        # 3. Path deviation (stay close to center/opt line)
        J_path = 0.5 * ca.sumsqr(n)
        #0.5 funguje mega dobre

    
        opti.minimize(J_Time+J_path+J_steer_rate)
        #opti.minimize(J_Time)

        # =========================
        # Initial guess 
        # =========================
   
        # Mapping guess to variables
       
        g_vx = init_guess['v']
        g_n  = init_guess['n']
        g_xi = init_guess['xi']
        g_r  = init_guess['r']
        g_t  = init_guess['t']
        g_delta = init_guess['delta']
        g_facc  = init_guess['F_acc']
        g_fbrk  = init_guess['F_brake']

        # set initial (handling minimal length mismatch if any)
        L_state = min(len(g_vx), N + 1)
        opti.set_initial(vx[:L_state], g_vx[:L_state])
        opti.set_initial(vy[:L_state], 0.0) # Start with 0 slip
        opti.set_initial(n[:L_state],  g_n[:L_state])
        opti.set_initial(xi[:L_state], g_xi[:L_state])
        opti.set_initial(r[:L_state],  g_r[:L_state])
        opti.set_initial(t[:L_state],  g_t[:L_state])
        
        L_ctrl = min(len(g_delta), N)
        opti.set_initial(delta[:L_ctrl], g_delta[:L_ctrl])
        opti.set_initial(F_acc[:L_ctrl], g_facc[:L_ctrl])
        opti.set_initial(F_brake[:L_ctrl], g_fbrk[:L_ctrl])

        # =========================
        # Solver
        # =========================
        p_opts = {"expand": True}
        s_opts = {
            "max_iter": 3000, 
            "tol": 1e-3, 
            "print_level": 5, 
            "mu_strategy": "adaptive" 
        }
        opti.solver("ipopt", p_opts, s_opts)

        try:
            sol = opti.solve()
            
            # --- Post processing / Sensitivities ---
            L_lagrange = opti.f + ca.dot(opti.lam_g, opti.g)
            sens_m = sol.value(ca.jacobian(L_lagrange, p_mass))
            sens_cl = sol.value(ca.jacobian(L_lagrange, p_cl))
        
            res_vx = sol.value(vx)
            res_vy = sol.value(vy)
            res_r  = sol.value(r)
            res_delta = sol.value(delta)
            
            # Recompute alphas for output (k=0..N-1)
            # (Simplified re-calculation for visualization)
            res_alpha_f = []
            res_alpha_r = []
            for k in range(N):
                v_x, v_y, yaw_r, delt = res_vx[k], res_vy[k], res_r[k], res_delta[k]
                vy_f = v_y + self.car.lf * yaw_r
                af = -np.arctan2(vy_f * np.cos(delt) - v_x * np.sin(delt), vy_f * np.sin(delt) + v_x * np.cos(delt))
                vy_r = v_y - self.car.lr * yaw_r
                ar = -np.arctan2(vy_r, v_x)
                res_alpha_f.append(af)
                res_alpha_r.append(ar)

            return SolverResults(
                success=True,
                lap_time=sol.value(t[-1]),
                t=sol.value(t),
                v=sol.value(vx), # approx total speed
                s=s_sim,
                f_acc=sol.value(F_acc) / self.car.power_eff,
                f_brake=sol.value(F_brake) * self.car.regen_eff,
                delta=sol.value(delta),
                vx=res_vx,
                vy=res_vy,
                r=res_r,
                n=sol.value(n),
                xi=sol.value(xi),
                alpha_f=np.array(res_alpha_f),
                alpha_r=np.array(res_alpha_r),
                E=sol.value(E),
                drs_usage=sol.value(drs_ef),
                sens={"dTime_dm": sens_m, "dTime_dCL": sens_cl}
            )

        except RuntimeError as e:
            print(f"Solver failed: {e}")
            # Return partial results (debug values) if available
            return SolverResults(False, 0.0, [], [], [], [], [], [], {}, mu_x=self.car.mu_x, mu_y=self.car.mu_y)
--- KONEC SOUBORU: src\optimization\solvers\single_track.py ---

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: src\optimization\solvers\strategy_maker.py ---
import casadi as ca
from src.optimization.solvers.base_solver import BaseSolver


class StrategyMaker(BaseSolver):

    def __init__(self,car,laptime_map,laps,e_target_min,e_target_max):
        self.car = car
        self.laptime_map = laptime_map
        self.laps = laps
        self.e_target_min = e_target_min
        self.e_target_max = e_target_max
        pass
 

    def ef_guess(self,energy_target_endurance):
        #this function is used only as a backup if solver fails
        #simulate the car with x kWh with max regen to estimate winning EF_score
        N = self.laps
        total_time = 0
        energy_target_lap = energy_target_endurance/self.laps
        SoC_start = 0.8

        #simulate the race
        for i in range(0,N,1):

            SoC_current = SoC_start - i*(energy_target_lap/self.car.acp_capacity)
            laptime = float(self.laptime_map([SoC_current,energy_target_lap,0.94]))
            total_time += laptime
            #print(f"lap {i+1}, laptime {laptime:.2f}, SoC start: {SoC_current:.2f} SoC end: {SoC_start - (i+1)*(energy_target_lap/self.car.acp_capacity):.2f}")
        
        ef_score = total_time**2*energy_target_endurance
        #print(f"Total time: {total_time}")
        #print(f"Ef_score {ef_score}")

        return ef_score
    

    def best_T_guess(self,delta):
        #this function is used only as a backup if solver fails
        #simulate the car with full energy usage to simulate best time
        N = self.laps
        total_time = 0
        energy_target_lap = (self.car.acp_capacity-0.4)/self.laps
        SoC_start = 1.0

        #simulate the race
        for i in range(0,N,1):

            SoC_current = SoC_start - i*(energy_target_lap/self.car.acp_capacity)
            laptime = float(self.laptime_map([SoC_current,energy_target_lap,0.94]))
            total_time += laptime
            print(f"lap {i+1}, laptime {laptime:.2f}, SoC start: {SoC_current:.2f} SoC end: {SoC_start - (i+1)*(energy_target_lap/self.car.acp_capacity):.2f}")
        
        
        print(f"Total time: {total_time}")
        #print(f"Ef_score {ef_score}")
        return total_time*(1-delta)
    
    def getPoints(self,regen,energy_target,delta,ef_min,t_min):
         #this function is used only as a backup if solver fails
        total_time = 0
        SoC_start = 1.0
        energy_target_lap = energy_target/self.laps

         #simulate the race
        for i in range(0,self.laps,1):

            SoC_current = SoC_start - i*(energy_target_lap/self.car.acp_capacity)
            if SoC_current < 0.05: 
                return 0.0
            laptime = float(self.laptime_map([SoC_current,energy_target_lap,regen]))
            total_time += laptime


        #calculate total points from endurance
        T_min_adj = t_min*(1-delta)
        T_max = 1.5 * T_min_adj # Podle tabulky 11 v pravidlech            
        P_max_end = 250.0
        P_min_end = 0.1 * P_max_end

        if total_time > T_max:
            nom_end = 0.0
        elif total_time < T_min_adj:
            nom_end = 1.0
        else:
            nom_end = (T_max - total_time) / (T_max - T_min_adj)
            
        Endurance_points = (P_max_end - P_min_end) * (nom_end**2) + P_min_end

        if total_time > T_max: 
            Endurance_points = 0.0

        P_max_eff = 75.0
        EF_max = 2 * ef_min
        Ef_team = (total_time**2) * energy_target

        if Ef_team > EF_max:
            nom_eff = 0.0
        elif Ef_team < ef_min:
            nom_eff = 1.0
        else:
            nom_eff = (EF_max - Ef_team) / (EF_max - ef_min)
            
        Efficiency_points = P_max_eff * (nom_eff**2)
        

        return Endurance_points + Efficiency_points


    def solve(self,ef_min=0,T_min=0,objective="points",enforce_full_soc = False,):
            opti = ca.Opti()
            N = self.laps

            E_lap = opti.variable(N)

            
            SoC_start = opti.variable()

            if enforce_full_soc:
                opti.subject_to(SoC_start == 1.0)
        
            
            # Stav baterie (SoC) na začátku každého kola + na úplném konci závodu (N+1)
            SoC = opti.variable(N + 1)    

            MIN_SOC = 0.11

    
            opti.subject_to(SoC[0] == SoC_start) 
        

            total_time = 0
            
            for k in range(N):
            
                opti.subject_to(SoC[k+1] == SoC[k] - (E_lap[k] / self.car.acp_capacity))
               
                state_vec = ca.vertcat(SoC[k], E_lap[k], self.car.regen_eff)
                laptime = self.laptime_map(state_vec)
    
                total_time += laptime


    
            opti.subject_to(SoC >= MIN_SOC)
            opti.subject_to(SoC_start >= 0.5)
            opti.subject_to(SoC_start <= 1.0)
            #opti.subject_to(SoC <= 1.0)
            
        
            opti.subject_to(E_lap >= self.e_target_min) 
            opti.subject_to(E_lap <= self.e_target_max) 


            E_guess = 0.25
            opti.set_initial(E_lap, E_guess)
            opti.set_initial(SoC_start, 1.0)
            soc_guess = [1.0 - k * (E_guess / self.car.acp_capacity) for k in range(N + 1)]
            opti.set_initial(SoC, soc_guess)
           
     
            
            T_max = 1.5 * T_min # Podle tabulky 11 v pravidlech
            
            P_max_end = 250.0
            P_min_end = 0.1 * P_max_end 

            # ==========================================
            # Endurance Score
            # ==========================================
            if objective == "points":
                opti.subject_to(total_time <= T_max) 
            
            nom_end = (T_max - total_time) / (T_max - T_min)
            Endurance_points = (P_max_end - P_min_end) * (nom_end**2) + P_min_end

            # ==========================================
            # Efficiency Score
            # ==========================================
            P_max_eff = 75.0
            EF_max = 2 * ef_min
            Ef_team = (total_time**2) * ca.sum1(E_lap)

            if objective == "points":
                opti.subject_to(Ef_team <= EF_max)

            nom_eff = (EF_max - Ef_team) / (EF_max - ef_min)
            Efficiency_points = P_max_eff * (nom_eff**2)
            
            # ==========================================
            # Objective Function
            # ==========================================
            Total_points = Endurance_points + Efficiency_points
            
            if objective == "points":
                opti.minimize(-Total_points)

            elif objective == "time":
                opti.minimize(total_time)

            elif objective == "efficiency":
                opti.minimize(Ef_team)
            
            # ==========================================
            # 8. Solver settings
            # ==========================================
            p_opts = {"expand": True}
            s_opts = {
                "max_iter": 5000, 
                "tol": 1e-3,                   
                "acceptable_tol": 1e-2,           
                "print_level": 0,
                "hessian_approximation": "limited-memory"
            }
            opti.solver('ipopt', p_opts, s_opts)

            try:
                
                sol = opti.solve()
                
                print(f"Total time: {sol.value(total_time):.2f} s")
                
               
                opt_soc = sol.value(SoC)
                opt_e_targets = sol.value(E_lap)
                
                if N == 1:
                    opt_e_targets = [opt_e_targets]
                    opt_soc = [opt_soc, sol.value(SoC)[1]]

                opt_lap_times = []
                
               
                for k in range(N):
                    
                    soc_k = float(opt_soc[k])
                    e_k = float(opt_e_targets[k])
                    regen_k = float(self.car.regen_eff)
                    
                   
                    lap_time_k = float(sol.value(self.laptime_map(ca.vertcat(soc_k, e_k, regen_k))))
                    opt_lap_times.append(lap_time_k)
                    
                    #happens when solver tries something outside of the laptime map, which evaluates the laptime to zero
                    if lap_time_k < 1: 
                        print(f"   Laptime: {lap_time_k:.2f} s")
                        print(f"   Vstupy -> SoC_start: {soc_k:.4f}, E_lap: {e_k:.4f}, Regen: {regen_k:.2f}")
                        return {"success":False}

                opt_total_time = sol.value(total_time)

                return {
                    "success": True,
                    "total_time": opt_total_time,
                    "e_targets": opt_e_targets,     
                    "soc_profile": opt_soc,
                    "soc_start": sol.value(SoC_start),
                    "lap_times": opt_lap_times,
                    "points": sol.value(Total_points),
                    "ef_score": sol.value(Ef_team)
                }
                
            except RuntimeError as e:
                print(f"Solver failed: {e}")
                opti.debug.show_infeasibilities()
                return {"success": False}

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: src\optimization\tasks\base_task.py ---
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod

@dataclass
class SolverPayload:
    kind: str
    data: object
    meta: dict = field(default_factory=dict)

class SimulationTask(ABC):
    #abstract base class from which all simulation tasks inherit
    
    @abstractmethod
    def execute(self, progress_callback) -> SolverPayload:
        """
        Method called by the worker, returns a SolverPayload with the results
        """
        pass
--- KONEC SOUBORU: src\optimization\tasks\base_task.py ---

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: src\optimization\tasks\laptime_map_task.py ---
import os
import time
import numpy as np
import json
from dataclasses import asdict
from scipy.interpolate import griddata
from src.optimization.solvers.base_solver import InitConditions
from .base_task import SimulationTask, SolverPayload

class LaptimeMapTask(SimulationTask):
    def __init__(self, solver, car, track, ds, laps):
        self.solver = solver
        self.car = car
        self.track = track
        self.ds = ds
        self.laps = laps

    def execute(self, progress_callback) -> SolverPayload:
        start = time.time()
        
        acp_targets = np.linspace(3.5, 9.0, num=10, endpoint=True)
        e_targets = np.round(acp_targets / self.laps, 2)
        regen_targets = np.linspace(0.0, 1.1, num=10, endpoint=True)
        soc_targets = np.linspace(0.1, 1.05, num=10, endpoint=True)

        total_steps = len(e_targets) * len(regen_targets) * len(soc_targets)
        results_log = []
        flat_lap_times = []
        current_step = 0
        last_slowest_lap = 40
        prev_res = None

        for soc in soc_targets:
            for e_target in e_targets:
                for regen in regen_targets:
                    current_step += 1
                    progress_callback(current_step, total_steps)
                    
                    if soc * self.car.acp_capacity < e_target:
                        flat_lap_times.append(np.nan)
                        continue
                    
                    self.car.regen_eff = regen
                    self.car.energy_limit = e_target
                    E_start = np.round(self.car.acp_capacity * (1-soc), 2)
                    init_cond = InitConditions(v=self.track.speed[0]-2, T=0.0, E=E_start)
                    
                    res = self.solver.solve(init_cond=init_cond, ds=self.ds, init_guess=prev_res)
                    
                    if res.success:
                        laptime = res.lap_time
                        flat_lap_times.append(laptime)
                        prev_res = {
                            'v': res.v,
                            'F_acc': res.f_acc 
                        }
                        if laptime > last_slowest_lap:
                            last_slowest_lap = laptime

                        res.meta_soc = soc
                        res.meta_energy = e_target
                        res.meta_regen = regen
                        res.meta_mass = self.car.weight
                        results_log.append(res)
                    else:
                        flat_lap_times.append(np.nan) 

        flat_lap_times = np.array(flat_lap_times, dtype=float)
        
        if np.isnan(flat_lap_times).any():
            S, E_grid, R = np.meshgrid(soc_targets, e_targets, regen_targets, indexing='ij')
            points = np.column_stack((S.ravel(), E_grid.ravel(), R.ravel()))
            values = flat_lap_times
            valid_mask = ~np.isnan(values)
            
            if valid_mask.any():
                valid_points = points[valid_mask]
                valid_values = values[valid_mask]
                filled_values = griddata(valid_points, valid_values, points, method='nearest')
                flat_lap_times = filled_values

        end = time.time()
        print(f"Time elapsed: {end - start:.6f} s")

        save_dir = os.path.join("data", "tracks", "lapmaps")
        os.makedirs(save_dir, exist_ok=True)
        file_path = os.path.join(save_dir, f"{self.track.name}_laptime_map.npz")

        # Extract car config as json string (selected parameters only)
        allowed_keys = ["name", "weight", "cd", "cl", "acp_capacity", "drs_allowed", "power_max"]
        car_dict = {k: getattr(self.car, k) for k in allowed_keys if hasattr(self.car, k)}
        car_json = json.dumps(car_dict)

        np.savez_compressed(
            file_path,
            soc_targets=np.array(soc_targets),
            e_targets=np.array(e_targets),
            regen_targets=np.array(regen_targets),
            flat_lap_times=np.array(flat_lap_times), 
            laps=np.array(self.laps),
            acp_capacity=np.array(self.car.acp_capacity),
            car_config=np.array([car_json])
        )

        return SolverPayload(
            kind="acp_test",
            data=results_log,
            meta={"laps": self.laps, "car": self.car, "track": self.track}
        )

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: src\optimization\tasks\optimization_task.py ---
import optuna
from dataclasses import dataclass
from src.core.car import Car
from src.core.track import Track
from src.optimization.solvers.quasi_steady import QuasiSteadySolver
from src.optimization.solvers.base_solver import InitConditions
from src.optimization.tasks.base_task import SimulationTask, SolverPayload


@dataclass
class OptimizationPayload:
    best_params: dict
    best_lap_time: float
    study: optuna.Study
    base_vehicle_name: str
    base_car_params: dict  
    tracks: list           

class OptimizationTask(SimulationTask):
    def __init__(self, car_params: dict, ds: float, vehicle_name: str, tracks: list, bounds: dict):
        super().__init__()
        self.base_car_params = car_params
        self.ds = ds
        self.vehicle_name = vehicle_name
        self.bounds = bounds
       
        self.n_trials = 10
        
        self.loaded_tracks = [Track.load(t_name) for t_name in tracks]

    def execute(self, progress_callback=None):
        

        def objective(trial):
            
            current_params = self.base_car_params.copy()
            for param_name, (p_min, p_max) in self.bounds.items():
               
                val = trial.suggest_float(param_name, p_min, p_max)
                current_params[param_name] = val

          
            car = Car(**current_params)

            base_solver = QuasiSteadySolver(car, self.loaded_tracks[0])
            base_solver.generate_ggv()
            
            total_lap_time = 0.0
            
          
            for track in self.loaded_tracks:
               
                if track.speed is not None:
                    init_cond = InitConditions(v=track.speed[0], T=0.0, E=0.0)
                else:
                    init_cond = InitConditions(v=5, T=0.0, E=0.0)

                solver = QuasiSteadySolver(car, track)

                solver.set_ggv(
                    df=base_solver.df,
                    slices_ax_pos=base_solver.slices_ax_pos,
                    slices_ax_neg=base_solver.slices_ax_neg,
                    slices_ay_throttle=base_solver.slices_ay_throttle,
                    slices_ay_brake=base_solver.slices_ay_brake
                )
                
                res = solver.solve(ds=self.ds, init_cond=init_cond)
                
          
                total_lap_time += res.lap_time
                
            return total_lap_time

        optuna.logging.set_verbosity(optuna.logging.WARNING)

        study = optuna.create_study(direction="minimize")
        
        
        def optuna_callback(study, trial):
            if progress_callback:
                
                progress_callback(len(study.trials), self.n_trials)


        study.optimize(objective, n_trials=self.n_trials, callbacks=[optuna_callback])
        
        
        results = OptimizationPayload(
            best_params=study.best_params,
            best_lap_time=study.best_value,
            study=study,
            base_vehicle_name=self.vehicle_name,
            base_car_params=self.base_car_params,        
            tracks=[t.name for t in self.loaded_tracks]
        )

        return SolverPayload(
            kind="optimization",
            data=results,
            meta={
                    "car": self.vehicle_name,
                    "tracks": [t.name for t in self.loaded_tracks],   
                }
        )
