--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: src\optimization\tasks\single_lap.py ---
import time
from src.optimization.solvers.base_solver import InitConditions
from .base_task import SimulationTask, SolverPayload

class SingleLapTask(SimulationTask):
    def __init__(self, solver, car, track, ds):
        self.solver = solver
        self.car = car
        self.track = track
        self.ds = ds

    def execute(self, progress_callback) -> SolverPayload:
        start = time.time()

        E = self.car.acp_capacity * (1 - self.car.soc_start)
        
        if self.track.speed is not None:
            init_cond = InitConditions(v=self.track.speed[0], T=0.0, E=E)
        else:
            init_cond = InitConditions(v=5, T=0.0, E=E)

       # init_guess = calculate_initial_guess(self.car, self.track, init_cond, ds=self.ds)
        
        #ggv = self.solver.qs_car.find_GGV(delta_max=0.2, beta_max=0.07, kappa_max=0.15, tol=0.1, N=9)
        res = self.solver.solve(init_cond, ds=self.ds, init_guess=None)
        
        end = time.time()
        print(f"Time elapsed: {end - start:.2f} s")
        
        if not res.success:
            raise Exception("Optimization failed for single lap.")
        
        return SolverPayload(
            kind="single_lap",
            data=res,
            meta={"ds": self.ds, "car": self.car, "track": self.track}
        )

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: src\optimization\tasks\strategy.py ---
import numpy as np
from scipy.interpolate import RegularGridInterpolator
from .base_task import SimulationTask, SolverPayload

class StrategyOptTask(SimulationTask):
    def __init__(self, solver, car):
        self.solver = solver
        self.car = car

    def execute(self, progress_callback) -> SolverPayload:
        ef_min_res = self.solver.solve(0,0,objective="efficiency")
        ef_min = ef_min_res["ef_score"] if ef_min_res["success"] else self.solver.ef_guess(4)

        t_min_res = self.solver.solve(0,0,objective="time")
        t_min = t_min_res["total_time"] if t_min_res["success"] else self.solver.best_T_guess(0)

        regen_targets = np.linspace(0.15, 1.0, num=10, endpoint=True)
        delta_targets = np.linspace(0.0, 0.15, num=10, endpoint=True)

        e_opt_grid = np.zeros((len(regen_targets), len(delta_targets)))
        soc_opt_grid = np.zeros((len(regen_targets), len(delta_targets)))

        current_step = 0
        total_steps = len(regen_targets) * len(delta_targets)

        for i, regen_target in enumerate(regen_targets):
            for j, delta_target in enumerate(delta_targets):
                current_step += 1
                progress_callback(current_step, total_steps)
                
                self.car.regen_eff = regen_target
                t_min_adj = t_min * (1 - delta_target)
              
                res = self.solver.solve(ef_min, t_min_adj, objective="points")
                
                if res and res.get("success", False):
                    e_opt_grid[i, j] = np.sum(res["e_targets"])
                    soc_opt_grid[i, j] = res["soc_start"]
                else:
                    e_opt_grid[i, j] = np.nan
                    soc_opt_grid[i, j] = np.nan

        e_interpolant = RegularGridInterpolator((regen_targets, delta_targets), e_opt_grid, method='linear', bounds_error=False, fill_value=np.nan)
        soc_interpolant = RegularGridInterpolator((regen_targets, delta_targets), soc_opt_grid, method='linear', bounds_error=False, fill_value=np.nan)
     
        return SolverPayload(
            kind="endurance_map",
            data=None,
            meta={
                "ef_min": ef_min, "t_min": t_min,
                "e_interpolant": e_interpolant, "soc_interpolant": soc_interpolant,  
                "grid_regen": regen_targets, "grid_delta": delta_targets,
                "z_min_e": np.nanmin(e_opt_grid), "z_max_e": np.nanmax(e_opt_grid),
                "car": self.car, "track": None
            }
        )

class DeploymentOptTask(SimulationTask):
    def __init__(self, solver, car):
        self.solver = solver
        self.car = car

    def execute(self, progress_callback) -> SolverPayload:
        ef_min_res = self.solver.solve(0,0,objective="efficiency")
        ef_min = ef_min_res["ef_score"] if ef_min_res["success"] else self.solver.ef_guess(4)

        t_min_res = self.solver.solve(0,0,objective="time")
        t_min = t_min_res["total_time"] if t_min_res["success"] else self.solver.best_T_guess(0)

        t_min_adj = t_min * (1 - self.car.performance_delta)

        res_opt = self.solver.solve(ef_min, t_min_adj, objective="points")
        res_full = self.solver.solve(ef_min, t_min_adj, objective="points", enforce_full_soc=True)

        return SolverPayload(
            kind="endurance_deployment",
            data={"opt_soc": res_opt, "full_soc": res_full},
            meta={
                "ef_min": ef_min, "t_min": t_min,
                "regen_eff": self.car.regen_eff, "delta": self.car.performance_delta,
                "car": self.car, "track": None
            }
        )

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: src\optimization\tasks\track_generation_task.py ---
from .base_task import SimulationTask, SolverPayload
from src.core.trackGenerator import generate_smoothed_track

class TrackGenerationTask(SimulationTask):
    def __init__(self, data, lap_infos, is_closed, r_penalty, w_penalty):
        self.data = data
        self.lap_infos = lap_infos
        self.is_closed = is_closed
        self.r_penalty = r_penalty
        self.w_penalty = w_penalty

    def execute(self, progress_callback) -> SolverPayload:
        # emit start progress (casadi takes over the terminal, so we just use an indefinite loader in ui)
        progress_callback(0, 1)
        
        result_data = generate_smoothed_track(
            self.data, 
            self.lap_infos, 
            self.is_closed, 
            self.r_penalty,
            self.w_penalty
        )
        
        if result_data is None:
            raise RuntimeError("track smoothing failed to converge.")
            
        progress_callback(1, 1)
        
        return SolverPayload(
            kind="track_generation",
            data=result_data,
            meta={}
        )

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: src\ui\compare_results.py ---
import numpy as np
import pyqtgraph as pg
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import (QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QLabel, QTabWidget, QComboBox, QHBoxLayout)
import json
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import QTableWidget, QTableWidgetItem, QHeaderView, QAbstractItemView
from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt
from PyQt6.QtGui import QFont
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from matplotlib.backends.backend_qt5agg import FigureCanvasQTAgg
from matplotlib.figure import Figure
from matplotlib.colors import TwoSlopeNorm
import seaborn as sns


class MplCanvas(FigureCanvasQTAgg):
    def __init__(self, parent=None, width=5, height=4, dpi=100):
        self.fig = Figure(figsize=(width, height), dpi=dpi)
        self.fig.patch.set_facecolor('#ffffff') # pure white background
        self.axes = self.fig.add_subplot(111)
        
        super(MplCanvas, self).__init__(self.fig)
        self.setParent(parent)
        self.fig.tight_layout() # use maximum space
        FigureCanvasQTAgg.updateGeometry(self)


class ComparisonPlotWindow(QWidget):
    # modern color palette (tailwind css colors) for graphs
    PALETTE = ['#3b82f6', '#ef4444', '#10b981', '#f59e0b', '#8b5cf6', '#ec4899']

    def __init__(self, selected_data):
        super().__init__()
        
        # global antialiasing for nicer graphs
        pg.setConfigOptions(antialias=True)
        
        self.selected_data = selected_data
        self.runs = []
        
        self.setWindowTitle("Telemetry Comparison")
        self.resize(1100, 850)
        
        # --- application of modern look (qss) ---
        self.setStyleSheet("""
            QWidget {
                font-family: 'Segoe UI', system-ui, sans-serif;
                font-size: 10pt;
                color: #334155;
                background-color: #f8f9fa;
            }
            QTabWidget::pane {
                border: 1px solid #e2e8f0;
                border-radius: 8px;
                background: #ffffff;
            }
            QTabBar::tab {
                background: #e2e8f0;
                color: #64748b;
                padding: 10px 20px;
                border-top-left-radius: 6px;
                border-top-right-radius: 6px;
                margin-right: 4px;
                font-weight: bold;
            }
            QTabBar::tab:selected {
                background: #ffffff;
                color: #0f172a;
                border-bottom: 2px solid #3b82f6; /* highlighting active tab */
            }
            QTabBar::tab:hover:!selected {
                background: #cbd5e1;
            }
            QComboBox {
                background: #ffffff;
                border: 1px solid #cbd5e1;
                border-radius: 6px;
                padding: 6px 15px;
                min-width: 180px;
                font-weight: bold;
                color: #0f172a;
            }
            QComboBox:hover {
                border: 1px solid #94a3b8;
            }
            QComboBox::drop-down {
                border: none;
            }
        """)

        # 1. layout initialization
        self.main_layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.main_layout.setContentsMargins(15, 15, 15, 15)
        self.main_layout.setSpacing(10)
        
        # 2. data loading
        self.load_data()
        
        # 3. data check
        if not self.runs:
            error_lbl = QLabel(
                "Could not load the selected logs.\n"
                "Please generate new simulations and try again."
            )
            error_lbl.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
            error_lbl.setStyleSheet("font-size: 14px; font-weight: bold; color: #ef4444; background: transparent;")
            self.main_layout.addWidget(error_lbl)
            return

        # 4. tabs
        self.tabs = QTabWidget()
        self.main_layout.addWidget(self.tabs)
        
        self.init_dynamics_tab()
        self.init_energy_tab()
        self.init_map_tab()
        self.init_parameters_tab()
        
    def load_data(self):
        self.runs = []
        for item in self.selected_data:
            try:
                data = np.load(item['path'], allow_pickle=True)
                
                # --- safe loading of json car parameters ---
                car_dict = {}
                if 'car_config' in data:
                    raw_config = data['car_config']
                    
                    # 1. output of raw data and type
                    print(f"--- DEBUG {item['legend']} ---")
                    print(f"1. Raw type: {type(raw_config)}")
                    print(f"2. Raw value: {repr(raw_config)}")
                    
                    try:
                        # extracting clean string
                        if hasattr(raw_config, 'item'):
                            raw_config = raw_config.item()
                             
                        car_dict = json.loads(str(raw_config))
                       
                    except Exception as e:
                        car_dict = {"Data status": f"Parse error: {e}"}
                else:
                    car_dict = {"Data status": "No car parameters saved"}

                run_dict = {
                    's': data['s'],
                    'v': data['v'],
                    't': data['t'],
                    'E': data['E'],
                    'x': data['x'], 
                    'y': data['y'], 
                    'f_acc': data['f_acc'],
                    'f_brake': data['f_brake'],
                    'time': float(data['lap_time']),
                    'name': item['legend'],
                    'car_dict': car_dict  # <-- now contains either json or error message
                }
                self.runs.append(run_dict)
                data.close()
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error loading {item['path']}: {e}")

        if not self.runs: return

        # sort: fastest first
        self.runs.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])
        self.ref_run = self.runs[0]

    def _style_plot(self, plot, title, y_label, units):
        """helper method for clean pyqtgraph plot appearance"""
        plot.setBackground('#ffffff')
        plot.setTitle(title, color='#0f172a', size='12pt', bold=True)
        plot.setLabel('left', y_label, units=units, color='#64748b')
        plot.setLabel('bottom', 'Distance', units='m', color='#64748b')
        
        # coloring axes and text with soft grey
        ax_pen = pg.mkPen(color='#94a3b8', width=1)
        plot.getAxis('left').setPen(ax_pen)
        plot.getAxis('left').setTextPen('#475569')
        plot.getAxis('bottom').setPen(ax_pen)
        plot.getAxis('bottom').setTextPen('#475569')
        
        # soft grid
        plot.showGrid(x=True, y=True, alpha=0.15)
        return plot

    def init_dynamics_tab(self):
        widget = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        layout.setContentsMargins(15, 15, 15, 15)
        layout.setSpacing(15)
        
        # --- 1. speed graph ---
        plot_v = pg.PlotWidget()
        self._style_plot(plot_v, "Speed Telemetry", "Speed", "km/h")
        
        # modern legend placement
        legend = plot_v.addLegend(offset=(20, 20), pen=pg.mkPen('#e2e8f0'), brush=pg.mkBrush('#ffffffEE'))
        legend.setLabelTextColor('#0f172a')
        
        # --- 2. delta time graph ---
        plot_dt = pg.PlotWidget()
        self._style_plot(plot_dt, "Delta Time (Accumulated Gap)", "Time", "s")
        plot_dt.setXLink(plot_v)
        
        # zero line (thin dark line)
        plot_dt.addItem(pg.InfiniteLine(pos=0, angle=0, pen=pg.mkPen('#334155', width=1.5)))

        # rendering
        for i, run in enumerate(self.runs):
            # index 0 is always ref_run -> use blue
            color_hex = self.PALETTE[i % len(self.PALETTE)]
            color_val = pg.mkColor(color_hex)
            width = 2.5 if run is self.ref_run else 2.0
            
            # a) speed
            plot_v.plot(run['s'], run['v'] * 3.6, pen=pg.mkPen(color_val, width=width), name=f"({run['time']:.3f}s)")
            
            # b) delta time (fixed with interpolation)
            # take distance and time axes from reference lap
            s_ref = self.ref_run['s']
            t_ref = self.ref_run['t']
            
            # interpolate comparison lap time to reference lap distance points
            t_run_interp = np.interp(s_ref, run['s'], run['t'])
            
            # safely subtract as both arrays have the same length and correspond to same positions
            delta = t_run_interp - t_ref
            
            dt_curve = plot_dt.plot(s_ref, delta, pen=pg.mkPen(color_val, width=width), name=f"({run['time']:.3f}s)")
            
            # delta fill (with soft transparency)
            if run is not self.ref_run:
                # use s_ref instead of s_axis
                dummy_zero = pg.PlotCurveItem(s_ref, np.zeros_like(s_ref), pen=None)
                plot_dt.addItem(dummy_zero)
                
                fill_color = pg.mkColor(color_hex)
                fill_color.setAlpha(40) # soft 15% transparency (0-255)
                
                fill = pg.FillBetweenItem(dt_curve, dummy_zero, brush=fill_color)
                plot_dt.addItem(fill)

        layout.addWidget(plot_v)
        layout.addWidget(plot_dt)
        widget.setLayout(layout)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Dynamics")

    def init_energy_tab(self):
        widget = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        layout.setContentsMargins(15, 15, 15, 15)
        layout.setSpacing(15)
        
        plot_p = pg.PlotWidget()
        self._style_plot(plot_p, "Powertrain Power", "Power", "kW")
        legend = plot_p.addLegend(offset=(20, 20), pen=pg.mkPen('#e2e8f0'), brush=pg.mkBrush('#ffffffEE'))
        legend.setLabelTextColor('#0f172a')
        
        plot_e = pg.PlotWidget()
        self._style_plot(plot_e, "Energy consumption", "Energy", "kWh")
        plot_e.setXLink(plot_p)

        for i, run in enumerate(self.runs):
            color_hex = self.PALETTE[i % len(self.PALETTE)]
            color_val = pg.mkColor(color_hex)
            width = 2.5 if run is self.ref_run else 2.0
            
            # power calc
            min_len = min(len(run['f_acc']), len(run['v']))
            f_tot = run['f_acc'][:min_len] + run['f_brake'][:min_len]
            v = run['v'][:min_len]
            pwr = (f_tot * v) / 1000.0
            s_axis = run['s'][:min_len]
            
            plot_p.plot(s_axis, pwr, pen=pg.mkPen(color_val, width=width), name=f"({run['time']:.3f}s)")
            
            # energy - fill under curve for better readability
            e_curve = plot_e.plot(run['s'], run['E']-run['E'][0], pen=pg.mkPen(color_val, width=width), name=f"({run['time']:.3f}s)")
            
            if run is self.ref_run:
                dummy_zero = pg.PlotCurveItem(run['s'], np.zeros_like(run['s']), pen=None)
                fill_color = pg.mkColor(color_hex)
                fill_color.setAlpha(20)
                plot_e.addItem(pg.FillBetweenItem(e_curve, dummy_zero, brush=fill_color))

        layout.addWidget(plot_p)
        layout.addWidget(plot_e)
        widget.setLayout(layout)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Energy")

    def init_map_tab(self):
        widget = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        layout.setContentsMargins(20, 20, 20, 20)
        layout.setSpacing(15)
        
        # --- controls (lap selection for comparison) ---
        controls_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        
        lbl_ref = QLabel(f"Reference Lap: <b>{self.ref_run['name']}</b>")
        lbl_ref.setStyleSheet("font-size: 12pt; color: #0f172a; background: transparent;")
        controls_layout.addWidget(lbl_ref)
        
        lbl_vs = QLabel("  vs  ")
        lbl_vs.setStyleSheet("font-size: 11pt; color: #64748b; background: transparent;")
        controls_layout.addWidget(lbl_vs)
        
        self.combo_compare = QComboBox()
        self.compare_candidates = [r for r in self.runs if r is not self.ref_run]
        
        if not self.compare_candidates:
            layout.addWidget(QLabel("Need at least 2 logs to show comparison map."))
            widget.setLayout(layout)
            self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Delta V Map")
            return

        for run in self.compare_candidates:
            diff = run['time'] - self.ref_run['time']
            sign = "+" if diff > 0 else ""
            self.combo_compare.addItem(f"{run['name']} ({sign}{diff:.3f}s)")
            
        self.combo_compare.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.update_heatmap)
        controls_layout.addWidget(self.combo_compare)
        controls_layout.addStretch()
        
        layout.addLayout(controls_layout)

        # --- matplotlib canvas ---
        # wrap in qwidget with white background and border
        canvas_container = QWidget()
        canvas_container.setStyleSheet("background: #ffffff; border: 1px solid #e2e8f0; border-radius: 8px;")
        canvas_layout = QVBoxLayout(canvas_container)
        canvas_layout.setContentsMargins(5, 5, 5, 5)
        
        self.map_canvas = MplCanvas(self, width=5, height=4, dpi=100)
        canvas_layout.addWidget(self.map_canvas)
        
        layout.addWidget(canvas_container)
        
        widget.setLayout(layout)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Delta V Map")
        
        # first render
        self.update_heatmap()

    def update_heatmap(self):
        if not self.compare_candidates: return

        idx = self.combo_compare.currentIndex()
        comp_run = self.compare_candidates[idx]
        ref_run = self.ref_run

        s_ref = ref_run['s']
        v_ref = ref_run['v'] * 3.6 
        v_comp_interp = np.interp(s_ref, comp_run['s'], comp_run['v']) * 3.6
        
        delta_v = v_comp_interp - v_ref

        self.map_canvas.axes.clear()
        ax = self.map_canvas.axes
        
        max_diff = max(abs(np.min(delta_v)), abs(np.max(delta_v)))
        if max_diff == 0: max_diff = 1 
        
        norm = TwoSlopeNorm(vmin=-max_diff, vcenter=0, vmax=max_diff) 
        
        sc = ax.scatter(ref_run['x'], ref_run['y'], c=delta_v, cmap='coolwarm', s=12, norm=norm)
        
        ax.set_title(f"Speed Delta [km/h]", pad=15, fontweight='bold', color='#0f172a')
        ax.set_aspect('equal')
        
        
        ax.axis('off')
        
        if not hasattr(self, 'cax'):
            # first time reduce main graph and create fixed axis on the right
            self.map_canvas.fig.subplots_adjust(right=0.85)
            self.cax = self.map_canvas.fig.add_axes([0.88, 0.15, 0.02, 0.7])
        else:
            # next redraw just clear legend axis, graph remains untouched
            self.cax.clear()
            
        # render legend into our fixed axis (cax instead of ax)
        self.cbar = self.map_canvas.fig.colorbar(sc, cax=self.cax)
        self.cbar.outline.set_visible(False)
        self.cbar.ax.tick_params(colors='#475569')
        
        self.map_canvas.draw()

    def init_parameters_tab(self):
        widget = QWidget()
        # horizontal layout stacks tables side by side
        layout = QHBoxLayout(widget)
        layout.setContentsMargins(15, 15, 15, 15)
        layout.setSpacing(20)

        for run in self.runs:
            run_container = QWidget()
            run_layout = QVBoxLayout(run_container)
            run_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
            
            # --- obtaining car name ---
            car_dict = run.get('car_dict', {})
            # create copy so we can safely pop 'name'
            display_dict = dict(car_dict) if isinstance(car_dict, dict) else {}
            car_name = display_dict.pop('name', 'Unknown Car')
            
            # table header with car name and simulation name
            lbl = QLabel(f"Vehicle: <b>{car_name}</b><br><span style='font-size: 10pt; color: #64748b;'>Log: {run['name']}</span>")
            lbl.setStyleSheet("font-size: 12pt; color: #0f172a; margin-bottom: 5px;")
            run_layout.addWidget(lbl)
            
            # the table itself
            table = QTableWidget()
            table.setColumnCount(2)
            table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(["Parameter", "Value"])
            
            # styling table for modern design
            table.horizontalHeader().setStretchLastSection(True)
            table.horizontalHeader().setSectionResizeMode(0, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents)
            table.verticalHeader().setVisible(False)
            table.setAlternatingRowColors(True)
            table.setEditTriggers(QAbstractItemView.EditTrigger.NoEditTriggers)
            table.setSelectionMode(QAbstractItemView.SelectionMode.NoSelection)
            table.setFocusPolicy(Qt.FocusPolicy.NoFocus)
            
            table.setStyleSheet("""
                QTableWidget {
                    background-color: #ffffff;
                    alternate-background-color: #f8fafc;
                    border: 1px solid #e2e8f0;
                    border-radius: 6px;
                    font-size: 10pt;
                    color: #334155;
                }
                QHeaderView::section {
                    background-color: #f1f5f9;
                    padding: 8px;
                    border: none;
                    border-bottom: 1px solid #cbd5e1;
                    font-weight: bold;
                    color: #475569;
                }
                QTableWidget::item {
                    padding: 5px 10px;
                    border-bottom: 1px solid #f1f5f9;
                }
            """)

            table.setRowCount(len(display_dict))
            
            # data output (without 'name' key)
            for row, (key, value) in enumerate(display_dict.items()):
                item_key = QTableWidgetItem(str(key).replace('_', ' ').capitalize())
                item_val = QTableWidgetItem(str(value))
                
                # highlighting parameter text
                item_key.setForeground(pg.mkColor('#64748b'))
                item_val.setForeground(pg.mkColor('#0f172a'))
                
                table.setItem(row, 0, item_key)
                table.setItem(row, 1, item_val)
                
            run_layout.addWidget(table)
            layout.addWidget(run_container)
            
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Car Configs")

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: src\ui\endurance_results.py ---
import datetime
import os
import numpy as np
import pyqtgraph as pg
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import (QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel, 
                             QTabWidget, QGroupBox, QGridLayout, QFileDialog, 
                             QComboBox, QApplication, QPushButton)
from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt
from src.utils.saver import save_results_to_npz

class EnduranceResultsWindow(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, results_list, car, track, ds):
        super().__init__()
        self.results_list = results_list
        self.car = car
        self.track = track
        self.ds = ds
        
        # default to first lap
        self.current_idx = 0
        self.current_res = self.results_list[0]

        self.resize(1100, 800)
        self.center_window()
        self.setWindowTitle("Endurance simulation results")
        
        # layout setup
        self.layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.init_header()
        self.init_summary_panel()
        
        # tabs setup
        self.tabs = QTabWidget()
        self.layout.addWidget(self.tabs)
        
        self.init_dynamics_tab()
        self.init_powertrain_tab()
        self.init_forces_tab()
        self.init_map_tab()
        self.init_evolution_tab()
        
        # initial render
        self.update_view()

    def center_window(self):
        qr = self.frameGeometry()
        cp = QApplication.primaryScreen().availableGeometry().center()
        qr.moveCenter(cp)
        self.move(qr.topLeft())

    def init_header(self):
        """top bar with lap selection and save button."""
        header_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        header_layout.addWidget(QLabel("<b>Select Lap:</b>"))
        
        # lap selection combo
        self.combo_laps = QComboBox()
        self.combo_laps.setFixedWidth(150)
        items = [f"Lap {i+1}" for i in range(len(self.results_list))]
        self.combo_laps.addItems(items)
        self.combo_laps.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.on_lap_changed)
        header_layout.addWidget(self.combo_laps)
        
        header_layout.addStretch()
        
        # save button
        self.btn_save = QPushButton("Save All (.npz)")
        self.btn_save.setFixedWidth(150)
        self.btn_save.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self.btn_save.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                background-color: #17a2b8; color: white; font-weight: bold; 
                padding: 8px; border-radius: 4px;
            }
            QPushButton:hover { background-color: #138496; }
        """)
        self.btn_save.clicked.connect(self.on_save_clicked)
        header_layout.addWidget(self.btn_save)
        
        self.layout.addLayout(header_layout)

    def on_lap_changed(self, index):
        self.current_idx = index
        self.current_res = self.results_list[index]
        self.update_view()

    def init_summary_panel(self):
        group = QGroupBox("Lap Summary")
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        
        stats_widget = QWidget()
        grid = QGridLayout(stats_widget)
        grid.setContentsMargins(10, 10, 10, 10)
        grid.setHorizontalSpacing(30)
        grid.setVerticalSpacing(10)
        
        # labels
        self.lbl_lap_time = QLabel("0.000")
        self.lbl_energy = QLabel("0.000")
        self.lbl_max_v = QLabel("0.0")
        self.lbl_avg_v = QLabel("0.0")
        self.lbl_soc = QLabel("0.0")

        style = "color: #007bff; font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold; font-family: Consolas;"
        for lbl in [self.lbl_lap_time, self.lbl_energy, self.lbl_max_v, self.lbl_avg_v, self.lbl_soc]:
            lbl.setStyleSheet(style)
            lbl.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignLeft)

        def add_stat_to_grid(row, col_offset, label_text, value_widget, unit_text):
            lbl_name = QLabel(f"{label_text}:")
            lbl_name.setStyleSheet("font-weight: bold; font-size: 14px;")
            lbl_name.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignRight | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)
            
            lbl_unit = QLabel(unit_text)
            lbl_unit.setStyleSheet("color: #888;")
            
            grid.addWidget(lbl_name, row, 0 + col_offset)
            grid.addWidget(value_widget, row, 1 + col_offset)
            grid.addWidget(lbl_unit, row, 2 + col_offset)

        # left column
        add_stat_to_grid(0, 0, "Lap Time", self.lbl_lap_time, "s")
        add_stat_to_grid(1, 0, "Max Speed", self.lbl_max_v, "km/h")
        add_stat_to_grid(2, 0, "Cumulative Energy", self.lbl_soc, "kWh")

        # right column
        add_stat_to_grid(0, 4, "Energy Used (Lap)", self.lbl_energy, "kWh")
        add_stat_to_grid(1, 4, "Avg Speed", self.lbl_avg_v, "km/h")
        
        grid.setColumnMinimumWidth(3, 40) 

        layout.addWidget(stats_widget)
        group.setLayout(layout)
        self.layout.addWidget(group)

    def init_dynamics_tab(self):
        widget = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        
        # speed plot
        self.plot_v = pg.PlotWidget(title="Speed profile")
        self.plot_v.setLabel('left', 'Speed', units='km/h')
        self.plot_v.showGrid(x=True, y=True)
        self.curve_v = self.plot_v.plot([], [], pen=pg.mkPen('b', width=2))
        
        # time plot
        self.plot_t = pg.PlotWidget(title="Time Accumulation (Relative to Lap Start)")
        self.plot_t.setLabel('left', 'Time', units='s')
        self.plot_t.setLabel('bottom', 'Distance', units='m')
        self.plot_t.showGrid(x=True, y=True)
        self.plot_t.setXLink(self.plot_v)
        self.curve_t = self.plot_t.plot([], [], pen=pg.mkPen('r', width=2))
        
        layout.addWidget(self.plot_v)
        layout.addWidget(self.plot_t)
        widget.setLayout(layout)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Dynamics")

    def init_powertrain_tab(self):
        widget = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        
        # power plot
        self.plot_p = pg.PlotWidget(title="Power Usage")
        self.plot_p.setLabel('left', 'Power', units='kW')
        self.plot_p.showGrid(x=True, y=False)
        self.curve_acc = self.plot_p.plot([], [], pen='g', name="Acceleration", width=3)
        self.curve_reg = self.plot_p.plot([], [], pen='r', name="Regen", width=3)
        
        # energy plot
        self.plot_e = pg.PlotWidget(title="Energy Consumption (Cumulative)")
        self.plot_e.setLabel('left', 'Energy', units='kWh')
        self.plot_e.setLabel('bottom', 'Distance', units='m')
        self.plot_e.showGrid(x=True, y=True)
        self.plot_e.setXLink(self.plot_p)
        self.curve_e = self.plot_e.plot([], [], pen=pg.mkPen('orange', width=2))
        
        layout.addWidget(self.plot_p)
        layout.addWidget(self.plot_e)
        widget.setLayout(layout)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Powertrain")

    def init_forces_tab(self):
        widget = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        
        self.plot_f = pg.PlotWidget(title="Longitudinal Forces")
        self.plot_f.setLabel('left', 'Force', units='N')
        self.plot_f.setLabel('bottom', 'Distance', units='m')
        self.plot_f.showGrid(x=True, y=True)
        self.plot_f.addLegend()
        
        self.curve_f_acc = self.plot_f.plot([], [], pen='g', name="Traction")
        self.curve_f_brake = self.plot_f.plot([], [], pen='r', name="Braking")
        
        layout.addWidget(self.plot_f)
        widget.setLayout(layout)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Forces")

    def init_map_tab(self):
        widget = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout()

        self.map_plot = pg.PlotWidget(title="Power Map")
        self.map_plot.setAspectLocked(True)
        self.map_plot.setLabel('bottom', 'X Position', units='m')
        self.map_plot.setLabel('left', 'Y Position', units='m')

        # scatter item
        self.scatter = pg.ScatterPlotItem(size=6, pen=None, hoverable=True, hoverSize=10)
        self.map_plot.addItem(self.scatter)
        
        # markers
        self.start_marker = pg.ScatterPlotItem(size=15, symbol='o', brush='w')
        self.map_plot.addItem(self.start_marker)
        
        self.cursor_label = pg.TextItem(anchor=(0, 1), color="w")
        self.map_plot.addItem(self.cursor_label)
        
        self.v_line = pg.InfiniteLine(angle=90, movable=False, pen=pg.mkPen('w', style=Qt.PenStyle.DashLine))
        self.h_line = pg.InfiniteLine(angle=0, movable=False, pen=pg.mkPen('w', style=Qt.PenStyle.DashLine))
        self.map_plot.addItem(self.v_line)
        self.map_plot.addItem(self.h_line)
        
        self.map_plot.scene().sigMouseMoved.connect(self.on_map_mouse_move)

        layout.addWidget(self.map_plot)
        widget.setLayout(layout)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Power Map")

    def init_evolution_tab(self):
        widget = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        
        # data prep
        laps = np.arange(2, len(self.results_list) + 1)
        subset = self.results_list[1:]
        mus_x = [r.mu_x for r in subset]
        mus_y = [r.mu_y for r in subset]
        regens = [r.regen_eff for r in subset]
        lap_times = [r.t[-1] - r.t[0] for r in subset]
        
        # 1. grip evolution
        self.plot_grip = pg.PlotWidget(title="Grip evolution (Mu)")
        self.plot_grip.setLabel('left', 'Friction coeff [-]')
        self.plot_grip.setLabel('bottom', 'Lap Number')
        self.plot_grip.addLegend()

        self.plot_grip.plot(laps, mus_x, pen='r', symbol='o', name="Mu X (Long)") 
        self.plot_grip.plot(laps, mus_y, pen='b', symbol='t', name="Mu Y (Lat)")
        
        self.grip_marker = pg.InfiniteLine(pos=1, angle=90, pen=pg.mkPen('b', width=2, style=Qt.PenStyle.DashLine))
        self.plot_grip.addItem(self.grip_marker)

        # 2. regen evolution
        self.plot_regen = pg.PlotWidget(title="Regen Efficiency")
        self.plot_regen.setLabel('left', 'Efficiency [-]')
        self.plot_regen.setLabel('bottom', 'Lap Number')
        
        self.plot_regen.plot(laps, regens, pen='g', symbol='s', name="Regen Eff")
        
        self.regen_marker = pg.InfiniteLine(pos=1, angle=90, pen=pg.mkPen('b', width=2, style=Qt.PenStyle.DashLine))
        self.plot_regen.addItem(self.regen_marker)

        # 3. lap time evolution
        self.plot_times = pg.PlotWidget(title="Lap Time Evolution")
        self.plot_times.setLabel('left', 'Time [s]')
        self.plot_times.setLabel('bottom', 'Lap Number')
        self.plot_times.showGrid(x=False, y=False)
        
        self.plot_times.plot(laps, lap_times, pen='c', symbol='d', name="Lap Time")
        
        self.time_marker = pg.InfiniteLine(pos=1, angle=90, pen=pg.mkPen('b', width=2, style=Qt.PenStyle.DashLine))
        self.plot_times.addItem(self.time_marker)

        # link axes
        self.plot_grip.setXLink(self.plot_times)
        self.plot_regen.setXLink(self.plot_times)

        layout.addWidget(self.plot_grip)
        layout.addWidget(self.plot_regen)
        layout.addWidget(self.plot_times)
        
        widget.setLayout(layout)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Evolution")

    def update_view(self):
        res = self.current_res
        
        # update summary labels
        lap_duration = res.t[-1] - res.t[0]
        energy_used = res.E[-1] - res.E[0]
        max_v = np.max(res.v) * 3.6
        avg_v = np.mean(res.v) * 3.6
        total_energy = res.E[-1]

        self.lbl_lap_time.setText(f"{lap_duration:.3f}")
        self.lbl_energy.setText(f"{energy_used:.3f}")
        self.lbl_max_v.setText(f"{max_v:.1f}")
        self.lbl_avg_v.setText(f"{avg_v:.1f}")
        self.lbl_soc.setText(f"{total_energy:.3f}")
        self.setWindowTitle(f"Endurance results - Lap {self.current_idx + 1}")

        # data prep for plots (relative to lap start)
        s_rel = res.s - res.s[0]
        t_rel = res.t - res.t[0]
        min_len = min(len(res.f_acc), len(res.v))
        s_plot = s_rel[:min_len]
        v_plot = res.v[:min_len]
        
        # update dynamic plots
        self.curve_v.setData(s_rel, res.v * 3.6)
        self.curve_t.setData(s_rel, t_rel)
        
        power_kw = ((res.f_acc[:min_len] + res.f_brake[:min_len]) * v_plot) / 1000.0
        acc_mask = power_kw > 0
        reg_mask = power_kw <= 0
        
        p_acc = np.copy(power_kw)
        p_acc[~acc_mask] = 0
        p_reg = np.copy(power_kw)
        p_reg[~reg_mask] = 0
        
        self.curve_acc.setData(s_plot, p_acc)
        self.curve_reg.setData(s_plot, p_reg)
        self.curve_e.setData(s_rel, res.E) 
        self.curve_f_acc.setData(s_plot, res.f_acc[:min_len])
        self.curve_f_brake.setData(s_plot, res.f_brake[:min_len])

        # update evolution markers
        current_lap_num = self.current_idx + 1
        if hasattr(self, 'grip_marker'):
            self.grip_marker.setPos(current_lap_num)
        if hasattr(self, 'regen_marker'):
            self.regen_marker.setPos(current_lap_num)
        if hasattr(self, 'time_marker'):
            self.time_marker.setPos(current_lap_num)

        # update map
        self.update_map_data(s_rel, v_plot, power_kw)

    def update_map_data(self, s_rel, v_plot, power_kw):
        x_plot = np.interp(s_rel, self.track.s, self.track.x)
        y_plot = np.interp(s_rel, self.track.s, self.track.y)
        
        n = len(power_kw)
        x_plot = x_plot[:n]
        y_plot = y_plot[:n]
        s_plot = s_rel[:n]

        self.map_data = {
            'x': x_plot, 'y': y_plot, 'p': power_kw, 'v': v_plot * 3.6, 's': s_plot
        }

        # coloring logic
        p_min, p_max = np.min(power_kw), np.max(power_kw)
        span = p_max - p_min + 1e-9
        zero_pos = np.clip((0 - p_min) / span, 0.0, 1.0)

        pos = np.array([0.0, zero_pos, 1.0])
        color_def = np.array([
            [255, 0, 0, 255], [0, 150, 255, 255], [0, 255, 0, 255]
        ], dtype=np.ubyte)
        cmap = pg.ColorMap(pos, color_def)
        
        norm_power = (power_kw - p_min) / span
        brushes = cmap.map(norm_power, mode='qcolor')

        self.scatter.setData(x=x_plot, y=y_plot, brush=brushes)
        self.start_marker.setData(x=[x_plot[0]], y=[y_plot[0]])

    def on_map_mouse_move(self, pos):
        if self.map_plot.sceneBoundingRect().contains(pos):
            mouse_point = self.map_plot.plotItem.vb.mapSceneToView(pos)
            mx, my = mouse_point.x(), mouse_point.y()
            
            # find closest point
            dists = np.sqrt((self.map_data['x'] - mx)**2 + (self.map_data['y'] - my)**2)
            idx = np.argmin(dists)
            
            if dists[idx] < 20.0:
                x, y = self.map_data['x'][idx], self.map_data['y'][idx]
                p, v, s = self.map_data['p'][idx], self.map_data['v'][idx], self.map_data['s'][idx]
                
                self.v_line.setPos(x)
                self.h_line.setPos(y)
                
                color_hex = "#00ff00" if p > 0 else "#ff5555" if p < 0 else "#ffffff"
                html = (f"<div style='background-color: rgba(0,0,0,150); padding: 5px; font-size: 14px;'>"
                        f"<b>Dist: {s:.1f} m</b><br>Speed: {v:.1f} km/h<br>"
                        f"Power: <b style='color: {color_hex};'>{p:.1f} kW</b></div>")
                
                self.cursor_label.setHtml(html)
                self.cursor_label.setPos(x, y)
                self.cursor_label.show()
                self.v_line.show()
                self.h_line.show()
            else:
                self.cursor_label.hide()
                self.v_line.hide()
                self.h_line.hide()

    def on_save_clicked(self):
        now_str = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d_%H-%M")
        default_name = f"{self.track.name}_Endurance_{now_str}.npz"
        if not os.path.exists("results"): os.makedirs("results")
        
        filepath, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, "Save Endurance Results", 
                                                  os.path.join("results", default_name), 
                                                  "NumPy Zip (*.npz)")
        
        if filepath:
            try:
                # currently saving only the selected lap
                save_results_to_npz(filepath, self.current_res, self.track.name, self.ds,self.car)
                self.btn_save.setText("Saved Current Lap!")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error: {e}")

import numpy as np
import json
import casadi as ca
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import (QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, 
                             QSlider, QLabel, QMessageBox, QPushButton, 
                             QDoubleSpinBox, QTabWidget, QGroupBox, QApplication, QSizePolicy,
                             QDialog, QTableWidget, QTableWidgetItem, QHeaderView)
from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt, pyqtSignal
from PyQt6.QtGui import QGuiApplication
from matplotlib.backends.backend_qtagg import FigureCanvasQTAgg, NavigationToolbar2QT
from matplotlib.figure import Figure
from src.ui.loader import LoadingOverlay
from scipy.ndimage import median_filter

class MapTab(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, map_data, fixed_axis='soc', parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.map_data = map_data
        self.fixed_axis = fixed_axis
        
        self.laptime_map = map_data['interpolant']
        self.z_min = map_data['z_min']
        self.z_max = map_data['z_max']
        
        main_layout = QVBoxLayout(self) 
        
        # matplotlib canvas 
        self.fig = Figure(figsize=(8, 6), dpi=100)
        self.fig.patch.set_facecolor('#ffffff') # white background for modern look
        self.canvas = FigureCanvasQTAgg(self.fig)
                
        content_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.canvas.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding)
        content_layout.addWidget(self.canvas, stretch=1)
        
        # --- slider ---
        slider_layout = QVBoxLayout() 
        self.lbl_slider = QLabel("")
        self.lbl_slider.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter) 
        self.lbl_slider.setStyleSheet("font-weight: bold; color: #3b82f6; font-size: 14px;")
        
        self.slider = QSlider(Qt.Orientation.Vertical)
        self.slider.setTickPosition(QSlider.TickPosition.TicksRight) 
        
        if self.fixed_axis == 'soc':
            self.slider.setMinimum(10)
            self.slider.setMaximum(100)
            self.slider.setTickInterval(10)
            self.slider.setValue(50)
            self.X_mesh, self.Y_mesh = np.meshgrid(map_data['e_fine'], map_data['r_fine'])
        else:
            self.slider.setMinimum(int(map_data['r_min'] * 100))
            self.slider.setMaximum(100)
            self.slider.setTickInterval(10)
            self.slider.setValue(int(map_data['r_max'] * 50))
            self.X_mesh, self.Y_mesh = np.meshgrid(map_data['e_fine'], map_data['soc_fine'])

        slider_layout.addWidget(self.lbl_slider)
        slider_layout.addWidget(self.slider)
        
        content_layout.addLayout(slider_layout)
        main_layout.addLayout(content_layout)
        
        # camera default view
        self.ax3d = None
        self.view_elev = 20
        self.view_azim = 30
        
        self.slider.valueChanged.connect(self.on_slider_changed)
        self.on_slider_changed(self.slider.value()) 

    def on_slider_changed(self, val):
        if self.fixed_axis == 'soc':
            self.lbl_slider.setText(f"SoC:\n{val} %")
        else:
            self.lbl_slider.setText(f"Regen:\n{val/100.0:.2f}")
        self.update_plot()

    def update_plot(self):
        if self.fixed_axis == 'soc':
            soc_val = self.slider.value() / 100.0
            pts_casadi = np.vstack((
                np.full_like(self.X_mesh.ravel(), soc_val),
                self.X_mesh.ravel(),
                self.Y_mesh.ravel()
            ))
            x_label, y_label = 'Energy target [kWh]', 'Regen [%]'
            title = f'Laptime map (SoC = {soc_val*100:.0f} %)'
        else:
            regen_val = self.slider.value() / 100.0
            pts_casadi = np.vstack((
                self.Y_mesh.ravel(),
                self.X_mesh.ravel(),
                np.full_like(self.X_mesh.ravel(), regen_val)
            ))
            x_label, y_label = 'Energy target [kWh]', 'SoC [%]'
            title = f'Laptime map (Regen = {regen_val:.2f})'

        Z_laptime = np.array(self.laptime_map(pts_casadi)).reshape(self.X_mesh.shape)

        if self.ax3d is not None:
            self.view_elev = self.ax3d.elev
            self.view_azim = self.ax3d.azim

        self.fig.clear()
        self.ax3d = self.fig.add_subplot(1, 1, 1, projection='3d')
        
        surf = self.ax3d.plot_surface(
            self.X_mesh, self.Y_mesh*100, Z_laptime, 
            cmap='viridis_r', edgecolor='none', alpha=0.9,
            vmin=self.z_min, vmax=self.z_max
        )
        
        self.ax3d.set_zlim(self.z_min, self.z_max)
        
        # font style unification - removing bold and adjusting colors
        self.ax3d.set_xlabel('Energy target [kWh]', color='#475569', fontweight='normal')
        self.ax3d.set_ylabel(y_label, color='#475569', fontweight='normal')
        self.ax3d.set_zlabel('Lap time [s]', color='#475569', fontweight='normal')
        
        # title without bold, same color as results
        self.ax3d.set_title(title, color='#475569', fontweight='normal', pad=15)
        
        # styling axis ticks
        self.ax3d.xaxis.set_tick_params(colors='#64748b')
        self.ax3d.yaxis.set_tick_params(colors='#64748b')
        self.ax3d.zaxis.set_tick_params(colors='#64748b')
        
        self.ax3d.view_init(elev=self.view_elev, azim=self.view_azim)
        
        # margin optimization so the graph is not unnecessarily small
      # self.fig.subplots_adjust(left=0.0, right=1.0, bottom=0.0, top=0.95)
        self.canvas.draw_idle()

        main_layout.addWidget(self.tabs)

    def load_data(self, filepath):
        try:
            data = np.load(filepath)
            soc_targets = data['soc_targets']
            e_targets = data['e_targets']
            regen_targets = data['regen_targets']
            flat_lap_times = data['flat_lap_times']
            
            if 'car_config' in data:
                car_json = data['car_config'][0]
                self.car_config = json.loads(car_json)
            else:
                self.car_config = None
            
            shape_3d = (len(soc_targets), len(e_targets), len(regen_targets))
            lap_times_3d = flat_lap_times.reshape(shape_3d)
            
            # outlier filter
            smoothed_3d = median_filter(lap_times_3d, size=3)
            diff = np.abs(lap_times_3d - smoothed_3d)
            outliers_mask = diff > 5.0
            num_outliers = np.sum(outliers_mask)
            
            if num_outliers > 0:
                print(f"[Data Load] Repaired {num_outliers} outliers via median filter.")
                lap_times_3d[outliers_mask] = smoothed_3d[outliers_mask]
            
            flat_for_casadi = lap_times_3d.ravel(order='F')
            grid_axes = [soc_targets.tolist(), e_targets.tolist(), regen_targets.tolist()]
            interpolant = ca.interpolant('laptime_map', 'bspline', grid_axes, flat_for_casadi.tolist())

            self.min_e_target = min(e_targets)
            self.max_e_target = max(e_targets)
            
            return {
                'interpolant': interpolant,
                'e_fine': np.linspace(min(e_targets), max(e_targets), 50),
                'r_fine': np.linspace(min(regen_targets), 1.0, 50),
                'soc_fine': np.linspace(min(soc_targets), 1.0, 50),
                'r_min': min(regen_targets),
                'r_max': max(regen_targets),
                'z_min': np.nanmin(lap_times_3d),
                'z_max': np.nanmax(lap_times_3d) 
            }
        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Load Error", f"Could not load map from {filepath}:\n{e}")
            return None

    def resizeEvent(self, event):
        self.loading_overlay.resize(self.size())
        super().resizeEvent(event)

    def add_result_tab(self, payload):
        if payload.kind == "endurance_map": 
            has_opt_energy_tab = False
            for i in range(self.tabs.count()):
                if self.tabs.tabText(i) == "Optimal energy map":
                    has_opt_energy_tab = True
                    self.tabs.setCurrentIndex(i) 
                    
            if not has_opt_energy_tab:
                opt_energy_tab = OptimalEnergyMapTab(payload, self)
                self.tabs.addTab(opt_energy_tab, "Optimal energy map")
                self.tabs.setCurrentWidget(opt_energy_tab)

        elif payload.kind == "endurance_deployment":
            result_count = sum(1 for i in range(self.tabs.count()) if self.tabs.tabText(i).startswith("Deployment #"))
            result_tab = StrategyResultTab(payload, self)
            tab_name = f"Deployment #{result_count + 1}"
            self.tabs.addTab(result_tab, tab_name)
            self.tabs.setCurrentWidget(result_tab)

    def run_optimization(self):
        for i in range(self.tabs.count()):
            if self.tabs.tabText(i) == "Optimal energy map":
                self.tabs.setCurrentIndex(i)
                return
            
        regen_val = self.spin_regen.value() / 100
        delta_val = self.spin_delta.value() / 100
        self.loading_overlay.show_loading("Calculating optimal strategy...", indefinite=True, progress_text_fmt="Solving grid points ({} / {})")
        self.optimization_requested.emit(regen_val, delta_val, self.min_e_target, self.max_e_target, self.map_data)

    def run_deployment(self):
        regen_val = self.spin_regen.value() / 100
        delta_val = self.spin_delta.value() / 100
        self.loading_overlay.show_loading("Calculating optimum energy deployment...", indefinite=True)
        self.deployment_requested.emit(regen_val, delta_val, self.min_e_target, self.max_e_target, self.map_data)

    def stop_loading(self, *args, **kwargs):
        self.loading_overlay.hide_loading()

    def show_car_config(self):
        if not hasattr(self, 'car_config') or not self.car_config:
            return
            
        dialog = QDialog(self)
        dialog.setWindowTitle(f"Car Configuration: {self.car_config.get('name', 'Unknown')}")
        dialog.resize(500, 600)
        
        layout = QVBoxLayout(dialog)
        
        table = QTableWidget()
        table.setColumnCount(2)
        table.setRowCount(len(self.car_config))
        table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(["Parameter", "Value"])
        
        table.horizontalHeader().setSectionResizeMode(0, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.Stretch)
        table.horizontalHeader().setSectionResizeMode(1, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.Stretch)
        table.setAlternatingRowColors(True)
        table.setStyleSheet("""
            QTableWidget {
                background-color: #ffffff;
                alternate-background-color: #f8f9fa;
                selection-background-color: #e2e8f0;
                selection-color: #0f172a;
                border: 1px solid #cbd5e1;
                border-radius: 4px;
            }
            QHeaderView::section {
                background-color: #f1f5f9;
                color: #475569;
                padding: 5px;
                border: 1px solid #cbd5e1;
                font-weight: bold;
            }
        """)

        for row, (key, value) in enumerate(self.car_config.items()):
            item_key = QTableWidgetItem(str(key))
            item_key.setFlags(item_key.flags() & ~Qt.ItemFlag.ItemIsEditable)
            
            if isinstance(value, float):
                val_str = f"{value:.4g}"
            else:
                val_str = str(value)
                
            item_value = QTableWidgetItem(val_str)
            item_value.setFlags(item_value.flags() & ~Qt.ItemFlag.ItemIsEditable)
            
            table.setItem(row, 0, item_key)
            table.setItem(row, 1, item_value)
            
        layout.addWidget(table)
        
        close_btn = QPushButton("Close")
        close_btn.clicked.connect(dialog.accept)
        close_btn.setStyleSheet("background-color: #64748b; color: white; padding: 8px; border-radius: 4px; font-weight: bold;")
        layout.addWidget(close_btn)
        
        dialog.exec()


class StrategyResultTab(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, payload, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        
        self.res_opt = payload.data.get("opt_soc", {})
        self.res_full = payload.data.get("full_soc", {})
        self.meta = payload.meta
        
        main_layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        main_layout.setContentsMargins(15, 15, 15, 15)
        main_layout.setSpacing(10) # gap between summary and graph
        
        if not self.res_opt.get("success", False):
            error_lbl = QLabel("Optimization failed for these specific inputs. Try different values.")
            error_lbl.setStyleSheet("color: #ef4444; font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold;")
            main_layout.addWidget(error_lbl)
            return

        # --- 1. summary ---
        summary_container = QWidget()
        summary_container.setStyleSheet("""
            background-color: #f1f5f9; 
            border: 1px solid #cbd5e1; 
            border-radius: 8px;
        """)
        summary_layout = QHBoxLayout(summary_container)
        summary_layout.setContentsMargins(20, 20, 20, 20)
        
        # ... calculation of total_time, points etc. remains the same ...
        total_time = self.res_opt['total_time']
        points = self.res_opt['points']
        total_energy = sum(self.res_opt['e_targets'])
        soc_start = self.res_opt['soc_start']
        has_full_soc = self.res_full.get("success", False)
        
        if has_full_soc:
            time_saved = self.res_full['total_time'] - total_time
            points_diff = points - self.res_full['points']
            energy_diff = total_energy - sum(self.res_full['e_targets'])
            c_time = "#10b981" if time_saved >= 0 else "#ef4444"
            c_pts = "#10b981" if points_diff >= 0 else "#ef4444"
            c_eng = "#10b981" if energy_diff <= 0 else "#ef4444"
            
            info_text = (
                f"<span style='color:#3b82f6; font-size: 18px;'><b>Opt. SoC Start:</b> {soc_start * 100:.1f} %</span>"
                f"&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;"
                f"<span style='color:#64748b;'><b>Points:</b></span> <span style='color:#0f172a; font-size: 16px;'>{points:.2f}</span> "
                f"(<span style='color:{c_pts}; font-weight:bold;'>{'+' if points_diff > 0 else ''}{points_diff:.2f}</span>)"
                f"&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;|&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;"
                f"<span style='color:#64748b;'><b>Energy:</b></span> <span style='color:#0f172a; font-size: 16px;'>{total_energy:.3f} kWh</span> "
                f"(<span style='color:{c_eng}; font-weight:bold;'>{'+' if energy_diff > 0 else ''}{energy_diff:.3f}</span>)"
                f"&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;|&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;"
                f"<span style='color:#64748b;'><b>Total Time:</b></span> <span style='color:#0f172a; font-size: 16px;'>{total_time:.2f} s</span> "
                f"(<span style='color:{c_time}; font-weight:bold;'>{'-' if time_saved > 0 else '+'}{abs(time_saved):.2f} s</span>)"
            )
        else:
            info_text = (
                f"<span style='color:#3b82f6; font-size: 18px;'><b>Opt. SoC Start:</b> {soc_start * 100:.1f} %</span> &nbsp;&nbsp;|&nbsp;&nbsp; "
                f"<b>Points:</b> {points:.2f} &nbsp;&nbsp;|&nbsp;&nbsp; "
                f"<b>Energy used:</b> {total_energy:.3f} kWh &nbsp;&nbsp;|&nbsp;&nbsp; "
                f"<b>Time:</b> {total_time:.2f} s"
            )
        
        lbl_summary = QLabel(info_text)
        lbl_summary.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        lbl_summary.setStyleSheet("background: transparent;")
        summary_layout.addWidget(lbl_summary)
        
        main_layout.addWidget(summary_container)
        
        # --- 2. matplotlib graphs ---
        self.fig = Figure(figsize=(10, 8), dpi=100)
        self.fig.patch.set_facecolor('#ffffff') 
        self.canvas = FigureCanvasQTAgg(self.fig)
        # toolbar removed
        
        graph_container = QWidget()
        graph_container.setObjectName("CanvasContainer")
        graph_layout = QVBoxLayout(graph_container)
        graph_layout.setContentsMargins(5, 5, 5, 5)
        graph_layout.addWidget(self.canvas)
        
        main_layout.addWidget(graph_container)
        self.plot_results()

    def plot_results(self):
        self.fig.clear()
        laps = np.arange(1, len(self.res_opt['lap_times']) + 1)
        opt_lap_times = np.array(self.res_opt['lap_times'])
        opt_e_targets = np.array(self.res_opt['e_targets'])
        
        has_full_soc = self.res_full.get("success", False)
        
        # --- lap times ---
        ax1 = self.fig.add_subplot(2, 1, 1)
        ax1.plot(laps, opt_lap_times, marker='o', color='#3b82f6', linewidth=2.5, markersize=7, label='Optimal SoC Start')
        
        if has_full_soc:
            full_lap_times = np.array(self.res_full['lap_times'])
            ax1.plot(laps, full_lap_times, marker='x', color='#94a3b8', linewidth=2, linestyle='--', label='100% SoC Start')
        
        ax1.set_ylabel('Lap time [s]', color='#475569')
        ax1.tick_params(axis='y', labelcolor='#475569')
        ax1.tick_params(axis='x', labelcolor='#475569')
        ax1.set_xticks(laps)
        ax1.grid(True, linestyle='--', alpha=0.3)
        # bold removed, color softened
        ax1.set_title('Lap Times Evolution', color='#475569', fontweight='normal', pad=15)
        ax1.legend(frameon=True, edgecolor='#e2e8f0', loc='best')

        # --- energy ---
        ax2 = self.fig.add_subplot(2, 1, 2, sharex=ax1)
        ax2.plot(laps, opt_e_targets, marker='s', color='#10b981', linewidth=2.5, markersize=6, label='Optimal SoC Start')
        
        if has_full_soc:
            full_e_targets = np.array(self.res_full['e_targets'])
            ax2.plot(laps, full_e_targets, marker='^', color='#cbd5e1', linewidth=2, linestyle='--', label='100% SoC Start')
        
        ax2.set_xlabel('Lap number', color='#475569', fontweight='normal')
        ax2.set_ylabel('Energy target [kWh]', color='#475569')
        ax2.tick_params(axis='y', labelcolor='#475569')
        ax2.tick_params(axis='x', labelcolor='#475569')
        ax2.grid(True, linestyle='--', alpha=0.3)
        # bold removed, color softened
        ax2.set_title('Energy Deployment per Lap', color='#475569', fontweight='normal', pad=15)
        ax2.legend(frameon=True, edgecolor='#e2e8f0', loc='best')

        # key to success: tight_layout with h_pad defines vertical gaps between graphs
        self.fig.tight_layout(pad=3.0, h_pad=4.0) 
        self.canvas.draw()


class OptimalEnergyMapTab(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, solver_res, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.meta = solver_res.meta
        
        self.e_interpolant = self.meta['e_interpolant']
        self.soc_interpolant = self.meta['soc_interpolant']
        self.grid_regen = self.meta['grid_regen']
        self.grid_delta = self.meta['grid_delta']
        self.z_min = self.meta['z_min_e']
        self.z_max = self.meta['z_max_e']
        
        main_layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        main_layout.setContentsMargins(15, 15, 15, 15)
        
        # --- matplotlib canvas ---
        self.fig = Figure(figsize=(8, 6), dpi=100)
        self.fig.patch.set_facecolor('#ffffff')
        self.canvas = FigureCanvasQTAgg(self.fig)
        self.canvas.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding)
       #elf.toolbar = NavigationToolbar2QT(self.canvas, self)
        
        # --- controls (side panel left) ---
        control_panel = QWidget()
        control_panel.setFixedWidth(260) # fixed width, rest given to graph
        control_layout = QVBoxLayout(control_panel)
        control_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 10, 15, 10)
        
        # output (modern card)
        self.lbl_opt_energy = QLabel("Optimal energy:\n-- kWh\n\nOptimal SoC start:\n-- %")
        self.lbl_opt_energy.setStyleSheet("""
            font-size: 15px; 
            color: #0f172a; 
            background-color: #f1f5f9; 
            padding: 15px; 
            border: 1px solid #cbd5e1;
            border-radius: 8px;
        """)
        self.lbl_opt_energy.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        
        # vertical sliders side by side
        sliders_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        
        # regen slider
        regen_vbox = QVBoxLayout()
        self.lbl_regen = QLabel("Regen:\n0 %")
        self.lbl_regen.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        self.lbl_regen.setStyleSheet("font-weight: bold; color: #475569;")
        
        self.slider_regen = QSlider(Qt.Orientation.Vertical)
        self.slider_regen.setMinimum(int(min(self.grid_regen) * 100))
        self.slider_regen.setMaximum(100)
        self.slider_regen.setValue(int(max(self.grid_regen) * 50))
        self.slider_regen.setTickPosition(QSlider.TickPosition.TicksLeft)
        
        regen_vbox.addWidget(self.lbl_regen)
        regen_vbox.addWidget(self.slider_regen, alignment=Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignHCenter)
        
        # delta slider
        delta_vbox = QVBoxLayout()
        self.lbl_delta = QLabel("Delta:\n0 %")
        self.lbl_delta.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        self.lbl_delta.setStyleSheet("font-weight: bold; color: #475569;")
        
        self.slider_delta = QSlider(Qt.Orientation.Vertical)
        self.slider_delta.setMinimum(int(min(self.grid_delta) * 100))
        self.slider_delta.setMaximum(int(max(self.grid_delta) * 100))
        self.slider_delta.setValue(0)
        self.slider_delta.setTickPosition(QSlider.TickPosition.TicksRight)
        
        delta_vbox.addWidget(self.lbl_delta)
        delta_vbox.addWidget(self.slider_delta, alignment=Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignHCenter)
        
        sliders_layout.addLayout(regen_vbox)
        sliders_layout.addLayout(delta_vbox)
        
        control_layout.addWidget(self.lbl_opt_energy)
        control_layout.addSpacing(20)
        control_layout.addLayout(sliders_layout)
        
        # --- main container assembly with graph ---
        graph_container = QWidget()
        graph_container.setObjectName("CanvasContainer")
        graph_main_layout = QVBoxLayout(graph_container)
        
        #raph_main_layout.addWidget(self.toolbar)
        
        # splitting: left panel with control, right part giant graph
        content_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        content_layout.addWidget(control_panel)
        content_layout.addWidget(self.canvas, stretch=1) 
        
        graph_main_layout.addLayout(content_layout)
        main_layout.addWidget(graph_container)
        
        self.ax3d = None
        self.view_elev = 20  
        self.view_azim = 38
        self.point_plot = None 
        
        self.plot_optimal_surface()
        
        self.slider_regen.valueChanged.connect(self.update_point)
        self.slider_delta.valueChanged.connect(self.update_point)
        
        self.update_point()

    def plot_optimal_surface(self):
        r_fine = np.linspace(min(self.grid_regen), max(self.grid_regen), 50)
        d_fine = np.linspace(min(self.grid_delta), max(self.grid_delta), 50)

        R_mesh, D_mesh = np.meshgrid(r_fine, d_fine, indexing='ij')
        pts_scipy = np.column_stack((R_mesh.ravel(), D_mesh.ravel()))
        
        Z_opt_energy = self.e_interpolant(pts_scipy).reshape(R_mesh.shape)
        
        self.fig.clear()
        self.ax3d = self.fig.add_subplot(1, 1, 1, projection='3d')
        
        surf = self.ax3d.plot_surface(
            R_mesh * 100, D_mesh * 100, Z_opt_energy, 
            cmap='plasma', edgecolor='none', alpha=0.9,
            vmin=self.z_min, vmax=self.z_max
        )
        
        self.ax3d.set_xlabel('Recuperation [%]', color='#475569')
        self.ax3d.set_ylabel('Time delta [%]', color='#475569')
        self.ax3d.set_zlabel('Energy target [kWh]', color='#475569')

        
        self.ax3d.xaxis.set_tick_params(colors='#64748b')
        self.ax3d.yaxis.set_tick_params(colors='#64748b')
        self.ax3d.zaxis.set_tick_params(colors='#64748b')

        self.ax3d.view_init(elev=self.view_elev, azim=self.view_azim)
        
        # more aggressive margin reduction for matplotlib to utilize more space
        self.fig.subplots_adjust(left=0.0, right=0.95, bottom=0.05, top=0.95)

    def update_point(self):
        regen_val = self.slider_regen.value() / 100.0
        delta_val = self.slider_delta.value() / 100.0
        
        # added new lines (\n) so text above vertical slider looks good
        self.lbl_regen.setText(f"Regen:\n{regen_val*100:.0f} %")
        self.lbl_delta.setText(f"Delta:\n{delta_val*100:.0f} %")

        opt_e = float(self.e_interpolant([regen_val, delta_val])[0])
        opt_soc = float(self.soc_interpolant([regen_val, delta_val])[0])
        
        self.lbl_opt_energy.setText(
            f"<div style='font-size: 13px; color: #64748b; text-transform: uppercase;'>Optimal Energy</div>"
            f"<div style='font-size: 26px; color: #10b981; font-weight: bold;'>{opt_e:.3f} kWh</div>"
            f"<hr style='border: 1px solid #cbd5e1; margin: 10px 0;'>"
            f"<div style='font-size: 13px; color: #64748b; text-transform: uppercase;'>Optimal SoC Start</div>"
            f"<div style='font-size: 26px; color: #3b82f6; font-weight: bold;'>{opt_soc*100:.1f} %</div>"
        )

        if self.point_plot is not None:
            self.point_plot.remove()
            self.point_plot = None
            
        if self.ax3d is not None and not np.isnan(opt_e):
            self.point_plot, = self.ax3d.plot(
                [regen_val * 100], [delta_val * 100], [opt_e],
                marker='o', color='#0f172a', markersize=12, markeredgecolor='white', markeredgewidth=2, zorder=10
            )
            
            self.canvas.draw_idle()

from PyQt6.QtWidgets import QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QLabel, QProgressBar
from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt, QSize
from PyQt6.QtGui import QPalette, QColor, QMovie

class LoadingOverlay(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setAttribute(Qt.WidgetAttribute.WA_TransparentForMouseEvents, False) 
        self.hide()
        
        self.setAttribute(Qt.WidgetAttribute.WA_StyledBackground, True)
     
       
        self.setObjectName("LoadingOverlay")
        self.setStyleSheet("""
            QWidget#LoadingOverlay {
                background-color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 200);
            }
            QLabel {
                background: transparent;
            }
        """)
        
        # Layout
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        layout.setSpacing(20) # Mezera mezi GIFem a textem
        
        # gif label
        self.lbl_gif = QLabel()
        self.lbl_gif.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
     
        gif_path = "data/loading1.gif" 
        self.movie = QMovie(gif_path)
     
        self.lbl_gif.setFixedSize(300, 300)
        self.lbl_gif.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        
        self.movie.frameChanged.connect(self.update_gif_frame)
        self.movie.finished.connect(self.movie.start)
        
        # text label
        self.lbl_text = QLabel("Calculating...")
        self.lbl_text.setStyleSheet("color: white; font-size: 18px; font-weight: bold;")
        self.lbl_text.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        
        # progress bar
        self.progress = QProgressBar()
        self.progress.setFixedWidth(300)
        self.progress.setTextVisible(True)
        self.progress.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        self.progress.setStyleSheet("""
            QProgressBar {
                border: 2px solid grey;
                border-radius: 5px;
                background-color: #333;
                color: white;
                font-weight: bold;
            }
            QProgressBar::chunk {
                background-color: #007bff;
                width: 20px; 
            }
        """)
        
       
        layout.addWidget(self.lbl_gif)   # GIF nahoře
        layout.addWidget(self.lbl_text)  # Text uprostřed
        layout.addWidget(self.progress)  # Bar dole
        
        self.setLayout(layout)
        
        self.current_progress_fmt = None

    def show_loading(self, text="Calculating...", indefinite=True, progress_text_fmt=None):
        """
        shows overlay
        Args:
            text: Úvodní text.
            indefinite: 
                True = Single Lap / ACP -> Ukáže GIF, skryje Bar.
                False = Endurance -> Ukáže Bar, skryje GIF.
        """
        if self.parent():
            self.resize(self.parent().size())
            
        self.lbl_text.setText(text)
        
        # Uložení formátu textu
        if progress_text_fmt:
            self.current_progress_fmt = progress_text_fmt
        else:
            self.current_progress_fmt = f"{text} ({{}} / {{}})"

        if indefinite:
            # gif 
            self.progress.hide()      
            self.lbl_gif.show()        
            self.movie.start()        
        else:
            # progress bar
            self.lbl_gif.hide()       
            self.movie.stop()          
            
            self.progress.show()       
            self.progress.setRange(0, 100)
            self.progress.setValue(0)

        self.show()
        self.raise_()

    def update_gif_frame(self, frame_number):
        pixmap = self.movie.currentPixmap()
        if pixmap.isNull():
            return
            
        ratio = self.devicePixelRatioF()
        physical_size = int(300 * ratio)
        
        # Smoothly scale the frame to the target physical size
        scaled_pixmap = pixmap.scaled(
            physical_size, physical_size, 
            Qt.AspectRatioMode.KeepAspectRatio, 
            Qt.TransformationMode.SmoothTransformation
        )
        
        # Tell Qt this is a High-DPI pixmap before displaying it
        scaled_pixmap.setDevicePixelRatio(ratio)
        self.lbl_gif.setPixmap(scaled_pixmap)

    def update_progress(self, current, total):
       
        if self.current_progress_fmt:
            try:
                self.lbl_text.setText(self.current_progress_fmt.format(current, total))
            except:
                self.lbl_text.setText(f"{current} / {total}")
        
        if self.progress.minimum() == 0 and self.progress.maximum() == 0:
            self.progress.setRange(0, total)
            self.progress.setTextVisible(True)
            self.progress.setFormat("%v / %m") 
            
        self.progress.setValue(current)

    def hide_loading(self):
        self.movie.stop() 
        self.hide()

import os
import json
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import (QDialog, QVBoxLayout, QTableWidget, QTableWidgetItem, 
                             QPushButton, QHBoxLayout, QHeaderView, QMessageBox)
from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt
class LoadOptimizationDialog(QDialog):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setWindowTitle("Load optimization run")
        self.resize(700, 400)
        self.selected_data = None
        
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        
        self.table = QTableWidget(0, 4)
        self.table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(["Date", "Base Vehicle", "Tracks", "Best Lap Time"])
        self.table.horizontalHeader().setSectionResizeMode(QHeaderView.ResizeMode.Stretch)
        self.table.setSelectionBehavior(QTableWidget.SelectionBehavior.SelectRows)
        self.table.setSelectionMode(QTableWidget.SelectionMode.SingleSelection)
        self.table.setEditTriggers(QTableWidget.EditTrigger.NoEditTriggers)
        layout.addWidget(self.table)
        
        btn_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        btn_load = QPushButton("Load selected")
        btn_load.setObjectName("BtnOptimize")
        btn_load.clicked.connect(self.load_selected)
        
        btn_cancel = QPushButton("Cancel")
        btn_cancel.clicked.connect(self.reject)
        
        btn_layout.addStretch()
        btn_layout.addWidget(btn_load)
        btn_layout.addWidget(btn_cancel)
        layout.addLayout(btn_layout)
        
        self.files_map = {} 
        self.populate_table()
        
    def populate_table(self):
        save_dir = os.path.join("data", "optimizations")
        if not os.path.exists(save_dir):
            return
            
        row = 0
    
        for filename in sorted(os.listdir(save_dir), reverse=True):
            if filename.endswith(".json"):
                filepath = os.path.join(save_dir, filename)
                try:
                    with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                        data = json.load(f)
                        meta = data.get("metadata", {})
                        
                        date_str = meta.get("timestamp", filename)
                        vehicle = meta.get("base_vehicle_name", "N/A")
                        tracks = ", ".join(meta.get("tracks_used", []))
                        best_time = f"{meta.get('best_lap_time', 0):.3f} s"
                        
                        self.table.insertRow(row)
                        self.table.setItem(row, 0, QTableWidgetItem(date_str))
                        self.table.setItem(row, 1, QTableWidgetItem(vehicle))
                        self.table.setItem(row, 2, QTableWidgetItem(tracks))
                  
                        time_item = QTableWidgetItem(best_time)
                        time_item.setTextAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignRight | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)
                        self.table.setItem(row, 3, time_item)
                        
                        self.files_map[row] = filepath
                        row += 1
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"Failed to load {filename}: {e}")
                    
    def load_selected(self):
        selected = self.table.selectedItems()
        if not selected:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Warning", "Please select a run to load.")
            return
            
        row = selected[0].row()
        filepath = self.files_map.get(row)
        if filepath:
            try:
                with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    self.selected_data = json.load(f)
                self.accept()
            except Exception as e:
                QMessageBox.critical(self, "Error", f"Failed to load file:\n{e}")

class MplCanvas(FigureCanvasQTAgg):
    def __init__(self, parent=None, width=5, height=4, dpi=100):
        self.fig = Figure(figsize=(width, height), dpi=dpi)
        self.fig.patch.set_facecolor('#ffffff')
        self.axes = self.fig.add_subplot(111)
        self.axes.set_facecolor('#ffffff')
        super(MplCanvas, self).__init__(self.fig)
        self.setParent(parent)
        FigureCanvasQTAgg.updateGeometry(self)

    # ==========================================
    # vehicle profile management
    # ==========================================
    def load_vehicle_list(self, select_name=None):
        vehicles = ConfigManager.get_available_vehicles()
        
        # create default if directory is totally empty
        if not vehicles:
            ConfigManager.save_vehicle("default_car", self.default_params)
            vehicles = ["default_car"]
            
        self.combo_vehicle.blockSignals(True)
        self.combo_vehicle.clear()
        self.combo_vehicle.addItems(vehicles)
        
        if select_name and select_name in vehicles:
            self.combo_vehicle.setCurrentText(select_name)
        else:
            self.combo_vehicle.setCurrentIndex(0)
            
        self.combo_vehicle.blockSignals(False)
        
        # force load the selected vehicle
        self.current_vehicle_name = self.combo_vehicle.currentText()
        self.car_params = ConfigManager.load_vehicle(self.current_vehicle_name, self.default_params)
        self._update_ui_from_params()

    def on_vehicle_changed(self):
        # 1. save the outgoing vehicle first
        if self.current_vehicle_name:
            self._sync_params_from_ui()
            ConfigManager.save_vehicle(self.current_vehicle_name, self.car_params)
            
        # 2. load the incoming vehicle
        new_name = self.combo_vehicle.currentText()
        if new_name:
            self.current_vehicle_name = new_name
            self.car_params = ConfigManager.load_vehicle(new_name, self.default_params)
            self._update_ui_from_params()

    def on_new_vehicle_clicked(self):
        # ask user for a new name
        name, ok = QInputDialog.getText(self, "New vehicle", "Enter vehicle name:")
        if ok and name:
            name = name.strip()
            if name in ConfigManager.get_available_vehicles():
                QMessageBox.warning(self, "error", "Vehicle with this name already exists!")
                return
            
            # create new vehicle by duplicating current parameters
            self._sync_params_from_ui()
            ConfigManager.save_vehicle(name, self.car_params)
            self.load_vehicle_list(select_name=name)

    # ==========================================
    # ui initialization methods
    # ==========================================
    def open_car_config(self):
        dialog = CarConfigDialog(self.car_params, self)
        if dialog.exec() == QDialog.DialogCode.Accepted:
            self.car_params = dialog.get_params()
            self._sync_params_from_ui()
            ConfigManager.save_vehicle(self.current_vehicle_name, self.car_params)
            print(f"vehicle '{self.current_vehicle_name}' updated and saved.")

    def _update_ui_from_params(self):
        """pushes loaded dict values into the ui widgets safely."""
        idx = self.combo_model.findData(self.car_params.get("model_type", "mass_point"))
        if idx >= 0: self.combo_model.setCurrentIndex(idx)
        
        self.input_ds.setValue(self.car_params.get("ds", 0.5))
        
        saved_e_limit = self.car_params.get("energy_limit")
        self.check_energy_limit.setChecked(saved_e_limit is not None)
        self.input_limit_e.setValue(saved_e_limit if saved_e_limit is not None else 0.2)
        self.input_limit_e.setEnabled(self.check_energy_limit.isChecked())
        
        # update soc_start
        soc_val = self.car_params.get("soc_start", 0.67)
        self.input_soc.setValue(soc_val * 100.0)
        
        self.check_drs.setChecked(self.car_params.get("drs_allowed", True))

        group = QGroupBox("Run actions")
