    # ==========================================
    # core logic / sync
    # ==========================================
    def _sync_params_from_ui(self):
        self.car_params["model_type"] = self.combo_model.currentData()
        self.car_params["drs_allowed"] = self.check_drs.isChecked()
        self.car_params["energy_limit"] = self.input_limit_e.value() if self.check_energy_limit.isChecked() else None
        self.car_params["ds"] = self.input_ds.value()
        self.car_params["soc_start"] = self.input_soc.value()/100# grab soc from UI
        self.car_params["name"] = self.current_vehicle_name

    def _get_car_params(self):
        self._sync_params_from_ui()
        strict_params = self.car_params.copy()
        
        strict_params.pop("laps", None)
        strict_params.pop("ds", None) 
        strict_params.pop("mass", None)
        
        p_kw = strict_params.pop("power_limit", 80.0)
        strict_params["power_max"] = p_kw * 1000.0
        
        return strict_params

    def closeEvent(self, event):
        """ensures data is saved before the window is killed."""
        self._sync_params_from_ui()
        if self.current_vehicle_name:
            ConfigManager.save_vehicle(self.current_vehicle_name, self.car_params)
        super().closeEvent(event)

    # ==========================================
    # track loading
    # ==========================================
    def load_track_list(self):
        track_dir = "data/tracks"
        if not os.path.exists(track_dir):
            os.makedirs(track_dir)
            
        files = glob.glob(os.path.join(track_dir, "*npz"))
        track_names = [os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(f))[0] for f in files]
        
        current = self.combo_track.currentText()
        self.combo_track.blockSignals(True) 
        self.combo_track.clear()
        
        if track_names:
            self.combo_track.addItems(sorted(track_names))
            idx = self.combo_track.findText(current)
            if idx >= 0:
                self.combo_track.setCurrentIndex(idx)
        else:
            self.combo_track.addItem("no tracks found")
            self.combo_track.setEnabled(False)
            
        self.combo_track.blockSignals(False)
        self.on_track_changed()

    def on_track_changed(self):
        track_name = self.combo_track.currentText()
        if not track_name or track_name == "no tracks found":
            return
        try:
            self.current_track = Track.load(track_name)
            self.plot_track_map(self.current_track)
            
            dist_m = 22000
            laps = int(round(dist_m / self.current_track.length))
            self.car_params["laps"] = max(1, laps)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"error loading track '{track_name}': {e}")

    def plot_track_map(self, track: Track):
        self.mpl_canvas.fig.clf()
       
        ax = self.mpl_canvas.fig.add_subplot(111)
        ax.set_facecolor('#ffffff')
        
        x, y = track.x, track.y
        
        alpha_deg = np.degrees(track.alpha) if hasattr(track, 'alpha') and track.alpha is not None else np.zeros_like(x)

      
        points = np.array([x, y]).T.reshape(-1, 1, 2)
        segments = np.concatenate([points[:-1], points[1:]], axis=1)

  
        norm = Normalize(vmin=np.min(alpha_deg), vmax=np.max(alpha_deg))
        lc = LineCollection(segments, cmap='viridis', norm=norm)
        lc.set_array(alpha_deg[:-1])
        lc.set_linewidth(3.5) 
        
        line = ax.add_collection(lc)

       
        ax.set_xlim(x.min() - 20, x.max() + 20)
        ax.set_ylim(y.min() - 20, y.max() + 20)
        ax.set_aspect('equal')
        
      
        ax.plot(x[0], y[0], 'o', color='#10b981', markersize=8, markeredgecolor='black', zorder=5)
        
       
        self.mpl_canvas.fig.subplots_adjust(right=0.85)
        
        cax = self.mpl_canvas.fig.add_axes([0.88, 0.15, 0.02, 0.7])
        cb = self.mpl_canvas.fig.colorbar(line, cax=cax)
        

        cb.outline.set_visible(False) # Skrytí rámečku legendy
        cb.set_label('Elevation [deg]', color='#64748b', size=10, labelpad=10)
        cb.ax.yaxis.set_tick_params(colors='#64748b', labelsize=9)
        
        # Vypnutí os mapy
        ax.axis('off')
        
        # Překreslení
        self.mpl_canvas.draw()
  
    # ==========================================
    # request handlers
    # ==========================================
    def on_run_single_clicked(self):
        req = SingleLapRequest(
            car_params=self._get_car_params(),
            ds=self.input_ds.value(), 
            track_name=self.combo_track.currentText()
        )
        self.controller.handle_request(req)

    def on_lap_map_clicked(self):
        req = LapMapTestRequest(
            car_params=self._get_car_params(),
            ds=self.input_ds.value(), 
            track_name=self.combo_track.currentText(),
            laps=self.car_params["laps"] 
        )
        self.controller.handle_request(req)

    def on_endurance_strategy_clicked(self):
        track_name = self.combo_track.currentText()
        self.openStrategyRequested.emit(track_name)

    def on_optimize_car_clicked(self):
      
        dialog = OptimizationDialog(self.current_vehicle_name, self)
        if dialog.exec() == QDialog.DialogCode.Accepted:
            opt_data = dialog.get_optimization_data()
            
            
            base_car_params = ConfigManager.load_vehicle(opt_data["vehicle_name"], self.default_params)

            base_car_params.pop("laps", None)
            base_car_params.pop("ds", None) 
            base_car_params.pop("mass", None)
            
            p_kw = base_car_params.pop("power_limit", 80.0)
            base_car_params["power_max"] = p_kw * 1000.0
            
          
            req = OptimizationRequest(
                car_params=base_car_params,
                ds=self.input_ds.value(), 
                vehicle_name=opt_data["vehicle_name"],
                tracks=opt_data["tracks"],
                bounds=opt_data["bounds"]
            )
            
            
            self.controller.handle_request(req)

    def on_sensitivity_test_clicked(self):
        from src.ui.sensitivity_dialog import SensitivityDialog
        dialog = SensitivityDialog(self.current_vehicle_name, self.car_params, self)
        dialog.exec()

    def on_compare_clicked(self):
        self.openCompareRequested.emit()

    def on_create_track_clicked(self):
        from src.ui.track_generator_dialog import TrackGeneratorDialog
        dialog = TrackGeneratorDialog(self)
        if dialog.exec() != QDialog.DialogCode.Accepted:
            return  

        fname = dialog.selected_file
        is_closed_mode = dialog.radio_closed.isChecked()
        r_penalty = dialog.spin_r.value()
        w_penalty = dialog.spin_w.value()

        if not fname:
            return

        self.loading_overlay.show_loading("parsing log data...", indefinite=True)
        QApplication.processEvents()

        try:
            from src.handlers.telemetry_parser import filter_data
            data = filter_data(fname)
        except Exception as e:
            self.loading_overlay.hide_loading()
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "error", f"failed to parse file:\n{e}")
            return
            
        self.loading_overlay.hide_loading()
        if data is None: 
            return

        from src.ui.track_tools import run_lap_selector, manual_lap_selector
        
        # pass self as parent so dialogs center correctly over main window
        if is_closed_mode:
            lap_infos = run_lap_selector(data, self)
        else:
            lap_infos = manual_lap_selector(data, self)

        if not lap_infos:
            return 

        self.loading_overlay.show_loading("smoothing track (casadi)...", indefinite=True)
        from src.handlers.requests import TrackGenerationRequest
        
        req = TrackGenerationRequest(
            car_params=self._get_car_params(), 
            ds=self.input_ds.value(),          
            data=data,
            lap_infos=lap_infos,
            is_closed=is_closed_mode,
            r_penalty=r_penalty,
            w_penalty=w_penalty
        )
        self.controller.handle_request(req)

    def finish_track_generation(self, result_data):
        
    
        # show native pyqtgraph dialog instead of MapDRSEditor class
        dialog = DRSEditorDialog(result_data['x'], result_data['y'], result_data['s'], self)
        dialog.exec()
        result_data['drs'] = dialog.get_mask()

        avg_time = result_data.get('lap_time', 0.0)
        default_name = f"track_{avg_time:.2f}s.npz"
        
        save_path, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(
            self, "save baked track", 
            os.path.join("data/tracks", default_name), 
            "NumPy Zip (*.npz)"
        )

        if save_path:
            
            np.savez(save_path, **result_data)
            QMessageBox.information(self, "success", f"track saved successfully!\n{save_path}")
            
            self.load_track_list()
            track_name = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(save_path))[0]
            idx = self.combo_track.findText(track_name)
            if idx >= 0:
                self.combo_track.setCurrentIndex(idx)


class CarConfigDialog(QDialog):
    def __init__(self, car_params=None, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setWindowTitle("Vehicle configuration")
        self.setMinimumWidth(500)
        self.setMinimumHeight(700)
        
        # Profile parameters from UI 
        self.params = car_params if car_params else {}
        
        self._init_ui()
        self._populate_data()

    def _init_ui(self):
        # Main dialog layout 
        self.main_layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.main_layout.setSpacing(0)
        self.main_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)

        # 1. Scroll Area - fix for black background 
        self.scroll_area = QScrollArea()
        self.scroll_area.setWidgetResizable(True)
        self.scroll_area.setFrameShape(QScrollArea.Shape.NoFrame)
        # Force transparent viewport to see the container background 
        self.scroll_area.viewport().setStyleSheet("background: transparent;")
        
        # 2. Content Container 
        self.container = QWidget()
        # White background matches your QTabWidget > QWidget rule 
        self.container.setStyleSheet("background-color: #ffffff;")
        
        self.container_layout = QVBoxLayout(self.container)
        self.container_layout.setContentsMargins(20, 20, 20, 20)
        self.container_layout.setSpacing(15)

        # Helper to style groupboxes with blue border 
        blue_border_style = "QGroupBox { border: 1px solid #3b82f6; }"

        # --- Section: General ---
        group_general = QGroupBox("General")
        group_general.setStyleSheet(blue_border_style)
        form_general = QFormLayout()
        self.edit_name = QLineEdit()
        form_general.addRow("Profile name:", self.edit_name)
        group_general.setLayout(form_general)
        self.container_layout.addWidget(group_general)

        # --- Section: Chassis & Geometry ---
        group_chassis = QGroupBox("Chassis")
        group_chassis.setStyleSheet(blue_border_style)
        form_chassis = QFormLayout()
        self.spin_weight = self._create_double_spinbox(0, 1000, 280.0, decimals=1)
        self.spin_wb = self._create_double_spinbox(0.1, 5.0, 1.53, decimals=3)
        self.spin_tw = self._create_double_spinbox(0.1, 3.0, 1.20, decimals=3)
        self.spin_wd = self._create_double_spinbox(0.0, 1.0, 0.53, step=0.01)
        self.spin_cogz = self._create_double_spinbox(0.0, 1.5, 0.25, step=0.01)

        form_chassis.addRow("Total weight [kg]:", self.spin_weight)
        form_chassis.addRow("Wheelbase [m]:", self.spin_wb)
        form_chassis.addRow("Track width [m]:", self.spin_tw)
        form_chassis.addRow("Weight distribution (front) [0-1]:", self.spin_wd)
        form_chassis.addRow("CoG height [m]:", self.spin_cogz)
        group_chassis.setLayout(form_chassis)
        self.container_layout.addWidget(group_chassis)

        # --- Section: Aerodynamics ---
        group_aero = QGroupBox("Aerodynamics")
        group_aero.setStyleSheet(blue_border_style)
        form_aero = QFormLayout()
        self.spin_cd = self._create_double_spinbox(0, 10, 1.83, step=0.01)
        self.spin_cl = self._create_double_spinbox(0, 15, 5.64, step=0.05)
        self.spin_cop = self._create_double_spinbox(0, 1.0, 0.53, step=0.01)
        self.spin_cd_drs = self._create_double_spinbox(0, 10, 1.17, step=0.01)
        self.spin_cl_drs = self._create_double_spinbox(0, 15, 4.20, step=0.05)

        form_aero.addRow("Drag coefficient (CdA):", self.spin_cd)
        form_aero.addRow("Lift coefficient (ClA):", self.spin_cl)
        form_aero.addRow("Center of pressure [0-1]:", self.spin_cop)
        form_aero.addRow("CdA (DRS open):", self.spin_cd_drs)
        form_aero.addRow("ClA (DRS open):", self.spin_cl_drs)
        group_aero.setLayout(form_aero)
        self.container_layout.addWidget(group_aero)

        # --- Section: Powertrain ---
        group_power = QGroupBox("Powertrain")
        group_power.setStyleSheet(blue_border_style)
        form_power = QFormLayout()
        self.spin_power_max = self._create_double_spinbox(0, 250, 80.0, step=1.0, decimals=1)
        self.spin_M_max = self._create_double_spinbox(0, 500, 30.0, step=0.5)
        self.spin_i = self._create_double_spinbox(0, 50, 12.0, step=0.1)
        self.spin_regen = self._create_double_spinbox(0.0, 1.0, 0.8, step=0.05)

        form_power.addRow("Max power limit [kW]:", self.spin_power_max)
        form_power.addRow("Max motor torque [Nm]:", self.spin_M_max)
        form_power.addRow("Gear ratio (i):", self.spin_i)
        form_power.addRow("Driver recuperation factor [0-1]:", self.spin_regen)
        group_power.setLayout(form_power)
        self.container_layout.addWidget(group_power)

        self.scroll_area.setWidget(self.container)
        self.main_layout.addWidget(self.scroll_area)

        # --- Bottom Button Area ---

        self.button_container = QWidget()
        self.button_container.setObjectName("HighlightedGroup")
        
        self.button_layout = QHBoxLayout(self.button_container)
        self.button_layout.setContentsMargins(15, 15, 15, 15)

        self.buttons = QDialogButtonBox(QDialogButtonBox.StandardButton.Ok | QDialogButtonBox.StandardButton.Cancel)
        self.buttons.accepted.connect(self.accept)
        self.buttons.rejected.connect(self.reject)
        
        self.button_layout.addWidget(self.buttons)
        self.main_layout.addWidget(self.button_container)

    def _create_double_spinbox(self, v_min, v_max, default_val, step=0.1, decimals=2):
        sb = QDoubleSpinBox()
        sb.setRange(v_min, v_max)
        sb.setSingleStep(step)
        sb.setDecimals(decimals)
        sb.setValue(default_val)
        return sb

    def _populate_data(self):
        # Fill UI from dict 
        p = self.params
        self.edit_name.setText(p.get("name", "New Vehicle"))
        self.spin_weight.setValue(p.get("weight", 280.0))
        self.spin_wb.setValue(p.get("wb", 1.53))
        self.spin_tw.setValue(p.get("tw", 1.20))
        self.spin_wd.setValue(p.get("wd", 0.53))
        self.spin_cogz.setValue(p.get("cogz", 0.25))
        self.spin_cd.setValue(p.get("cd", 1.83))
        self.spin_cl.setValue(p.get("cl", 5.64))
        self.spin_cop.setValue(p.get("cop", 0.53))
        self.spin_cd_drs.setValue(p.get("cd_drs", 1.17))
        self.spin_cl_drs.setValue(p.get("cl_drs", 4.2))
        self.spin_power_max.setValue(p.get("power_limit", 80.0))
        self.spin_M_max.setValue(p.get("M_max", 30.0))
        self.spin_i.setValue(p.get("i", 12.0))
        self.spin_regen.setValue(p.get("regen_eff", 0.8))

    def get_params(self) -> dict:
        # Return updated dict 
        new_params = self.params.copy()
        new_params.update({
            "name": self.edit_name.text(),
            "weight": self.spin_weight.value(),
            "wb": self.spin_wb.value(),
            "tw": self.spin_tw.value(),
            "wd": self.spin_wd.value(),
            "cogz": self.spin_cogz.value(),
            "cd": self.spin_cd.value(),
            "cl": self.spin_cl.value(),
            "cop": self.spin_cop.value(),
            "cd_drs": self.spin_cd_drs.value(),
            "cl_drs": self.spin_cl_drs.value(),
            "power_limit": self.spin_power_max.value(),
            "M_max": self.spin_M_max.value(),
            "i": self.spin_i.value(),
            "regen_eff": self.spin_regen.value()
        })
        return new_params

import os
import glob
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import (QDialog, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QGroupBox, 
                             QScrollArea, QWidget, QCheckBox, QComboBox, 
                             QLabel, QDoubleSpinBox, QPushButton, QGridLayout, QMessageBox)
from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt
from src.utils.config_manager import ConfigManager
from src.ui.optimization_results_dialog import OptimizationResultsDialog
from src.ui.load_optimization_dialog import LoadOptimizationDialog

class OptimizationDialog(QDialog):
    def __init__(self, current_vehicle_name, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setWindowTitle("Car parameters optimization")
        self.setMinimumWidth(500)
        self.setMinimumHeight(600)
        
        self.current_vehicle_name = current_vehicle_name
        self._init_ui()
        
    def _init_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setSpacing(15)
       
        group_car = QGroupBox("1. Base vehicle")
        group_car.setStyleSheet("QGroupBox { border: 1px solid #3b82f6; }")
        layout_car = QHBoxLayout(group_car)
        
        self.combo_vehicle = QComboBox()
        vehicles = ConfigManager.get_available_vehicles()
        self.combo_vehicle.addItems(vehicles)
        if self.current_vehicle_name in vehicles:
            self.combo_vehicle.setCurrentText(self.current_vehicle_name)
            
        layout_car.addWidget(QLabel("Select base vehicle profile:"))
        layout_car.addWidget(self.combo_vehicle)
        layout.addWidget(group_car)
        
      
        group_tracks = QGroupBox("2. Tracks to evaluate")
        group_tracks.setStyleSheet("QGroupBox { border: 1px solid #3b82f6; }")
        layout_tracks = QVBoxLayout(group_tracks)
        
        scroll_tracks = QScrollArea()
        scroll_tracks.setWidgetResizable(True)
        scroll_tracks.setFrameShape(QScrollArea.Shape.NoFrame)
        scroll_tracks.viewport().setStyleSheet("background: transparent;")
        
        container_tracks = QWidget()
        container_tracks.setStyleSheet("background-color: #ffffff;")
        self.layout_track_list = QVBoxLayout(container_tracks)
        
        self.track_checkboxes = {}
        self._load_tracks()
        
        self.layout_track_list.addStretch()
        scroll_tracks.setWidget(container_tracks)
        layout_tracks.addWidget(scroll_tracks)
        layout.addWidget(group_tracks)
        
      
        group_params = QGroupBox("3. Parameters bounds")
        group_params.setStyleSheet("QGroupBox { border: 1px solid #3b82f6; }")
        layout_params = QGridLayout(group_params)
        
        layout_params.addWidget(QLabel("Optimize"), 0, 0)
        layout_params.addWidget(QLabel("Parameter"), 0, 1)
        layout_params.addWidget(QLabel("Min bound"), 0, 2)
        layout_params.addWidget(QLabel("Max bound"), 0, 3)
        
        self.param_widgets = {}
      
        params_setup = [
            ("cop", "Center of pressure [0-1]", 0.40, 0.60, 0.01),
            ("wb", "Wheelbase [m]", 1.40, 1.65, 0.01),
            ("tw", "Car track width [m]", 1.15, 1.35, 0.01),
            ("wd", "Weight dist. front [0-1]", 0.45, 0.55, 0.01),
            ("cogz", "CoG height [m]", 0.20, 0.35, 0.01)
        ]
        
        for i, (key, label, def_min, def_max, step) in enumerate(params_setup, start=1):
            cb_enable = QCheckBox()
            cb_enable.setChecked(True) 
            
            lbl_name = QLabel(label)
            
            spin_min = QDoubleSpinBox()
            spin_min.setRange(0.0, 5.0)
            spin_min.setSingleStep(step)
            spin_min.setValue(def_min)
            
            spin_max = QDoubleSpinBox()
            spin_max.setRange(0.0, 5.0)
            spin_max.setSingleStep(step)
            spin_max.setValue(def_max)
            
            cb_enable.toggled.connect(spin_min.setEnabled)
            cb_enable.toggled.connect(spin_max.setEnabled)
            
            layout_params.addWidget(cb_enable, i, 0, alignment=Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
            layout_params.addWidget(lbl_name, i, 1)
            layout_params.addWidget(spin_min, i, 2)
            layout_params.addWidget(spin_max, i, 3)
            
            self.param_widgets[key] = {"enable": cb_enable, "min": spin_min, "max": spin_max}
            
        layout.addWidget(group_params)
        
   
        btn_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.btn_cancel = QPushButton("Cancel")
        self.btn_cancel.clicked.connect(self.reject)
        
        
        self.btn_load_run = QPushButton("Load Previous Run")
        self.btn_load_run.setObjectName("BtnOptimize") 
        self.btn_load_run.clicked.connect(self.load_previous_run)
        
        self.btn_start = QPushButton("Start optimization")
        self.btn_start.setObjectName("BtnRunSingle") 
        self.btn_start.clicked.connect(self.validate_and_accept)
        
        btn_layout.addStretch()
        btn_layout.addWidget(self.btn_cancel)
        btn_layout.addWidget(self.btn_load_run) 
        btn_layout.addWidget(self.btn_start)
        layout.addLayout(btn_layout)

    def _load_tracks(self):
        track_dir = "data/tracks"
        if os.path.exists(track_dir):
            files = glob.glob(os.path.join(track_dir, "*npz"))
            track_names = sorted([os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(f))[0] for f in files])
            for t_name in track_names:
                cb = QCheckBox(t_name)
                self.layout_track_list.addWidget(cb)
                self.track_checkboxes[t_name] = cb

    def validate_and_accept(self):
    
        selected = [n for n, cb in self.track_checkboxes.items() if cb.isChecked()]
        if not selected:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Warning", "Please select at least one track for optimization.")
            return
            
      
        for key, w in self.param_widgets.items():
            if w["enable"].isChecked() and w["min"].value() >= w["max"].value():
                QMessageBox.warning(self, "Warning", f"Min bound must be strictly less than Max bound for {key}!")
                return
                
        self.accept()

    def get_optimization_data(self):
        selected_tracks = [name for name, cb in self.track_checkboxes.items() if cb.isChecked()]
        bounds = {}
        for key, widgets in self.param_widgets.items():
            if widgets["enable"].isChecked():
                bounds[key] = (widgets["min"].value(), widgets["max"].value())
                
        return {
            "vehicle_name": self.combo_vehicle.currentText(),
            "tracks": selected_tracks,
            "bounds": bounds
        }
    
    def load_previous_run(self):
        dialog = LoadOptimizationDialog(self)
        if dialog.exec():
          
            json_data = dialog.selected_data
            
            
            res_dialog = OptimizationResultsDialog(payload=None, json_data=json_data, parent=self)
            res_dialog.exec()

import os
import json
from datetime import datetime
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import pyqtgraph as pg
try:
    import optuna
except ImportError:
    pass

from sklearn.gaussian_process import GaussianProcessRegressor
from sklearn.gaussian_process.kernels import Matern, WhiteKernel
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler

from PyQt6.QtWidgets import (QDialog, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QGroupBox, 
                             QLabel, QPushButton, QTabWidget, QWidget, QComboBox, 
                             QSlider, QFileDialog, QMessageBox)
from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt

class OptimizationResultsDialog(QDialog):
    C_SIM = '#3b82f6'
    C_BEST = '#10b981'
    C_REF = '#334155'
    
    def __init__(self, payload=None, json_data=None, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.payload = payload
        self.json_data = json_data
        
        pg.setConfigOption('background', 'w')
        pg.setConfigOption('foreground', '#334155')
        pg.setConfigOptions(antialias=True)
        
        self.setWindowTitle("Optimization results")
        self.resize(1100, 850)
       
        self.setStyleSheet("""
            QDialog {
                font-family: 'Segoe UI', system-ui, sans-serif;
                font-size: 10pt;
                color: #334155;
                background-color: #f8f9fa;
            }
            QGroupBox {
                background-color: #ffffff;
                border: 1px solid #e2e8f0;
                border-radius: 8px;
                margin-top: 15px;
                padding-top: 5px;
            }
            QGroupBox::title {
                subcontrol-origin: margin;
                subcontrol-position: top left;
                padding: 0px 5px;
                color: #475569;
                font-weight: bold;
                font-size: 11pt;
            }
            QTabWidget::pane {
                border: 1px solid #e2e8f0;
                border-radius: 8px;
                background: #ffffff;
            }
            QTabBar::tab {
                background: #e2e8f0;
                color: #64748b;
                padding: 10px 20px;
                border-top-left-radius: 6px;
                border-top-right-radius: 6px;
                margin-right: 4px;

            }

                background: #ffffff;
                color: #0f172a;
                border-bottom: 2px solid #3b82f6;
            }
            QTabBar::tab:hover:!selected {
                background: #cbd5e1;
            }
            QPushButton {
                background-color: #64748b; 
                color: white; 
                font-weight: bold; 
                padding: 8px 15px; 
                border-radius: 6px;
                border: none;
            }
            QPushButton:hover {
                background-color: #475569;
            }
            QComboBox {
                border: 1px solid #cbd5e1;
                border-radius: 4px;
                padding: 5px;
                min-width: 150px;
                background: white;
            }
        """)
        
        self._prepare_data()
        self._init_ui()

    def _prepare_data(self):
        if self.payload:
            # data from live optuna oject
            df = self.payload.study.trials_dataframe()
            self.df = df[df['state'] == 'COMPLETE'].copy()
            
            self.base_vehicle_name = self.payload.base_vehicle_name
            self.best_lap_time = self.payload.best_lap_time
            self.best_params = self.payload.best_params
            self.tracks = self.payload.tracks
            self.base_car_params = self.payload.base_car_params
            
            try:
                importances = optuna.importance.get_param_importances(self.payload.study)
                self.importances = {k.replace('params_', ''): v for k, v in importances.items()}
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Could not calculate importances: {e}")
                self.importances = {}
                
        elif self.json_data:
            # Data from laoded JSON file
            self.df = pd.DataFrame(self.json_data['trials'])
            
            meta = self.json_data.get('metadata', {})
            self.base_vehicle_name = meta.get('base_vehicle_name', 'Unknown')
            self.best_lap_time = meta.get('best_lap_time', 0.0)
            self.tracks = meta.get('tracks_used', [])
            
            self.best_params = self.json_data.get('best_params', {})
            self.base_car_params = self.json_data.get('base_car_params', {})
            self.importances = self.json_data.get('parameter_importances', {})
        else:
            raise ValueError("Must provide either payload or json_data")

        self.trials = self.df['number'].values
        self.values = self.df['value'].values
        self.best_values = np.minimum.accumulate(self.values)
        
        self.param_cols = [c for c in self.df.columns if c.startswith('params_')]
        self.param_names = [c.replace('params_', '') for c in self.param_cols]
        
        self.y_min = self.df['value'].min()
        self.y_max = self.df['value'].max()

        # Fit Gaussian Process
        if len(self.df) > 0 and len(self.param_cols) > 0:
            X = self.df[self.param_cols].values
            y = self.df['value'].values
            
            self.scaler = StandardScaler()
            X_scaled = self.scaler.fit_transform(X)
            
            # Simple GP kernel covering most parameter behavior combinations
            kernel = Matern(nu=2.5) + WhiteKernel(noise_level=1)
            self.gp = GaussianProcessRegressor(kernel=kernel, n_restarts_optimizer=5, normalize_y=True)
            try:
                self.gp.fit(X_scaled, y)
                self.gp_fitted = True
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Warning: GP fitting failed: {e}")
                self.gp_fitted = False
        else:
            self.gp_fitted = False

    def _style_plot(self, plot, title, y_label, x_label, units=None):
        plot.setBackground("#FFFFFF")
        plot.setTitle(title, color='#0f172a', size='12pt', bold=True)
        plot.setLabel('left', y_label, units=units, color='#64748b') if units else plot.setLabel('left', y_label, color='#64748b')
        plot.setLabel('bottom', x_label, color='#64748b')
        
        ax_pen = pg.mkPen(color='#94a3b8', width=1)
        plot.getAxis('left').setPen(ax_pen)
        plot.getAxis('left').setTextPen('#475569')
        plot.getAxis('bottom').setPen(ax_pen)
        plot.getAxis('bottom').setTextPen('#475569')
        plot.showGrid(x=True, y=True, alpha=0.15)
        return plot

    def _init_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(15, 15, 15, 15)
        
        summary = QGroupBox(f"Optimization summary (Base: {self.base_vehicle_name})")
        s_layout = QVBoxLayout(summary)
        
        lbl_time = QLabel(f"<b>Best total time:</b> {self.best_lap_time:.3f} s")
        lbl_time.setStyleSheet("font-size: 14pt; color: #10b981;")
        s_layout.addWidget(lbl_time)
        
        params_list = [f"<b>{k}</b>: {v:.4f}" for k, v in self.best_params.items()]
        lbl_params = QLabel(f"<b>Best parameters:</b><br>{'  |  '.join(params_list)}")
        lbl_params.setWordWrap(True)
        lbl_params.setStyleSheet("font-size: 11pt; color: #334155;")
        s_layout.addWidget(lbl_params)
        
        layout.addWidget(summary)

        self.tabs = QTabWidget()
        self.init_history_tab()
        self.init_analysis_tab()
        self.init_importance_tab()
        layout.addWidget(self.tabs)
        
        btn_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        
       
        if self.payload:
            btn_save = QPushButton("Save results")
            btn_save.setMinimumWidth(120)
            btn_save.setStyleSheet("background-color: #10b981; color: white;")
            btn_save.clicked.connect(self.save_results)
            btn_layout.addWidget(btn_save)
            
        btn_layout.addStretch()
        
        btn_close = QPushButton("Close")
        btn_close.setMinimumWidth(120)
        btn_close.clicked.connect(self.accept)
        btn_layout.addWidget(btn_close)
        
        layout.addLayout(btn_layout)

    def save_results(self):
        timestamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d_%H-%M-%S")
        default_filename = f"{self.base_vehicle_name}_opt_{timestamp}.json"
        
        save_dir = os.path.join("data", "optimizations")
        os.makedirs(save_dir, exist_ok=True)
        
        file_path, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, "Save optimization results", os.path.join(save_dir, default_filename), "JSON Files (*.json)")
        
        if not file_path: return
            
        df_export = self.df.copy()
        for col in df_export.select_dtypes(include=['datetime', 'timedelta']).columns:
            df_export[col] = df_export[col].astype(str)
            
        data = {
            "metadata": {
                "timestamp": timestamp,
                "base_vehicle_name": self.base_vehicle_name,
                "tracks_used": self.tracks,
                "best_lap_time": self.best_lap_time
            },
            "base_car_params": self.base_car_params,
            "best_params": self.best_params,
            "parameter_importances": self.importances,
            "trials": df_export.to_dict(orient='records')
        }
        
        try:
            with open(file_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: json.dump(data, f, indent=4)
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Success", f"Results saved to:\n{file_path}")
        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Error", f"Failed to save results:\n{str(e)}")

    def init_history_tab(self):
        widget = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout(widget)
        plot = pg.PlotWidget()
        self._style_plot(plot, "Optimization history", "Objective value (Lap Time)", "Trial number", "s")
        plot.addItem(pg.ScatterPlotItem(x=self.trials, y=self.values, size=10, pen=pg.mkPen(None), brush=pg.mkBrush(self.C_SIM + '88')))
        plot.plot(self.trials, self.best_values, pen=pg.mkPen(self.C_BEST, width=3), name="Best Value")
        layout.addWidget(plot)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "History")

    def init_analysis_tab(self):
        widget = QWidget()
        main_layout = QHBoxLayout(widget)
        
        plot_layout = QVBoxLayout()
        self.combo_x = QComboBox()
        self.combo_x.addItems(self.param_names)
        self.combo_x.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.update_analysis_plot)
        
        header = QHBoxLayout()
        header.addWidget(QLabel("<b>X-Axis parameter:</b>"))
        header.addWidget(self.combo_x)
        header.addStretch()
        plot_layout.addLayout(header)
        
        self.plot_slice = pg.PlotWidget()
        
        self.gp_mean_curve = pg.PlotDataItem(pen=pg.mkPen('#e11d48', width=2, style=Qt.PenStyle.DashLine))
        self.gp_upper_curve = pg.PlotDataItem(pen=pg.mkPen(None))
        self.gp_lower_curve = pg.PlotDataItem(pen=pg.mkPen(None))
        self.gp_fill = pg.FillBetweenItem(self.gp_lower_curve, self.gp_upper_curve, brush=pg.mkBrush('#e11d4833'))
        
        self.slice_scatter = pg.ScatterPlotItem(size=12, pen=pg.mkPen('#ffffff', width=0.5), brush=pg.mkBrush(self.C_SIM + 'AA'))
        
        self.plot_slice.addItem(self.gp_fill)
        self.plot_slice.addItem(self.gp_mean_curve)
        self.plot_slice.addItem(self.slice_scatter)
        plot_layout.addWidget(self.plot_slice)
        main_layout.addLayout(plot_layout, stretch=3)
        
        self.filter_group = QGroupBox("Slice filters")
        self.filter_layout = QVBoxLayout(self.filter_group)
        self.sliders = {}
        self.slider_labels = {}
        
        for p_name in self.param_names:
            p_min, p_max = self.df[f"params_{p_name}"].min(), self.df[f"params_{p_name}"].max()
            p_layout = QVBoxLayout()
            lbl = QLabel(f"<b>{p_name}</b>")
            val_lbl = QLabel(f"Range: {p_min:.3f} - {p_max:.3f}")
            
            slider = QSlider(Qt.Orientation.Horizontal)
            slider.setRange(0, 100); slider.setValue(50)
            slider.valueChanged.connect(self.update_analysis_plot)
            
            p_layout.addWidget(lbl); p_layout.addWidget(slider); p_layout.addWidget(val_lbl)
            self.filter_layout.addLayout(p_layout)
            self.sliders[p_name] = slider; self.slider_labels[p_name] = val_lbl

        self.filter_layout.addStretch()
        main_layout.addWidget(self.filter_group, stretch=1)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Dynamic slice")
        self.update_analysis_plot()

    def update_analysis_plot(self):
        x_name = self.combo_x.currentText()
        if not x_name: return
        p_col_x = f"params_{x_name}"
        x_min, x_max = self.df[p_col_x].min(), self.df[p_col_x].max()
        
        x_margin = (x_max - x_min) * 0.05 if x_max != x_min else 1.0
        y_margin = (self.y_max - self.y_min) * 0.05 if self.y_max != self.y_min else 1.0
        
        self.plot_slice.setXRange(x_min - x_margin, x_max + x_margin, padding=0)
        self.plot_slice.setYRange(self.y_min - y_margin, self.y_max + y_margin, padding=0)
        
        mask = np.ones(len(self.df), dtype=bool)
        for p_name, slider in self.sliders.items():
            if p_name == x_name:
                self.slider_labels[p_name].setText("Current X-Axis")
                slider.setEnabled(False)
                continue
            
            slider.setEnabled(True)
            p_col = f"params_{p_name}"
            p_min, p_max = self.df[p_col].min(), self.df[p_col].max()
            
            target_val = p_min + (slider.value() / 100.0) * (p_max - p_min)
            margin = (p_max - p_min) * 0.1
            mask &= (self.df[p_col] >= target_val - margin) & (self.df[p_col] <= target_val + margin)
            self.slider_labels[p_name].setText(f"Value: ~{target_val:.3f}")

        filtered_df = self.df[mask]
        self.slice_scatter.setData(x=filtered_df[p_col_x].values, y=filtered_df['value'].values)
        
        # Update GP curve for current slice
        if getattr(self, 'gp_fitted', False):
            n_points = 100
            x_grid = np.linspace(x_min - x_margin, x_max + x_margin, n_points)
            
            X_pred = np.zeros((n_points, len(self.param_cols)))
            for i, p_name in enumerate(self.param_names):
                if p_name == x_name:
                    X_pred[:, i] = x_grid
                else:
                    slider = self.sliders[p_name]
                    p_col = f"params_{p_name}"
                    p_min_s, p_max_s = self.df[p_col].min(), self.df[p_col].max()
                    target_val = p_min_s + (slider.value() / 100.0) * (p_max_s - p_min_s)
                    X_pred[:, i] = target_val
                    
            try:
                X_pred_scaled = self.scaler.transform(X_pred)
                y_mean, y_std = self.gp.predict(X_pred_scaled, return_std=True)
                
                self.gp_mean_curve.setData(x=x_grid, y=y_mean)
                self.gp_upper_curve.setData(x=x_grid, y=y_mean + 1.96 * y_std)
                self.gp_lower_curve.setData(x=x_grid, y=y_mean - 1.96 * y_std)
                
                self.gp_mean_curve.show()
                self.gp_fill.show()
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"GP prediction failed: {e}")
                self.gp_mean_curve.hide()
                self.gp_fill.hide()
        else:
            self.gp_mean_curve.hide()
            self.gp_fill.hide()

        self._style_plot(self.plot_slice, f"Slice: {x_name} (Showing {len(filtered_df)} trials)", "Lap Time", x_name, "s")

    def init_importance_tab(self):
        widget = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout(widget)
        plot = pg.PlotWidget()
        self._style_plot(plot, "Hyperparameter importance", "Importance", "Parameters")
        
        if self.importances:
            sorted_items = sorted(self.importances.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
            x_pos = np.arange(len(sorted_items))
            y_vals = [val for _, val in sorted_items]
            labels = [key for key, _ in sorted_items]
            
            plot.addItem(pg.BarGraphItem(x=x_pos, height=y_vals, width=0.6, brush=pg.mkBrush(self.C_SIM)))
            plot.getAxis('bottom').setTicks([list(zip(x_pos, labels))])
        else:
            err_lbl = QLabel("Importance data is not available for this run.")
            err_lbl.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
            err_lbl.setStyleSheet("color: #64748b; font-weight: bold; font-size: 12pt;")
            layout.addWidget(err_lbl)

        layout.addWidget(plot)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Parameter importance")

import os

    def load_logs(self):
        self.tree.clear()
        results_dir = "results"
        
        if not os.path.exists(results_dir):
            os.makedirs(results_dir)
            return

        files = glob.glob(os.path.join(results_dir, "*.npz"))
        logs_data = []

        for filepath in files:
            try:
                with np.load(filepath, allow_pickle=True) as data:
                    track_name = str(data['track_name']) if 'track_name' in data else "Unknown"
                    lap_time = float(data['lap_time']) if 'lap_time' in data else 0.0
                    
                  
                    car_name = "Unknown"
                    if 'car_config' in data:
                        raw_config = data['car_config']
                        if hasattr(raw_config, 'item'):
                            raw_config = raw_config.item()
                            
                        try:
                            car_dict = json.loads(str(raw_config))
                            
                            car_name = car_dict.get('name', "Unknown")
                        except Exception:
                            pass
                    
                    filename = os.path.basename(filepath)
                    
                    logs_data.append({
                        'track': track_name,
                        'time': lap_time,
                        'car': car_name,
                        'file': filename,
                        'path': filepath
                    })
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error reading {filepath}: {e}")
                continue

        logs_data.sort(key=lambda x: (x['track'], x['time']))

        for item in logs_data:
            row = QTreeWidgetItem([
                item['track'],
                f"{item['time']:.3f}",
                item['car'],
                item['file']
            ])
            
            row.setCheckState(0, Qt.CheckState.Unchecked)
            row.setData(0, Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole, item['path'])
            
            self.tree.addTopLevelItem(row)

        self.tree.setCurrentItem(None)

    def on_compare_clicked(self):
        selected_data = []
        track_names_set = set()
        
        root = self.tree.invisibleRootItem()
        for i in range(root.childCount()):
            item = root.child(i)
            if item.checkState(0) == Qt.CheckState.Checked:
                path = item.data(0, Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole)
                track_name = item.text(0)
                lap_time = item.text(1)
                filename = item.text(3)
                
                selected_data.append({
                    'path': path,
                    'track': track_name,
                    'legend': f"{filename} ({lap_time}s)"
                })
                track_names_set.add(track_name)

        if len(selected_data) < 2:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Selection Required", "Please select at least 2 logs to compare.")
            return

        if len(track_names_set) > 1:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Track Mismatch", "Please select logs from the same track only.")
            return

        self.comparison_window = ComparisonPlotWindow(selected_data)
        self.comparison_window.show()

import datetime
import numpy as np
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import (QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel, 
                             QTabWidget, QGroupBox, QGridLayout, QFileDialog, QDoubleSpinBox, QApplication, QPushButton)

from src.utils.saver import save_results_to_npz
import os

class ResultsWindow(QWidget):
    # Moderní barevná paleta
    C_SIM = '#3b82f6'     # Modrá
    C_REF = '#334155'     # Tmavě šedá
    C_DELTA = '#ef4444'   # Červená
    C_ACC = '#10b981'     # Zelená
    C_BRK = '#ef4444'     # Červená

    def __init__(self, res, car, track, ds):

        self.car = car

        self.setStyleSheet("""
            QWidget {
                font-family: 'Segoe UI', system-ui, sans-serif;

                subcontrol-origin: margin;
                subcontrol-position: top left;

                color: #475569;
                font-weight: bold;
                font-size: 11pt;

                background: #ffffff;
            }
            QTabBar::tab {
                background: #e2e8f0;
                color: #64748b;
                padding: 10px 20px;
                border-top-left-radius: 6px;
                border-top-right-radius: 6px;
                margin-right: 4px;

            QTabBar::tab:selected {

    def center_window(self):
        qr = self.frameGeometry()
        cp = QApplication.primaryScreen().availableGeometry().center()
        qr.moveCenter(cp)
        self.move(qr.topLeft())
        self.showNormal()

    def _style_plot(self, plot, title, y_label, units):
        """Pomocná metoda pro krásný čistý vzhled pyqtgraph plotů"""
        plot.setBackground("#FFFFFF")
        plot.setTitle(title, color='#0f172a', size='12pt', bold=True)
        plot.setLabel('left', y_label, units=units, color='#64748b')
        plot.setLabel('bottom', 'Distance', units='m', color='#64748b')
        
        
        # Obarvení os a textu na jemnou šedou
        ax_pen = pg.mkPen(color='#94a3b8', width=1)
        plot.getAxis('left').setPen(ax_pen)
        plot.getAxis('left').setTextPen('#475569')
        plot.getAxis('bottom').setPen(ax_pen)
        plot.getAxis('bottom').setTextPen('#475569')
        
        # Jemná mřížka
        plot.showGrid(x=True, y=True, alpha=0.15)
        return plot

    def init_summary_panel(self):
        group = QGroupBox("Lap Summary")
        group.setObjectName("HighlightedGroup")
        main_h_layout = QHBoxLayout() 
        main_h_layout.setContentsMargins(15, 15, 15, 15)
        
        stats_widget = QWidget()
        grid = QGridLayout(stats_widget)
        grid.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        grid.setHorizontalSpacing(40)
        stats_widget.setStyleSheet("background-color: transparent;")
        
        def add_stat(row, col, label, value, unit):
            lbl_name = QLabel(f"<b>{label}:</b>")
            lbl_name.setStyleSheet("color: #64748b; font-size: 13px; background-color: transparent;")
            lbl_val = QLabel(f"{value}")
            lbl_val.setStyleSheet("color: #3b82f6; font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold; background-color: transparent;")
            lbl_unit = QLabel(unit)
            lbl_unit.setStyleSheet("color: #94a3b8; font-size: 13px; background-color: transparent;")
            
            grid.addWidget(lbl_name, row, col)
            grid.addWidget(lbl_val, row, col+1)
            grid.addWidget(lbl_unit, row, col+2)

    
        max_v = np.max(self.res.v) * 3.6
        avg_v = np.mean(self.res.v) * 3.6
        total_energy = self.res.E[-1] - self.res.E[0]
        
       
        soc_start = self.res.SoC[0] if self.res.SoC is not None else 0.0
        soc_end = self.res.SoC[-1] if self.res.SoC is not None else 0.0

        # První sloupec statistik
        add_stat(0, 0, "Lap Time", f"{self.res.lap_time:.3f}", "s")
        add_stat(1, 0, "Max Speed", f"{max_v:.1f}", "km/h")
        
        # Druhý sloupec statistik
        add_stat(0, 3, "Energy Used", f"{total_energy:.3f}", "kWh")
        add_stat(1, 3, "Avg Speed", f"{avg_v:.1f}", "km/h")

        # Třetí sloupec statistik (SoC data)
        add_stat(0, 6, "SoC Start", f"{soc_start:.1f}", "%")
        add_stat(1, 6, "SoC End", f"{soc_end:.1f}", "%")
        
        main_h_layout.addWidget(stats_widget)
        main_h_layout.addStretch()
        
        self.btn_save = QPushButton("Save Results (.npz)")
        self.btn_save.setFixedWidth(160)
        self.btn_save.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self.btn_save.clicked.connect(self.on_save_clicked)
        
        main_h_layout.addWidget(self.btn_save)
        group.setLayout(main_h_layout)
        self.layout.addWidget(group)

    def calculate_validation_metrics(self, sim_res, ref_data):
        if ref_data is None or ref_data['v'] is None:
            return None

        s_sim = sim_res.s
        s_ref = ref_data['s']
        v_ref_raw = ref_data['v']
        
        ds_ref = np.diff(s_ref, prepend=0)
        v_safe = np.maximum(v_ref_raw, 1.0) 
        dt_ref = ds_ref / v_safe
        t_ref_calculated = np.cumsum(dt_ref)

        v_ref_interp = np.interp(s_sim, s_ref, v_ref_raw)
        t_ref_interp = np.interp(s_sim, s_ref, t_ref_calculated)
        
        p_ref_interp = None
        if ref_data.get('p') is not None:
            p_ref_interp = np.interp(s_sim, s_ref, ref_data['p']) * 1000.0

        delta_v = sim_res.v - v_ref_interp 
        
        t_sim_zeroed = sim_res.t - sim_res.t[0]
        t_ref_zeroed = t_ref_interp - t_ref_interp[0]
        
        delta_t = t_sim_zeroed - t_ref_zeroed
        rmse_v = np.sqrt(np.mean(delta_v**2))
        total_time_diff = t_sim_zeroed[-1] - t_ref_zeroed[-1]
        
        return {
            's': s_sim,
            'v_ref': v_ref_interp,
            't_ref': t_ref_zeroed,
            'p_ref': p_ref_interp,
            'delta_v': delta_v,
            'delta_t': delta_t,
            'rmse_v': rmse_v,
            'diff_total': total_time_diff
        }

    def on_save_clicked(self):
        now_str = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d_%H-%M")
        default_name = f"{self.track.name}_{now_str}.npz"
        
        if not os.path.exists("results"):
            os.makedirs("results")
            
        default_path = os.path.join("results", default_name)

        filepath, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(
            self,
            "Save Simulation Results",
            default_path,
            "NumPy Zip (*.npz)"
        )

        if filepath:
            try:
                save_results_to_npz(filepath, self.res, self.track, self.ds,self.car)
                self.btn_save.setText("Saved!")
                self.btn_save.setStyleSheet("background-color: #10b981; color: white;")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error saving file: {e}")
                self.btn_save.setText("Error!")
                self.btn_save.setStyleSheet("background-color: #ef4444; color: white;")

    def init_dynamics_tab(self):
        widget = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        layout.setContentsMargins(15, 15, 15, 15)
        layout.setSpacing(15)
        
        # --- SPEED ---
        plot_v = pg.PlotWidget()
        self._style_plot(plot_v, "Velocity Comparison", "Speed", "km/h")
        
        legend_v = plot_v.addLegend(offset=(20, 20), pen=pg.mkPen('#e2e8f0'), brush=pg.mkBrush('#ffffffEE'))
        legend_v.setLabelTextColor('#0f172a')
      
        plot_v.plot(self.res.s, self.res.v * 3.6, pen=pg.mkPen(self.C_SIM, width=2.5), name="Simulation")
        
        val_data = None
        if self.track.ref_data is not None:
            val_data = self.calculate_validation_metrics(self.res, self.track.ref_data)
            if val_data:
                plot_v.plot(val_data['s'], val_data['v_ref'] * 3.6, 
                           pen=pg.mkPen(self.C_REF, width=2, style=Qt.PenStyle.DashLine), 
                           name=f"Real Log (RMSE: {val_data['rmse_v']*3.6:.1f} km/h)")

        layout.addWidget(plot_v)

        # --- DELTA TIME ---
        plot_dt = pg.PlotWidget()
        self._style_plot(plot_dt, "Delta Time (Accumulated Gap)", "Delta Time", "s")
        plot_dt.setXLink(plot_v)
        
        plot_dt.addItem(pg.InfiniteLine(pos=0, angle=0, pen=pg.mkPen('#334155', width=1.5)))

        if val_data:
            dt_curve = plot_dt.plot(val_data['s'], val_data['delta_t'], pen=pg.mkPen(self.C_DELTA, width=2.5))
            dummy_zero = pg.PlotCurveItem(val_data['s'], np.zeros_like(val_data['s']), pen=None)
            plot_dt.addItem(dummy_zero)
            
            fill_color = pg.mkColor(self.C_DELTA)
            fill_color.setAlpha(40)
            fill = pg.FillBetweenItem(dt_curve, dummy_zero, brush=fill_color)
            plot_dt.addItem(fill)
            
            diff = val_data['diff_total']
            sign = "+" if diff > 0 else ""
            plot_dt.setTitle(f"Delta Time (Final Gap: {sign}{diff:.3f} s)", color='#0f172a', size='12pt', bold=True)
        else:
            plot_dt.setTitle("No reference data available", color='#64748b')

        layout.addWidget(plot_dt)
        widget.setLayout(layout)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Dynamics")

    def init_powertrain_tab(self):
        widget = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        layout.setContentsMargins(15, 15, 15, 15)
        layout.setSpacing(15)
        
        # --- POWER ---
        v_interp = self.res.v[:-1]
        power_kw = (self.res.f_acc * v_interp) / 1000.0 
        brake_kw = (self.res.f_brake * v_interp) / 1000.0 
        
        plot_p = pg.PlotWidget()
        self._style_plot(plot_p, "Power Usage", "Power", "kW")
        legend_p = plot_p.addLegend(offset=(20, 20), pen=pg.mkPen('#e2e8f0'), brush=pg.mkBrush('#ffffffEE'))
        legend_p.setLabelTextColor('#0f172a')
        
        plot_p.plot(self.res.s[:-1], power_kw, pen=pg.mkPen(self.C_ACC, width=2.5), name="Traction")
        plot_p.plot(self.res.s[:-1], brake_kw, pen=pg.mkPen(self.C_BRK, width=2.5), name="Braking")
        plot_p.plot(self.res.s[:-1], -self.res.recuperation/1000, pen=pg.mkPen(self.C_REGEN, width=2.5), name="Recuperation")
        
        # --- ENERGY ---
        dt_sim = np.diff(self.res.t)
        pos_power_battery = np.maximum(power_kw, 0) / self.car.power_eff 
        e_step_sim = (pos_power_battery * dt_sim) / 3600.0
        e_cum_sim = np.insert(np.cumsum(e_step_sim), 0, 0.0)

        val_data = None
        e_cum_real = None
        
        if self.track.ref_data is not None:
            val_data = self.calculate_validation_metrics(self.res, self.track.ref_data)
            if val_data and val_data.get('p_ref') is not None:
                p_ref_kw = val_data['p_ref'] / 1000.0 
                plot_p.plot(val_data['s'], p_ref_kw, pen=pg.mkPen(self.C_REF, width=2, style=Qt.PenStyle.DotLine), name="Real Power")
                
                ds = np.diff(val_data['s'])
                v_ref_safe = np.maximum(val_data['v_ref'][:-1], 1.0)
                dt_ref = ds / v_ref_safe
                pos_power_real = np.maximum(p_ref_kw[:-1], 0)
                
                e_step_real = (pos_power_real * dt_ref) / 3600.0
                e_cum_real = np.insert(np.cumsum(e_step_real), 0, 0.0)

        plot_e = pg.PlotWidget()
        self._style_plot(plot_e, "Cumulative Energy (Discharge only)", "Energy Out", "kWh")
        legend_e = plot_e.addLegend(offset=(20, 20), pen=pg.mkPen('#e2e8f0'), brush=pg.mkBrush('#ffffffEE'))
        legend_e.setLabelTextColor('#0f172a')
        
        e_curve = plot_e.plot(self.res.s, e_cum_sim, pen=pg.mkPen(self.C_ENERGY, width=3), name="Sim")
        
        # Výplň energie
        dummy_zero = pg.PlotCurveItem(self.res.s, np.zeros_like(self.res.s), pen=None)
        fill_color = pg.mkColor(self.C_ENERGY)
        fill_color.setAlpha(30)
        plot_e.addItem(pg.FillBetweenItem(e_curve, dummy_zero, brush=fill_color))
        
        if e_cum_real is not None:
            plot_e.plot(val_data['s'], e_cum_real, pen=pg.mkPen(self.C_REF, width=2, style=Qt.PenStyle.DashLine), name="Real discharge")
        
        plot_e.setXLink(plot_p)
        
        layout.addWidget(plot_p)
        layout.addWidget(plot_e)
        widget.setLayout(layout)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Powertrain")

    def init_forces_tab(self):
        widget = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        layout.setContentsMargins(15, 15, 15, 15)
        
        plot_f = pg.PlotWidget()
        self._style_plot(plot_f, "Longitudinal Forces", "Force", "N")
        legend_f = plot_f.addLegend(offset=(20, 20), pen=pg.mkPen('#e2e8f0'), brush=pg.mkBrush('#ffffffEE'))
        legend_f.setLabelTextColor('#0f172a')
        
        plot_f.plot(self.res.s[:-1], self.res.f_acc, pen=pg.mkPen(self.C_ACC, width=2.5), name="Traction")
        plot_f.plot(self.res.s[:-1], self.res.f_brake, pen=pg.mkPen(self.C_BRK, width=2.5), name="Braking")
        
        layout.addWidget(plot_f)
        widget.setLayout(layout)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Forces")

    def init_map_tab(self):
        widget = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        layout.setContentsMargins(20, 20, 20, 20)

        # Kontejner s bílým pozadím a zaoblením
        canvas_container = QWidget()
        canvas_container.setStyleSheet("background: #ffffff; border: 1px solid #e2e8f0; border-radius: 8px;")
        canvas_layout = QVBoxLayout(canvas_container)
        canvas_layout.setContentsMargins(5, 5, 5, 5)

        self.map_plot = pg.PlotWidget(title="Power Map")
        self.map_plot.setBackground('#ffffff')
        self.map_plot.setTitle("Power Map", color='#0f172a', size='12pt', bold=True)
        self.map_plot.setAspectLocked(True)
        
        # Čistá mapa - skrytí os
        self.map_plot.hideAxis('bottom')
        self.map_plot.hideAxis('left')

        s_sim = self.res.s
        x_plot = np.interp(s_sim, self.track.s, self.track.x)[:-1]
        y_plot = np.interp(s_sim, self.track.s, self.track.y)[:-1]

        v_avg = self.res.v[:-1]
        power_kw = ((self.res.f_acc + self.res.f_brake) * v_avg) / 1000.0
        
        self.map_data = {'x': x_plot, 'y': y_plot, 'p': power_kw, 'v': v_avg * 3.6, 's': s_sim[:-1]}

        p_min = np.min(power_kw)
        p_max = np.max(power_kw)
        span = p_max - p_min + 1e-9 
        zero_pos = np.clip((0 - p_min) / span, 0.0, 1.0) 

        pos = np.array([0.0, zero_pos, 1.0])
        color_def = np.array([
            [239, 68, 68, 255],    # Red (Braking/Regen)
            [226, 232, 240, 255],  # Light Gray (0 kW)
            [16, 185, 129, 255]    # Green (Power)
        ], dtype=np.ubyte)
        
        cmap = pg.ColorMap(pos, color_def)
        brushes = cmap.map((power_kw - p_min) / span, mode='qcolor') 

        self.scatter = pg.ScatterPlotItem(x=x_plot, y=y_plot, size=8, pen=None, brush=brushes, hoverable=True, hoverSize=12)
        self.map_plot.addItem(self.scatter)
        
        start_marker = pg.ScatterPlotItem(x=[x_plot[0]], y=[y_plot[0]], size=15, symbol='o', brush='#0f172a')
        self.map_plot.addItem(start_marker)

        self.legend = pg.GradientLegend((20, 150), (10, 10)) 
        self.legend.setGradient(cmap.getGradient())
        self.legend.setLabels({f"{p_min:.0f} kW": 0, "0 kW": zero_pos, f"{p_max:.0f} kW": 1})
        self.legend.labelStyle = {'color': '#334155', 'font-size': '10pt'} # Tmavý text
        self.map_plot.addItem(self.legend)

        self.cursor_label = pg.TextItem(anchor=(0, 1), color="#334155") 
        self.map_plot.addItem(self.cursor_label)
        
        self.v_line = pg.InfiniteLine(angle=90, movable=False, pen=pg.mkPen('#94a3b8', style=Qt.PenStyle.DashLine))
        self.h_line = pg.InfiniteLine(angle=0, movable=False, pen=pg.mkPen('#94a3b8', style=Qt.PenStyle.DashLine))
        self.map_plot.addItem(self.v_line)
        self.map_plot.addItem(self.h_line)
        
        self.map_plot.scene().sigMouseMoved.connect(self.on_map_mouse_move)

        canvas_layout.addWidget(self.map_plot)
        layout.addWidget(canvas_container)
        widget.setLayout(layout)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Power Map")

    def on_map_mouse_move(self, pos):
        if self.map_plot.sceneBoundingRect().contains(pos):
            mouse_point = self.map_plot.plotItem.vb.mapSceneToView(pos)
            mx, my = mouse_point.x(), mouse_point.y()
            
            dists = np.sqrt((self.map_data['x'] - mx)**2 + (self.map_data['y'] - my)**2)
            idx = np.argmin(dists) 
            
            if dists[idx] < 20.0:
                p_val = self.map_data['p'][idx]
                html_text = (
                    f"<div style='background-color: rgba(255,255,255,220); border: 1px solid #cbd5e1; padding: 8px; border-radius: 4px; font-family: Segoe UI;'>"
                    f"<b>Dist:</b> {self.map_data['s'][idx]:.1f} m<br>"
                    f"<b>Speed:</b> {self.map_data['v'][idx]:.1f} km/h<br>"
                    f"<b>Power:</b> <span style='color: {'#10b981' if p_val > 0 else '#ef4444'};'>{p_val:.1f} kW</span>"
                    f"</div>"
                )
                self.cursor_label.setHtml(html_text)
                self.cursor_label.setPos(self.map_data['x'][idx], self.map_data['y'][idx])
                self.cursor_label.setVisible(True)
                self.v_line.setPos(self.map_data['x'][idx])
                self.h_line.setPos(self.map_data['y'][idx])
                self.v_line.setVisible(True)
                self.h_line.setVisible(True)
            else:
                self.cursor_label.setVisible(False)
                self.v_line.setVisible(False)
                self.h_line.setVisible(False)

    def init_sensitivity_tab(self):
        widget = QWidget()
        main_layout = QVBoxLayout()
        main_layout.setContentsMargins(15, 15, 15, 15)
        
        if not self.res.sens:
            main_layout.addWidget(QLabel("No sensitivity data available."))
            widget.setLayout(main_layout)
            self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Sensitivity")
            return

        raw = self.res.sens
        self.scenario_data = {
            'Weight (m)': {'val': self.car.weight, 'sens': raw.get('dTime_dm', 0.0), 'unit': 'kg'},
            'Drag (Cd)': {'val': self.car.cd, 'sens': raw.get('dTime_dCdA', 0.0), 'unit': '-'},
            'Downforce (Cl)': {'val': self.car.cl, 'sens': raw.get('dTime_dCL', 0.0), 'unit': '-'}
        }

        control_group = QGroupBox("Scenario Configuration)")
        
        control_group.setObjectName("HighlightedGroup")
        

        grid = QGridLayout()
        grid.setSpacing(15) 
        
        # Hlavičky s průhledným pozadím
        def create_header(text):
            lbl = QLabel(f"<b>{text}</b>")
            lbl.setStyleSheet("background-color: transparent; color: #334155;")
            return lbl

        grid.addWidget(create_header("Parameter"), 0, 0)
        grid.addWidget(create_header("Base Value"), 0, 1)
        grid.addWidget(create_header("Change (%)"), 0, 2)
        grid.addWidget(create_header("Delta Time"), 0, 3) 

        self.inputs = {} 
        self.deltas_labels = {}

        row = 1
        for name, data in self.scenario_data.items():
            lbl_name = QLabel(name)
            lbl_name.setStyleSheet("background-color: transparent; color: #334155; font-weight: bold;")
            
            lbl_val = QLabel(f"{data['val']:.3f} {data['unit']}")
            lbl_val.setStyleSheet("background-color: transparent; color: #64748b; font-family: Consolas;")
            
            spin = QDoubleSpinBox()
            spin.setRange(-50.0, 50.0)
            spin.setValue(0.0)
            spin.setSuffix(" %")
            spin.setSingleStep(0.5)
            spin.setFixedWidth(120)
            spin.valueChanged.connect(self.update_scenario) 
            self.inputs[name] = spin
            
            lbl_delta = QLabel("0.000 s")
            lbl_delta.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignRight)
            lbl_delta.setStyleSheet("background-color: transparent; font-family: Consolas; font-weight: bold; color: #475569;")
            self.deltas_labels[name] = lbl_delta

            grid.addWidget(lbl_name, row, 0)
            grid.addWidget(lbl_val, row, 1)
            grid.addWidget(spin, row, 2)
            grid.addWidget(lbl_delta, row, 3)
            row += 1

        btn_reset = QPushButton("Reset All")
        btn_reset.setStyleSheet("background-color: #64748b; color: white;")
        btn_reset.clicked.connect(self.reset_scenario)
        grid.addWidget(btn_reset, row, 2, 1, 2)

        control_group.setLayout(grid)
        main_layout.addWidget(control_group)

        self.lbl_scenario_results = QLabel()
        self.lbl_scenario_results.setTextFormat(Qt.TextFormat.RichText)
        self.lbl_scenario_results.setStyleSheet("""
            background-color: #f1f5f9; 
            border: 1px solid #cbd5e1; 
            border-radius: 8px;
            padding: 30px; 
            margin-top: 15px;
        """)
        self.lbl_scenario_results.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        
        main_layout.addWidget(self.lbl_scenario_results)
        main_layout.addStretch() 
        
        widget.setLayout(main_layout)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Sensitivity")
        self.update_scenario()

    def reset_scenario(self):
        for spin in self.inputs.values():
            spin.blockSignals(True) 
            spin.setValue(0.0)
            spin.blockSignals(False)
        self.update_scenario()

    def update_scenario(self):
        total_dt = 0.0
        
        for name, spin in self.inputs.items():
            pct = spin.value()
            data = self.scenario_data[name]
            d_param = data['val'] * (pct / 100.0)
            d_time = data['sens'] * d_param
            total_dt += d_time
            
            color = "#10b981" if d_time < 0 else "#ef4444" if d_time > 0 else "#94a3b8"
            sign = "+" if d_time > 0 else ""
            self.deltas_labels[name].setText(f"<span style='color:{color}; background-color: transparent;'>{sign}{d_time:.4f} s</span>")

        base_time = self.res.lap_time
        new_time = base_time + total_dt
        
        diff_color = "#10b981" if total_dt < 0 else "#ef4444" if total_dt > 0 else "#475569"
        diff_sign = "+" if total_dt > 0 else ""

        html = f"""
        <div style='text-align: center;'>
            <div style='color: #64748b; font-size: 14px; text-transform: uppercase; font-weight: bold;'>Predicted Lap Time</div>
            <div style='font-size: 56px; font-weight: bold; color: {diff_color}; margin: 15px 0;'>
                {new_time:.3f} s
            </div>
            <div style='font-size: 24px; color: {diff_color}; font-weight: bold;'>
                ({diff_sign}{total_dt:.3f} s)
            </div>
            <div style='margin-top: 25px; font-size: 15px; color: #64748b; border-top: 1px solid #cbd5e1; padding-top: 15px;'>
                Baseline: {base_time:.3f} s
            </div>
        </div>
        """
        self.lbl_scenario_results.setText(html)

    def init_drs_map_tab(self):
        widget = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        layout.setContentsMargins(20, 20, 20, 20)

        canvas_container = QWidget()
        canvas_container.setStyleSheet("background: #ffffff; border: 1px solid #e2e8f0; border-radius: 8px;")
        canvas_layout = QVBoxLayout(canvas_container)
        canvas_layout.setContentsMargins(5, 5, 5, 5)

        self.drs_plot = pg.PlotWidget(title="DRS Usage Map")
        self.drs_plot.setBackground('#ffffff')
        self.drs_plot.setTitle("DRS Usage Map", color='#0f172a', size='12pt', bold=True)
        self.drs_plot.setAspectLocked(True)
        self.drs_plot.hideAxis('bottom')
        self.drs_plot.hideAxis('left')

        s_sim = self.res.s[:-1]
        x_plot = np.interp(s_sim, self.track.s, self.track.x)
        y_plot = np.interp(s_sim, self.track.s, self.track.y) 

        pos = np.array([0.0, 1.0])
        color_def = np.array([
            [239, 68, 68, 255],   # Red (Closed)
            [16, 185, 129, 255]   # Green (Open)
        ], dtype=np.ubyte)
        
        cmap = pg.ColorMap(pos, color_def)
        brushes = cmap.map(self.res.drs_usage, mode='qcolor')

        self.drs_scatter = pg.ScatterPlotItem(x=x_plot, y=y_plot, size=8, pen=None, brush=brushes, hoverable=True, hoverSize=12)
        self.drs_plot.addItem(self.drs_scatter)
        self.drs_plot.plot([x_plot[0]], [y_plot[0]], symbol='o', symbolBrush='#0f172a', symbolSize=15)

        legend = pg.GradientLegend((20, 100), (10, 10))
        legend.setGradient(cmap.getGradient())
        legend.setLabels({"Closed": 0, "Open": 1})
        legend.labelStyle = {'color': '#334155', 'font-size': '10pt'}
        self.drs_plot.addItem(legend)

        canvas_layout.addWidget(self.drs_plot)
        layout.addWidget(canvas_container)
        widget.setLayout(layout)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "DRS Map")

    def init_soc_tab(self):
        if self.res.SoC is None: return

        widget = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        layout.setContentsMargins(15, 15, 15, 15)
        
        plot_soc = pg.PlotWidget()
        self._style_plot(plot_soc, "Battery State of Charge", "SoC", "%")
        
        s_plot = self.res.s[:len(self.res.SoC)]
        soc_curve = plot_soc.plot(s_plot, self.res.SoC, pen=pg.mkPen(self.C_SIM, width=3), name="SoC")
        
        dummy_zero = pg.PlotCurveItem(s_plot, np.zeros_like(s_plot), pen=None)
        fill_color = pg.mkColor(self.C_SIM)
        fill_color.setAlpha(30)
        plot_soc.addItem(pg.FillBetweenItem(soc_curve, dummy_zero, brush=fill_color))
        
        layout.addWidget(plot_soc)
        widget.setLayout(layout)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Battery")

import os
import glob
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import (QDialog, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QGroupBox, 
                             QScrollArea, QWidget, QCheckBox, 
                             QLabel, QDoubleSpinBox, QPushButton, QGridLayout, QMessageBox)
from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt

class SensitivityDialog(QDialog):
    def __init__(self, vehicle_name, car_params, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setWindowTitle("Sensitivity test")
        self.setMinimumWidth(500)
        self.setMinimumHeight(600)
        
        self.vehicle_name = vehicle_name
        self.car_params = car_params
        self._init_ui()
        
    def _init_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setSpacing(15)
        
        # 1. Tracks to evaluate
        group_tracks = QGroupBox("1. Tracks to evaluate")
        group_tracks.setStyleSheet("QGroupBox { border: 1px solid #3b82f6; }")
        layout_tracks = QVBoxLayout(group_tracks)
        
        scroll_tracks = QScrollArea()
        scroll_tracks.setWidgetResizable(True)
        scroll_tracks.setFrameShape(QScrollArea.Shape.NoFrame)
        scroll_tracks.viewport().setStyleSheet("background: transparent;")
        
        container_tracks = QWidget()
        container_tracks.setStyleSheet("background-color: #ffffff;")
        self.layout_track_list = QVBoxLayout(container_tracks)
        
        self.track_checkboxes = {}
        self._load_tracks()
        
        self.layout_track_list.addStretch()
        scroll_tracks.setWidget(container_tracks)
        layout_tracks.addWidget(scroll_tracks)
        layout.addWidget(group_tracks)
        
        # 2. Parameters
        group_params = QGroupBox("2. Parameters to calculate sensitivities")
        group_params.setStyleSheet("QGroupBox { border: 1px solid #3b82f6; }")
        layout_params = QGridLayout(group_params)
        
        layout_params.addWidget(QLabel("Select"), 0, 0)
        layout_params.addWidget(QLabel("Parameter"), 0, 1)
        layout_params.addWidget(QLabel("Base Value"), 0, 2)
        
        self.param_widgets = {}
        
        params_setup = [
            ("weight", "Total weight [kg]", 280.0, 0.0, 1000.0, 1.0, 1),
            ("wb", "Wheelbase [m]", 1.53, 0.1, 5.0, 0.01, 3),
            ("tw", "Car track width [m]", 1.20, 0.1, 3.0, 0.01, 3),
            ("wd", "Weight dist. front [0-1]", 0.53, 0.0, 1.0, 0.01, 2),
            ("cogz", "CoG height [m]", 0.25, 0.0, 1.5, 0.01, 2),
            ("cd", "Drag coefficient (CdA)", 1.83, 0.0, 10.0, 0.01, 2),
            ("cd_drs", "CdA (DRS open)", 1.17, 0.0, 10.0, 0.01, 2),
            ("cl", "Lift coefficient (ClA)", 5.64, 0.0, 15.0, 0.05, 2),
            ("cl_drs", "ClA (DRS open)", 4.20, 0.0, 15.0, 0.05, 2),
            ("cop", "Center of pressure [0-1]", 0.53, 0.0, 1.0, 0.01, 2)
        ]
        
        for i, (key, label, default_val, p_min, p_max, step, decimals) in enumerate(params_setup, start=1):
            cb_enable = QCheckBox()
            cb_enable.setChecked(True)
            
            lbl_name = QLabel(label)
            
            val = self.car_params.get(key, default_val)
            spin_val = QDoubleSpinBox()
            spin_val.setRange(p_min, p_max)
            spin_val.setSingleStep(step)
            spin_val.setDecimals(decimals)
            spin_val.setValue(val)
            
            cb_enable.toggled.connect(spin_val.setEnabled)
            
            layout_params.addWidget(cb_enable, i, 0, alignment=Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
            layout_params.addWidget(lbl_name, i, 1)
            layout_params.addWidget(spin_val, i, 2)
            
            self.param_widgets[key] = {"enable": cb_enable, "value": spin_val}
            
        layout.addWidget(group_params)
        
        btn_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.btn_close = QPushButton("Close")
        self.btn_close.clicked.connect(self.reject)
        
        self.btn_run = QPushButton("Run")
        self.btn_run.setObjectName("BtnSensitivity")
        self.btn_run.clicked.connect(self.on_run_clicked)
        
        btn_layout.addStretch()
        btn_layout.addWidget(self.btn_close)
        btn_layout.addWidget(self.btn_run)
        layout.addLayout(btn_layout)

    def _load_tracks(self):
        track_dir = "data/tracks"
        if os.path.exists(track_dir):
            files = glob.glob(os.path.join(track_dir, "*npz"))
            track_names = sorted([os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(f))[0] for f in files])
            for t_name in track_names:
                cb = QCheckBox(t_name)
                self.layout_track_list.addWidget(cb)
                self.track_checkboxes[t_name] = cb
        else:
            cb = QCheckBox("No tracks found")
            cb.setEnabled(False)
            self.layout_track_list.addWidget(cb)

    def on_run_clicked(self):
        selected = [n for n, cb in self.track_checkboxes.items() if cb.isChecked()]
        if not selected:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Warning", "Please select at least one track.")
            return

        # implement
        pass

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: src\ui\track_generator_dialog.py ---
import os
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import (QDialog, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QPushButton,
                             QLabel, QFileDialog, QSpinBox, QDoubleSpinBox, QRadioButton,
                             QGroupBox, QMessageBox)

class TrackGeneratorDialog(QDialog):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setWindowTitle("Track generation")
        self.resize(400, 350)
        
        self.selected_file = None
        self.init_ui()

    def init_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout()

        # --- 1. telemetry data ---
        file_group = QGroupBox("1. Telemetry data (.mat)")
        file_layout = QVBoxLayout()
        self.btn_load_file = QPushButton("Select .mat file")
        self.btn_load_file.clicked.connect(self.load_file)
        
        self.lbl_file_path = QLabel("No file selected.")
        self.lbl_file_path.setStyleSheet("color: gray; font-style: italic;")
        self.lbl_file_path.setWordWrap(True)
        
        file_layout.addWidget(self.btn_load_file)
        file_layout.addWidget(self.lbl_file_path)
        file_group.setLayout(file_layout)
        layout.addWidget(file_group)

        # --- 2. track mode ---
        mode_group = QGroupBox("2. Track mode")
        mode_layout = QVBoxLayout()
        mode_group.setStyleSheet("color: black;")
        self.radio_closed = QRadioButton("Closed circuit (Endurance / Autocross)")
        self.radio_manual = QRadioButton("Manual section (Acceleration / Skidpad)")
        self.radio_closed.setChecked(True) 
        
        mode_layout.addWidget(self.radio_closed)
        mode_layout.addWidget(self.radio_manual)
        mode_group.setLayout(mode_layout)
        layout.addWidget(mode_group)

        # --- 3. smoothing parameters ---
        param_group = QGroupBox("3. Smoothing parameters")
        param_layout = QVBoxLayout()
        
        # r parameter
        layout_r = QHBoxLayout()
        lbl_r = QLabel("Smoothness factor (r):")
        self.spin_r = QDoubleSpinBox()
        self.spin_r.setRange(100, 10000)
        self.spin_r.setSingleStep(100)
        self.spin_r.setValue(3000)
        layout_r.addWidget(lbl_r)
        layout_r.addWidget(self.spin_r)
        
        # w parameter
        layout_w = QHBoxLayout()
        lbl_w = QLabel("Position weight (w):")
        self.spin_w = QDoubleSpinBox()
        self.spin_w.setRange(1.0, 500.0)
        self.spin_w.setSingleStep(10.0)
        self.spin_w.setValue(50.0)
        layout_w.addWidget(lbl_w)
        layout_w.addWidget(self.spin_w)

        param_layout.addLayout(layout_r)
        param_layout.addLayout(layout_w)
        param_group.setLayout(param_layout)
        layout.addWidget(param_group)

        # --- 4. start button ---
        self.btn_generate = QPushButton("Continue to lap selection")
        self.btn_generate.setStyleSheet("font-weight: bold; background-color: #2E86C1; color: white; padding: 10px;")
        self.btn_generate.setEnabled(False) 
        self.btn_generate.clicked.connect(self.start_generation)
        
        layout.addSpacing(10)
        layout.addWidget(self.btn_generate)
        self.setLayout(layout)

    def load_file(self):
        file_path, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(
            self, "Select telemetry file", "", "MAT Files (*.mat);;All Files (*)"
        )
        if file_path:
            self.selected_file = file_path
            self.lbl_file_path.setText(os.path.basename(file_path))
            self.lbl_file_path.setStyleSheet("color: black;")
            self.btn_generate.setEnabled(True)

    def start_generation(self):
        if not self.selected_file:
            return
        # close and accept dialog
        self.accept()
